# Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot

ברטנורא על משנה טהרות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Tahorot

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2022 (Segments: 413)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר דָּבָר.** צְרִיכָה מַחֲשָׁבָה. שֶׁיַּחֲשֹׁב עָלֶיהָ לַאֲכִילַת אָדָם. שֶׁכָּל הָאֳכָלִים הַמֻּתָּרִים אֵין צְרִיכִים מַחֲשָׁבָה, אֲבָל זוֹ הוֹאִיל וְהִיא אֲסוּרָה בַּאֲכִילָה צְרִיכָה מַחֲשָׁבָה וְאַחַר כָּךְ תְּטַמֵּא. וְדַוְקָא בַּכְּפָרִים דְּלֵיכָּא רֹב עַם, אֲבָל בַּשְּׁוָקִים אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה מַחֲשָׁבָה, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ רֹב עַם הַבָּאִים לַשּׁוּק אִיכָּא טוּבָא דְּאָכְלֵי נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר. וְהָכִי מְפֹרָשׁ בְּפֶרֶק בַּתְרָא דְּעֳקָצִין:


שלשה עשר דבר. צריכה מחשבה – that he would intend upon it for human consumption. For all foods that are permitted do not require intention/thought. But this because it is prohibited for eating requires intention and afterwards it defiles. And especially in villages where there aren’t a multitude of people, but in the markets, it does not require intention/thought, for since there are a multiple of people that come to the market, there are a lot [of people] who eat the carrion of a clean bird. And this is explained sin in the last chapter of [Tractate] Utkzin [Mishnayot 1 and 3].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:2)


**אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה הֶכְשֵׁר.** אֶלָּא מְטַמְּאָה טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים כְּכָל שְׁאָר אֳכָלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְּשֶׁרֶץ שֶׁהֵם נַעֲשִׂים רִאשׁוֹן לַטֻּמְאָה. וְאֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם מַיִם אוֹ שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִים וְלֹא שֶׁתִּגַּע בְּשֶׁרֶץ. דְּהֶכְשֵׁר בִּזְרָעִים הוּא דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) וְכִי יֻתַּן מַיִם עַל זֶרַע וְגוֹ', וְדָרְשִׁינַן מַה זְּרָעִים שֶׁאֵין סוֹפָן לִטָּמֵא טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה צָרִיךְ הֶכְשֵׁר, אַף כָּל שֶׁאֵין סוֹפוֹ לִטָּמֵא טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה צָרִיךְ הֶכְשֵׁר, יָצְאָה נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר שֶׁסּוֹפָהּ לִטָּמֵא טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה, שֶׁהִיא מְטַמְּאָה אָדָם וּבְגָדִים בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה, שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה הֶכְשֵׁר:


ואינה צריכה הכשר (but does not require fitness/preparation) – but defiles the ritual impurity of foods like all other foods that came in contact with/touched a creeping animal which are made first-degree of ritual impurity, but they don’t require that water or other liquids come upon them and that they don’t come in contact with a creeping animal, for their rendering produce susceptible to ritual impurity is with seeds, as it is written (Leviticus 11:38): “But if water is put on the seed, etc. [i.e., and any part of the carcass falls upon it, it shall be impure for you],” and we (i.e., through the Rabbis) expound that just as seeds whose end is not to become defiled with a grave defilement requires preparation/fitness, even all whose end is not to become defiled with a grave defilement, that it defiles humans and garments in the esophagus, that does not require preparation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:3)


**וּמְטַמְּאָה טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין בְּכַבֵּיצָה.** דְּכַבֵּיצָה מִמֶּנָּה אִם נָגְעָה בְּאֳכָלִין טְהוֹרִים נִטְמְאוּ:


ומטמאה טומאת אוכלין בכביצה (and renders unclean with food uncleanness when it is the size of an egg) – that is like the size of an egg from it that came in contact with ritually pure foods they were defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:4)


**וְכַזַּיִת בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** אָדָם שֶׁאָכַל כַּזַּיִת מִמֶּנָּה נִטְמָא, וְנִטְמְאוּ בְּגָדָיו כְּשֶׁתַּגִּיעַ לְבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה. דִּכְתִיב (שם יז) וְהַנֶּפֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר תֹּאכַל נְבֵלָה וּטְרֵפָה וְגוֹ', וּבְנִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, וּכְתִיב תֹּאכַל, וְאֵין אֲכִילָה פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת:


וכזית בבית הבליעה – a person who ate from it an olive’s bulk is defiled, and his garments were defiled when it reached the esophagus, as it is written (Leviticus 17:15): Any person, whether citizen or stranger who eats what has died or has been torn, etc. [i.e., shall wash his clothes, bathe in water, and remain impure until evening; then he shall be pure].” And the verse is speaking of someone who consumes that carrion of a clean bird,’ and it is written, "תאכל"/”who eats”. But there is no consumption less that of an olive’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:5)


**וְטָעוּן הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ.** וְלֹא סַגִּי בִּטְבִילָה לְחוֹדַהּ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:6)


**וְחַיָּבִים עָלֶיהָ עַל בִּיאַת הַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** כִּשְׁאָר כָּל הַטֻּמְאוֹת. דִּכְתִיב בְּטֻמְאַת מִקְדָּשׁ וְקָדָשָׁיו (ויקרא ה) לְכֹל טֻמְאָתוֹ אֲשֶׁר יִטְמָא בָּהּ, וְדָרְשִׁינַן לְרַבּוֹת בּוֹלֵעַ נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר:


וחייבים עליה על ביאת המקדש – like the rest of all of the defilements, as it is written regarding the defilement of the Sanctuary and its Holy Things (Leviticus 5:4): “[Or when he touches human impurity] – any such impurity whereby one becomes impure” and we expound to include swallowing the carrion of a clean bird.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:7)


**וְשׂוֹרְפִין עָלֶיהָ אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה.** אִם נָגְעָה נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר בַּתְּרוּמָה, נִשְׂרֶפֶת, שֶׁכְּשֶׁאֵינָהּ בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה דִּין רִאשׁוֹן לַטֻּמְאָה יֵשׁ לָהּ וּמְטַמְּאָה אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה בִּנְגִיעָה לִהְיוֹת שֵׁנִי:


ושורפין עליה את התרומה (and they burn heave-offering on its account) – if the carrion of a clean bird touched heave -offering, it is burned, that when it is not in the esophagus, it has the law of first degree of ritual impurity and it defiles the heave-offering through its contact to become second-degree of ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:8)


**וְהָאוֹכֵל אֵבָר מִן הַחַי מִמֶּנָּה סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה הָאֵבָר פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת. וּסְתָם מַתְנִיתִין דְּלֹא כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר, דְּאִלּוּ רַבִּי מֵאִיר הָא שָׁמְעִינַן לֵיהּ בְּשִׁלְהֵי גִּיד הַנָּשֶׁה דְּאָמַר אֵבָר מִן הַחַי אֵינוֹ נוֹהֵג אֶלָּא בִּבְהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה בִּלְבַד:


והאוכל אבר מן החי ממנה סופג את הארבעים - and even if the limb was less than an olive’s bulk. But the anonymous Mishnah is not according to Rabbi Meir, for if it were according to Rabbi Meir, we would learn of it from the end of the [chapter] of “The Sciatic Nerve”/גיד הנשה (Chapter 7 of Tractate Hullin, folio 102a and see Mishnah 6) as he said that the [the law of a] limb from a living animal does not apply other than with pure cattle alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:9)


**שְׁחִיטָתָהּ וּמְלִיקָתָהּ מְטַהֲרוֹת אֶת טְרֵפָתָהּ.** וּמוֹצִיאָהּ מִידֵי נְבֵלָה שֶׁלֹּא תְטַמֵּא. לְפִי שֶׁהַמְּלִיקָה מַתֶּרֶת בִּפְנִים לַכֹּהֲנִים כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהַשְּׁחִיטָה מַתֶּרֶת בַּחוּץ, וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁטִּהֲרַתּוֹ שְׁחִיטָתוֹ, כָּךְ תְּטַהֲרֶנּוּ מְלִיקָתוֹ. וּשְׁחִיטָה דִּמְטַהֶרֶת אֶת הַטְּרֵפָה מִידֵי נְבֵלָה נַפְקָא לָן מִקְּרָא, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) וְכִי יָמוּת מִן הַבְּהֵמָה וְגוֹ'‏ כָּל הַנֹּגֵעַ בְּנִבְלָתָהּ יִטְמָא, יֵשׁ מִן הַבְּהֵמָה שֶׁהִיא מְטַמְּאָה, וְיֵשׁ שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה, פְּרָט לִטְרֵפָה שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטָה, וְהוּא הַדִּין לִטְרֵפַת עוֹף:


שחיטתה ומליקתה מטהרת את טריפתה (slaughtering it and wringing its neck render it no longer unclean even if it is Terefah) – and removes it from [the status] of a carrion that it would not defile. For the wringing/pinching of the neck permits the inside to the Kohanim, as the ritual slaughter permits the outside. And just as its ritual slaughter purifies it, so its pinching/wringing of the neck purifies it. And the ritual slaughter that purifies the torn animal/Terefah from the status of a carrion we derive from Scripture, as it is written (Leviticus 11:39): “If an animal [that you may eat] has died, anyone who touches its carcass shall be impure [until evening],” there is what from the animal that defiles and there is that which does not defile, excluding the torn animal/Terefah that was slaughtered, and the same law applies to the torn bird.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:10)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֵינָן מְטַהֲרוֹת.** דְּלֹא יָלְפִינַן טְרֵפַת עוֹף מִטְּרֵפַת בְּהֵמָה:


ר' יהודה אומר אינן מטהרות – that we don’t derive the torn bird from the torn cattle/animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:1:11)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר שְׁחִיטָתָהּ מְטַהֶרֶת וְלֹא מְלִיקָתָהּ.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר שחיטתה מטהרת ולא מליקתה – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:1)


**מִטַּמְּאוֹת.** אִם נָגַע בָּהֶן שֶׁרֶץ נִטְמְאוּ. וְאִם טְמֵאִים הֵן וְנָגְעוּ הַכְּנָפַיִם וְהַנּוֹצָה בְּאֳכָלִין טְהוֹרִים, נִטְמְאוּ, דַּחֲשִׁיבֵי יָד לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא, וְכָל שֶׁהוּא יָד מִטַּמֵּא וּמְטַמֵּא:


מיטמאות (become susceptible to uncleanness) – if a creeping animal touched them (i.e., the large feathers and down) they are defiled. And if they are impure, and the large feathers and down touched pure foods, they are defiled. For they are considered a handle to bring in and to remove/take out, and anything which is a handle becomes susceptible to becoming defiled and defiles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:2)


**וְלֹא מִצְטָרְפוֹת.** אִם הָיָה אֹכֶל פָּחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה אֵין הַכְּנָפַיִם וְהַנּוֹצָה מִצְטָרְפוֹת לְהַשְׁלִימוֹ לְכַבֵּיצָה לְטַמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים, דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי שׁוֹמֵר שֶׁמִּצְטָרֵף, כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ עֳקָצִין:


ולא מצטרפות (but they do not join together) – if the food was less than an egg’s bulk, the large feathers and the down do not combine to complete it to an egg’s bulk to defile through the ritual impurity of foods, as is taught in the beginning of [Tractate] Uktzin (see Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 – as it is not considered a protective part of the plant).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:3)


**הַנּוֹצָה מִצְטָרֶפֶת.** דַּחֲשִׁיב לַהּ שׁוֹמֵר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


הנוצה מצטרפת – for it is considered like a protective part of the animal. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yishmael.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:4)


**הַחַרְטוֹם.** שֶׁהָעוֹף מְנַקֵּר בּוֹ:


החרטום (nose/beak) – that the bird gnaws/bites it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:2:5)


**רָאשֵׁי אֲגַפַּיִם וְרֹאשׁ הַזָּנָב.** הַנִּשְׁאָרִים מְחֻבָּרִים לַגּוּף אַחַר שֶׁמָּרְטוּ הַנּוֹצָה, רְגִילִים לְחָתְּכָם וּלְהַשְׁלִיכָם, דְּלֹא חֲזוּ לַאֲכִילָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁבָּעוֹפוֹת הַמְפֻטָּמִים מַנִּיחִים אוֹתָן וְאוֹכְלִים אוֹתָן עִם הָעוֹף, וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמַר שֶׁכֵּן מַנִּיחִים בַּפְּטוּמוֹת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי. וְהָנֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר דָּבָר דַּחֲשִׁיב בְּמַתְנִיתִין, בְּבָבָא קַמַּיְתָא אִיכָּא תִּשְׁעָה, דִּשְׁחִיטָתָהּ וּמְלִיקָתָהּ חֲדָא חֲשִׁיב לְהוּ, דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ הֲווּ טָהֳרָה לִטְרֵפָתָהּ, וְהַכְּנָפַיִם, וְהַנּוֹצָה, וְהַחַרְטוֹם, וְהַצִּפָּרְנַיִם, הֲרֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר דָּבָר:


ראשי אגפים הראש הזנב (tips of the wings and the tip of the tail)- that remain attached to the body after it plucks off the down, and it is customary to cut/sever them and cast them away for they are not appropriate for eating. But on the fattened birds, we leave them and eat them with the bird, and that is what it states [by Rabbi Yossi at the conclusion of the Mishnah]: "שכן מניחים בפטומות"/”for so they are allowed to remain on fattened birds.” But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi. And these thirteen matters that are considered in our Mishnah, in the first section, there are night, for its ritual slaughter and its pinching/wringing of the neck are considered one, and both of them are pure for its torn animal, and the large feathers and the down and the nose/beak and the nails a, these [make up] the thirteen matters.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:1)


**נִבְלַת עוֹף טָמֵא.** שֶׁאֵין סוֹפָהּ לְטַמֵּא טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה, שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה אָדָם וּבְגָדִים:


נבלת עוף טמא – that its end is not to defile a stringent defilement/uncleanness which does not defile humans and garments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:2)


**צְרִיכָה מַחֲשָׁבָה.** שֶׁיַּחֲשֹׁב עָלֶיהָ לַאֲכִילָה:


צריכה מחשבה – that he will intend to eat/consume it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:3)


**וְהֶכְשֵׁר.** מַיִם אוֹ שְׁאָר מַשְׁקִין, וְאַחַר כָּךְ תִּגַּע בְּשֶׁרֶץ:


והכשר (and preparation)– of water or the rest of the liquids [for susceptibility to defilement], and afterwards it will come in contact with a creeping animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:4)


**לִפְסֹל אֶת הַגְּוִיָּה.** אִם נִטְמֵאת וְאָכַל מִמֶּנָּה חֲצִי פְּרָס שֶׁהִיא בֵּיצָה וּמֶחֱצָה, נִפְסַל גּוּפוֹ לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה עַד שֶׁיִּטְבֹּל:


לפסול את הגויה (to render the body unfit) – if it had been defiled, and he ate from half a loaf’s bulk, which is an egg and a half [of an egg], his (i.e., that of a Kohen) body is made unfit to eat heave-offering until he immerses [in a Mikveh].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:5)


**וְאֵין בָּהּ כַּזַּיִת בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם כב) נְבֵלָה וּטְרֵפָה לֹא יֹאכַל לְטָמְאָה בָהּ, מִי שֶׁאִסּוּרוֹ מִשּׁוּם אוֹכֵל נְבֵלָה מְטַמֵּא בַּאֲכִילָה בְּבֵית הַבְּלִיעָה, יָצָא זֶה שֶׁאֵין אִסּוּרוֹ מִשּׁוּם בַּל תֹּאכַל נְבֵלָה אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם בַּל תֹּאכַל דָּבָר טָמֵא:


ואין בה כזית בבית הבליעה – as it is written (Leviticus 22:8): “He shall not eat anything that died or was torn by beasts.” He whose prohibition is because of the food of a carrion defiles through eating in the esophagus, excluding this whose prohibition is not because of eating carrion but rather because of eating anything that is impure/


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:6)


**וְהָאוֹכְלָהּ אֵין טָעוּן הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ.** הָאוֹכֵל חֲצִי פְּרָס מִמֶּנָּה לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּטְמֵאת, שֶׁהוּא פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַגְּוִיָּה לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה, אִם טָבַל לְאַחַר שֶׁאֲכָלָהּ מֻתָּר בַּתְּרוּמָה מִיָּד וְאֵין טָעוּן הַעֲרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ:


והאוכלה אין טעון הערב שמש – the person who consumes half of a loaf of it after it was defiled, for he invalidates the body to eat heave-offering, if he immersed [in a Mikveh] after he consumed it, he is permitted [to consume] heave-offering immediately, and does not require [the arrival of] a sunset.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:7)


**וְאֵין חַיָּבִים עָלֶיהָ עַל בִּיאַת מִקְדָּשׁ.** אִם אָכַל חֲצִי פְּרָס נִבְלַת עוֹף טָמֵא וְנִכְנַס לַמִּקְדָּשׁ. דְּטֻמְאָה דְּרַבָּנָן הִיא, אַף עַל גַּב דְּשׂוֹרְפִין עָלֶיהָ תְּרוּמָה:


ואין חייבין עליה על ביאת מקדש – if he ate one-half a loaf of carrion of an impure bird and entered into the Sanctuary, it is defilement of the Rabbis, and even though that burn heave-offering upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:8)


**וְהָאוֹכֵל אֵבָר מִן הַחַי מִמֶּנָּה אֵינוֹ סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאוֹכֵל כַּזַּיִת בֵּין בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֲפִלּוּ לְמַאן דְּאָמַר אֵבָר מִן הַחַי צָרִיךְ כַּזַּיִת דַּאֲכִילָה כְּתִיב בֵּיהּ, הָכָא אִיכָּא כַּזַּיִת, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵינוֹ סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים. וּמִשּׁוּם טָמֵא לֹא מִחַיַּב, דְּלֵיכָּא כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר:


והאוכל אבר מן החי ממנה סופג את הארבעים – and even though that eat an olive’s bulk whether it is flesh or sinews and bones, for now even according to the one who holds that [eating] the limb of a living animal requires an olive’s bulk for eating is written regarding it, here there is an olive’s bulk, nevertheless, he does not receive forty (really, thirty-nine) lashes. But because of something impure, he is not obligated, for there isn’t an olive’s bulk of flesh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:3:9)


**וְאֵין שְׁחִיטָתָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ.** בַּאֲכִילָה, לְבֶן נֹחַ שֶׁמֻּתָּר בְּעוֹף טָמֵא וְאָסוּר בְּאֵבָר מִן הַחַי, וְאִם שְׁחָטָהּ וְהִיא מְפַרְכֶּסֶת לֹא טִהֲרַתָּהּ שְׁחִיטָתָהּ בַּאֲכִילָה עַד שֶׁתָּמוּת, אַף עַל גַּב דְּבִטְהוֹרָה אֲפִלּוּ מְפַרְכֶּסֶת מַתֶּרֶת הַשְּׁחִיטָה לְבֶן נֹחַ מִגּוֹ דְּמִכַּשְּׁרָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. וְהָכִי מִפָּרְשָׁה מַתְנִיתִין בְּשִׁלְהֵי פֶּרֶק גִּיד הַנָּשֶׁה:


ואין שחיטתה מטהרתה (for slaughtering it does not render it clean) – for eating. For a son of Noah who is permitted [to consume] an impure bird but is prohibited from [consuming] the limb of a living animal, but if he slaughtered it and it is moves convulsively/kicks, his slaughtering of it does not purify it for eating until it dies. Even though that with a clean [animal] even it is convulses, the slaughter is permitted for a son of Noah, in consequence of that it would be fit for an Israelite (i.e., the legal status required for its legitimacy might have easily been obtained). And this is how our Mishnah is explained at the end of the chapter, “The Sciatic Nerve”/גיד הנשה (see Talmud Tractate Hullin 102a).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:4:1)


**וּבַבְּהֵמָה הָעוֹר וְהָרֹטֶב.** כֻּלַּהּ מַתְנִיתִין מִפָּרְשָׁה בְּחֻלִּין פֶּרֶק הָעוֹר וְהָרֹטֶב:


ובבהמה העור והרוטב – all of this Mishnah is explained in Tractate Hullin, in the Chapter "העור והרוטב"/The Hide and the Fat/Juice (Chapter 9 of Tractate Hullin, Mishnah 1).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:1)


**מִצְטָרְפִין זֶה עִם זֶה לְטַמֵּא.** אִם אֵין בְּאֶחָד מֵהֶן כַּבֵּיצָה, מִצְטָרְפִין לְשִׁעוּר לְטַמֵּא כַּקַּל שֶׁבִּשְׁנֵיהֶם, שֶׁאִם נָגַע בַּתְּרוּמָה פְּסָלָהּ, כְּדִין אֹכֶל שֶׁנִּטְמָא בִּוְלַד הַטֻּמְאָה שֶׁפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמְּאָהּ:


מצטרפין זה עם זה לטמא – if there isn’t in one of them the equivalent of an egg’s bulk, they combine to the measurement to defile according to the lighter/lenient of the two of them, for if it came in contact with/touched heave-offering, it invalidated it, according to the law of food that was defiled by the offspring of uncleanness that invalidates the heave-offering but does not defile it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:2)


**כַּחֲצִי בֵיצָה וְכוּ'.** שֶׁבְּלָלָן זֶה בָּזֶה:


כחצי ביצה וכו' – that they mixed this one with that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:3)


**שֵׁנִי.** הַתַּעֲרֹבֶת הַזֶּה תּוֹרַת שֵׁנִי עָלָיו וּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


שני – this mixture/combination has the law of second degree [of ritual impurity] upon it and it invalidates the heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:4)


**שְׁלִישִׁי.** וּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


שלישי – and invalidates the Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:5)


**רִאשׁוֹן.** וְעוֹשֶׂה מַגָּעוֹ שֵׁנִי. דְּהָא אִיכָּא שִׁעוּרָא דְּרִאשׁוֹן בַּגְּוִיָּה לִתֵּן טֻמְאָה:


ראשון – and makes [through] its contact, second-degree [of ritual impurity]. For there is a measurement that first-degree [of ritual impurity] on the body to grant defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:5:6)


**חִלְּקָן זֶה שֵׁנִי וְזֶה שֵׁנִי.** שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּכָל אֶחָד חֲצִי שִׁעוּר דְּרִאשׁוֹן וַחֲצִי שִׁעוּר דְּשֵׁנִי, וּתְנַן לְעֵיל רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי מִצְטָרְפִין לַקַּל שֶׁבָּהֶן:


חלקן זה שני וזה שני – for in each of them is half of the measurement of the first, and half the measurement of the second. And it is taught in the Mishnah above (see this Mishnah, Tractate Taharot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 5) that first-degree [of ritual impurity] and second-degree [of ritual impurity] combine to the lighter of the two of them.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:6:1)


**[כָּל הָנָךְ בָּבוֹת פְּשׁוּטוֹת הֵן].** וְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאֲנִי יָכוֹל לִתְלוֹת שֶׁיֵּשׁ כַּבֵּיצָה מִן הֶחָמוּר בָּזֶה וּבָזֶה, אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר חָמוּר. וְאִם לָאו, אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר הַקַּל:


(all of these sections/segments are simple.) And every place where I am able to suspend that there is the bulk of an egg, it is the more severe of this one and that one, we follow after the more severe/stringent. But if not, we follow after the lenient/lighter.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:1)


**מִקְרָצוֹת.** חֲתִיכוֹת שֶׁל בָּצֵק. לְשׁוֹן קֶרֶץ מִצָּפוֹן בָּא (ירמיה מו):


מקרצות (pieces of dough – separated from the main dough in the trough) – pieces of dough. It is language of (Jeremiah 46:20): “[Egypt is a handsome heifer] A gadfly from the north is coming, coming!.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:2)


**נוֹשְׁכוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ.** שֶׁהֵן דְּבוּקוֹת זוֹ בָּזוֹ וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַפְרִישָׁן אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נוֹשְׁכוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ:


נושכות זו את זו (stick to one another) – that they are attached to each other and it is impossible to separate them unless they are stuck together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:3)


**כֻּלָּן תְּחִלָּה.** דַּחֲשׁוּבוֹת כְּאַחַת וּכְאִלּוּ נָגַע בְּכֻלָּן:


כולן תחילה – for they are considered as one and is if they touched all of them


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:4)


**פֵּרְשׁוּ.** הַחֲתִיכוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ:


פירשו (if they were separated) – the pieces, this one from that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:5)


**בְּמַשְׁקִין כֻּלָּן שְׁנִיּוֹת.** דְּמַשְׁקִין תְּחִלָּה הֲווּ וְעוֹשִׂין שֵׁנִי:


במשקין כלן שניות – for as liquids they were first-degree [of ritual impurity] and they make [other things] second-degree [of ritual impurity].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:7:6)


**בְּיָדַיִם כֻּלָּן שְׁלִישִׁיּוֹת.** דְּיָדַיִם שְׁנִיּוֹת הֵן, וְעוֹשׂוֹת שְׁלִישִׁי בַּתְּרוּמָה:


בידים כלן שלשיות – for hands are second degree [of ritual impurity] and make heave-offering third-degree [of ritual impurity].



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:8:1)


**מִקְרֶצֶת.** חֲתִיכַת בָּצֵק שֶׁהָיְתָה תְּחִלָּה וְחִבֵּר לָהּ חֲתִיכוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת:


מקרצת – a piece of dough that was first degree [of ritual defilement] and he attached to it other pieces.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:8:2)


**כֻּלָּן תְּחִלָּה.** וְהַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּאֲחֵרוֹת כְּנוֹגֵעַ בָּרִאשׁוֹן לַטֻּמְאָה, דַּחֲשִׁיבָא חִבּוּר. וְכֵן נַמִּי הָיְתָה שְׁנִיָּה, חֲשׁוּבוֹת חִבּוּר, מֵאַחַר דְּאִם פָּרְשׁוּ אִשְׁתְּיַר בְּהוּ טֻמְאָה. אֲבָל הָיְתָה שְׁלִישִׁית, דְּאִם פָּרְשׁוּ לֵית בְּהוּ טֻמְאָה, דְּאֵין שְׁלִישִׁי עוֹשֶׂה רְבִיעִי בַּתְּרוּמָה, אֲפִלּוּ כִּי לֹא פָּרְשׁוּ לָא חֲשִׁיבָן חִבּוּר, וּטְהוֹרוֹת:


כולן תחילה – and one who has contact with the others is like one who touches the first degree [of ritual defilement], and it is considered connection/junction. And similarly, also, if it was second degree [of ritual defilement], they are considered connection/junction, for after if they separated, the defilement remains in them. But if it was third degree [of ritual defilement], if they separated, they don’t have defilement, for [something] third degree [of ritual defilement] does not make heave-offering fourth degree [of ritual defilement], even if they didn’t separate, they are not considered a connection/junction, and they are ritually pure.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:9:1)


**כִּכְּרוֹת הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** כְּגוֹן שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם וְלֶחֶם הַפָּנִים וּמְנָחוֹת וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶן:


ככרות הקודש – as, for example the two loaves (brought as a communal offering on Shavuot – see Leviticus 23:27 – which were leavened, resembling that of the shewbread which, however was unleavened, together with two lambs, ceremonially waved, and afterwards, divided among the priests and eaten in the Temple courtyard) and the shewbread (see Leviticus 24:5-9 – describing the twelve shewbread loaves that were placed on the sacred table in the Sanctuary each Shabbat; the bread of the previous week was divided among the priests and eaten; it was placed on the table in two arrangements of six loaves with frankincense placed between them or atop them) and similar kinds of things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:9:2)


**שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ גֻּמּוֹתֵיהֶן מַיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים.** שֶׁהָיוּ בְּתוֹךְ הַגֻּמּוֹת שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַכִּכָּר מַיִם שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ, וְנִטְמָא כִּכָּר אֶחָד בְּשֶׁרֶץ, וְנָגַע הַכִּכָּר שֶׁנִּטְמָא בַּשֵּׁנִי, וְהַשֵּׁנִי בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי, וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן מֵאָה כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִים, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגְעוּ בַּמַּיִם שֶׁבַּכִּכָּר אֶלָּא נָגַע כָּל כִּכָּר בַּחֲבֵרוֹ, נִטְמְאוּ כֻּלָּן, שֶׁחִבַּת הַקֹּדֶשׁ עוֹשָׂה כְּאִלּוּ הַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ כָּל כִּכָּר שֶׁהוּא נַעֲשָׂה תְּחִלָּה הוּא שֶׁנָּגַע בַּכִּכָּר וְטִמְּאוֹ. אֲבָל בַּתְּרוּמָה אֵינוֹ כֵּן, אֶלָּא הַכִּכָּר הָרִאשׁוֹן הַנִּטְמָא בַּשֶּׁרֶץ בִּלְבַד מְטַמֵּא אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ לִהְיוֹת שֵׁנִי, וְהַשֵּׁנִי פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְתוּ לֹא, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן לְמַשְׁקֶה שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַגֻּמָּא:


שבתוך גומותיהן מים המקודשים (that within their indentations is preserved water in cleanness fitting for Holy Things)- that there were within the indentations that are on top of the loaf water that was made on the purity of Holy Things, and one loaf was defiled by a moving creature/unclean reptile, and the loaf that was defiled touched something that was second degree [of ritual impurity] , and the second [touched] that which was third degree [of ritual impurity] and even if they are one-hundred, all of them are ritually impure, and even though they didn’t touch the water that was in the loaf but rather all of the loaf touched/came in contact with its neighbor/fellow [loaf], all of them were defiled, for the honor in which sacred objects are held makes them [fit for Levitical uncleanness (even without contact with liquids)], as if the liquid that is within each loaf that is made first degree [of ritual impurity]that which touched the loaf and defiled it. But heave-offering is not such, but rather, the first loaf [of heave-offering] that is defiled by a moving creature/unclean reptile alone defiles its neighbor/fellow loaf to become second degree [of ritual impurity], and the second-degree invalidates the third-degree, but not further, and we don’t worry about the liquid that is within the hole/indentation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 1:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/1:9:3)


**אִם יֵשׁ בֵּין הַכִּכָּרוֹת מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ אַף בַּתְּרוּמָה כֻּלָּן טְמֵאוֹת.** הַמַּשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ שֶׁבֵּין כָּל כִּכָּר וְכִכָּר חוֹזֵר לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה, וּמְטַמֵּא הַכִּכָּר שֶׁהוּא נוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ שֵׁנִי, וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, כִּכְּרוֹת הַקֹּדֶשׁ הַמֻּנָּחוֹת בְּטַבְלָא הָעֲשׂוּיָה גֻּמּוֹת גֻּמּוֹת, וְכָל כִּכָּר מֻנָּח בְּתוֹךְ גֻּמָּא שֶׁלּוֹ. וְהַמַּיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים גָּרְסִינַן, פֵּרוּשׁ, וְכֵן הַמַּיִם הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים הַמֻּנָּחִים בִּכְלִי הֶעָשׂוּי גֻּמּוֹת גֻּמּוֹת, אִם נִטְמָא אֶחָד מֵהֶן בְּשֶׁרֶץ, נִטְמְאוּ כֻּלָּן, שֶׁהַכְּלִי מְצָרֵף מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ לַקֹּדֶשׁ, אֲבָל לֹא לַתְּרוּמָה, דְּבַתְּרוּמָה אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי וּפוֹסֵל שְׁלִישִׁי הֵיכָא דְּנָגְעוּ זֶה בָּזֶה, וְאֵין כְּלִי מְצָרֵף. וְאִם יֵשׁ בֵּין הַגֻּמּוֹת מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ הַכֹּל טָמֵא, שֶׁהַכִּכָּר שֶׁהוּא שֵׁנִי עוֹשֶׂה הַמַּשְׁקֶה רִאשׁוֹן וְחוֹזֵר וּמְטַמֵּא כִּכָּר הָאַחֵר, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


אם יש בין הככרות משקה טופח אף תרומה כולן טמאות (if there is between the loaves dripping liquid, even in the case of heave offering, the whole is ritually unclean) – that the liquid dripping that is between each and every loaf returns to become first degree [of ritual impurity], and defiles the loaf that it touches to make it second degree [of ritual impurity], and similarly, forever. Another interpretation: Holy loaves that are placed on a plank/board that are made with indentations/holes, and each loaf is placed within its hole/indentation. But the מים המקודשים/preserved water in cleanness fitting for Holy Things, we have the reading, meaning, and similarly, the preserved water in cleanness fitting for Holy Things that are placed in a vessel that is made with indentations/holes, if one of them is defiled by a moving insect/unclean reptile, all of them are defiled, for the vessel combines/joins what is within it to the Holy Things. But not for heave-offering, for heave offering does not defile other than first degree [of ritual impurity] and second degree [of ritual impurity] and invalidates third-degree [of ritual impurity] where they touched one another, but the vessel does not combine/join. But if there is dripping liquid between the holes/indentations, everything is ritually impure, for the loaf which is second degree [of ritual impurity] makes the liquid first-degree [of ritual impurity] and returns and defiles another loaf, and similarly all of it.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה.** בְּאִשָּׁה טְהוֹרָה אַיְרֵי:


האשה – we are speaking of a ritually pure woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:2)


**שֶׁהָיְתָה כוֹבֶשֶׁת יָרָק.** שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בִּקְדֵרָה. וְאוֹתוֹ יָרָק לֹא הֻכְשַׁר לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, אֶלָּא כְּדֶרֶךְ הַכּוֹבְשִׁים שֶׁמְּחַתְּכִים אֶת הַיָּרָק בְּלֹא מַשְׁקֶה וּמוֹלְחִים אוֹתוֹ וְנוֹתְנִים בִּקְדֵרָה אוֹ בְּחָבִית וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוֹתְנִים עָלָיו יַיִן אוֹ חֹמֶץ אוֹ שֶׁמֶן, (והכשר אותו העלה):


שהיתה כובשת ירק (that was pickling/pressing vegetables) – of heave offering in a pot and that vegetable was not susceptible to receive ritual defilement, but rather in the manner of those who pickle that they cut/sever the vegetable without liquid and salt it and place them in a pot or in a jar and afterwards place upon them wine or vinegar or oil (and that leaf is made susceptible to receive ritual defilement).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:3)


**נָגְעָה.** בְּיָדַיִם מְסֹאָבוֹת שֶׁפּוֹסְלִים אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


ונגעה – with unwashed hands which make the heave-offering/priest’s due unit/invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:4)


**בְּעָלֶה.** שֶׁל יָרָק שֶׁהָיָה חֶצְיוֹ בַּקְּדֵרָה עִם הַמַּשְׁקִין וְחֶצְיוֹ חוּץ לַקְּדֵרָה נָגוּב, וְנָגְעָה בְּמָקוֹם הַנָּגוּב:


בעלה – [on the leaf] of a vegetable that is half in the pot with the liquids and half outside of the pot dry, and she touched it in the dry place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:5)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ כַבֵּיצָה.** שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר לְטַמֵּא אֳכָלִים:


אע"פ שיש בו כביצה – which is the measurement to defile foods.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:6)


**הוּא טָמֵא.** אוֹתוֹ הֶעָלֶה:


הוא טמא – that leaf [is impure].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:7)


**וְהַכֹּל טָהוֹר.** שְׁאָר הַיָּרָק וְהַמַּשְׁקִין שֶׁבַּקְּדֵרָה. דְּלֹא אָתֵי הַאי עָלֶה שֶׁמִּקְצָתוֹ בַּקְּדֵרָה וּמְטַמֵּא לְהוּ, דְּיָדַיִם שְׁנִיּוֹת, וְהֶעָלֶה שְׁלִישִׁי, וְאֵין שְׁלִישִׁי עוֹשֶׂה רְבִיעִי בַּתְּרוּמָה:


והכל טהור – the rest of the vegetable and the liquids that are in the pot, for this leaf part of which is in the pot does not come defile it, for the hands are second degree [of ritual impurity] and the leaf is third degree [of ritual impurity], but something that is third-degree does not make something fourth-degree in regard to heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:8)


**נָגְעָה.** בִּסְתָם יָדַיִם שֶׁהֵם שְׁנִיּוֹת:


נגעה – for mere hands are second degree [of ritual uncleanness].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:9)


**בִּמְקוֹם מַשְׁקֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין הֶעָלֶה נָגוּב, נַעֲשָׂה מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁעָלָיו תְּחִלָּה, דְּכָל הַפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה מְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקִין לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה, וְהַמַּשְׁקֶה עוֹשֶׂה אֶת הֶעָלֶה שֵׁנִי, הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ בֶּעָלֶה כַּבֵּיצָה הַיְנוּ שִׁעוּר לְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים:


במקום משקה – that is to say, in a place where the leaf is not dried, the liquid that is upon it (i.e., the leaf) becomes first degree [of ritual defilement], for everything that invalidates the heave-offering defiles liquids to become first degree [of ritual defilement], and the liquid makes the leaf second-degree [of ritual defilement. Therefore, when there is on a leaf an egg’s bulk, that is the the measure to defile other things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:10)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** אַף הַקְּדֵרָה עַצְמָהּ, דְּמִקְצָת עָלֶה שֶׁבַּקְּדֵרָה עוֹשֶׂה מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁבַּקְּדֵרָה תְּחִלָּה וְחוֹזְרִין וּמְטַמְּאִין אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה, כִּדְאִיתָא בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְּשַׁבָּת, הָאֳכָלִים וְהַכֵּלִים שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בְּמַשְׁקִין:


הכל טמא – even the pot itself [is ritually impure], for part of the leaf that is in the pot makes the liquid that is in the pot first degree [of ritual defilement] and it (i.e., the leaf) returns and defiles the pot, as is brought in the first chapter of [Tractate] Shabbat [see the Bartenura commentary to Mishnah 4] concerning food and vessels that were defiled by liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:11)


**אֵין בּוֹ כַבֵּיצָה הוּא טָמֵא.** אוֹתוֹ עָלֶה שֶׁנָּגְעָה בוֹ:


אין בו כביצה הוא טמא – that leaf that she touched [is ritually impure].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:12)


**וְהַכֹּל טָהוֹר.** דְּפָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה אֵין מְטַמֵּא אַחֵר:


והכל טהור – for less than an egg’s bulk it does not defile other [things].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:13)


**חָזַר לַקְּדֵרָה.** אוֹתוֹ מְקוֹם הַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁנָּגַע בּוֹ:


חזר לקדירה – that place that liquid touched it (i.e., the leaf).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:14)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בֶּעָלֶה כַּבֵּיצָה, שֶׁהַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁנָּגַע בּוֹ חָזַר וְטִמֵּא כָּל מַה שֶּׁבַּקְּדֵרָה, וְאַף הַקְּדֵרָה עַצְמָהּ:


הכל טמא -even though there isn’t on the leaf an egg’s bulk [of liquid], for the liquid that touched it returned and defiled all that was in the pot, and even the pot itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:15)


**הָיְתָה מַגַּע טְמֵא מֵת.** אִשָּׁה זוֹ שֶׁכּוֹבֶשֶׁת אֶת הַיָּרָק. הֲרֵי הָאִשָּׁה רִאשׁוֹן וְהֶעָלֶה שֶׁנָּגְעָה בּוֹ שֵׁנִי, הִלְכָּךְ אִם יֵשׁ בּוֹ כַּבֵּיצָה הַכֹּל טָמֵא:


היתה מגע טמא מת – this woman who is pickling the vegetables, for the woman is first-degree [of ritual impurity] and the leaf that she touched is second degree [of ritual impurity], therefore, if there is an egg’s bulk on it, everything is ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:16)


**הָיְתָה טְבוּלַת יוֹם מְנַעֶרֶת אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא יֵשׁ בְּאִשָּׁה זוֹ שְׁתֵּי טֻמְאוֹת, חֲדָא דִּטְבוּלַת יוֹם וְאִידָךְ דִּסְתָם יָדַיִם שֶׁהֵם שְׁנִיּוֹת, כִּדְּקָתָנֵי בְּיָדַיִם מְסֹאֲבוֹת. וְתַרְוַיְהוּ צְרִיכִין מִשּׁוּם סֵיפָא. מְנַעֶרֶת, מְעָרָה מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹךְ הַקְּדֵרָה. לִישָׁנָא אַחֲרִינָא, מְנַעֶרֶת, מְגִיסָה בְּתוֹךְ הַקְּדֵרָה בְּכַף שֶׁבְּיָדָהּ:


היתה טבולת יום מנערת את הקדירה (if one who had immersed herself on that same day was emptying (or stirring) the pot) – for now the woman has two defilements: one – that she is someone who had immersed herself that day, and the other – that hands are second-degree of ritual impurity, as it is taught regarding unwashed hands, and both of them are necessary [to teach in the Mishnah] because of the concluding clause. [The word} מנערת – that she empties out what is within the pot (another version), מנערת – she stirs with a spoon that is in her hand that which is in the pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:17)


**סָפֵק מִן הַקְּדֵרָה נִתְּזוּ.** וְלֹא נִטְמָא מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ:


ספק מן הקדירה נתזו (doubt whether it – the liquid had splashed from the pot) – and what is within the pot is not defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:18)


**סָפֵק שֶׁהַקֶּלַח.** שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ מָלֵא מַשְׁקֶה וְהוּא בְּתוֹךְ הַקְּדֵרָה נָגַע בְּיָדֶיהָ:


ספק שהקלח (a doubt whether the jet/the stalk) – which is completely filled with liquid and it is within the pot touched her hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:19)


**הַיָּרָק פָּסוּל.** מִשּׁוּם סְפֵקָא דִּטְבוּל יוֹם. אֲבָל מִשּׁוּם סְפֵק יָדַיִם לֹא, כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק ב'‏ דִּטְבוּל יוֹם (משנה ב), חֹמֶר בִּטְבוּל יוֹם שֶׁסְּפֵק טְבוּל יוֹם פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, וְהַיָּדַיִם סְפֵקָן טָהוֹר:


הירק פסול – because of doubt of being someone who immersed that day, but not because of doubtfulness of [the status] of her hands, as is taught in the Mishnah in Chapter 2 [Mishnah 2] (regarding the stringency of whether her hands were unclean or not), there is a greater stringency regarding someone who immersed that day for the doubtfulness concerning [whether] a person who immersed that day invalidates the heave offering/priest’s due, but the hands are doubtfully pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:1:20)


**וְהַקְּדֵרָה טְהוֹרָה.** דְּמִשּׁוּם טְבוּל יוֹם לֹא מְטַמְּאָה אֲפִלּוּ וַדַּאי נָגְעָה בַּמַּשְׁקִין, דְּאֵין טְבוּל יוֹם עוֹשֶׂה מַשְׁקֶה תְּחִלָּה, כְּדִתְנַן (פרה פ"ח מ"ו), כָּל הַפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה חוּץ מִטְּבוּל יוֹם. וְאִי מִשּׁוּם יָדַיִם, לֹא מְטַמְּאָה, דְּכֵיוָן דִּסְפֵקָא הוּא שֶׁמָּא נִתְּזוּ עַל יָדֶיהָ, מִסְּפֵיקָא קְדֵרָה טְהוֹרָה, שֶׁסְּפֵק יָדַיִם לְטַמֵּא, טָהוֹר:


והקדירה טהורה – because a person who immersed that day did not defile it (i.e., the pot) even it is she certainly touched the liquids, for a person who immersed that day does not make liquids first degree [of ritual impurity], as is taught in the Mishnah [Tractate Parah, Chapter 8, Mishnah 7]: “All who renders the priest’s due/heave offering unit defiles the liquids to be first-degree [of ritual impurity] except for a person who had immersed that day.” But if it is because of [her] hands, she does not defile it, for since there is a doubt lest it (i.e., the liquid from the pot) splashed upon her hands, from doubt the pot is ritually pure, for hands that are doubtfully impure are pure.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל רִאשׁוֹן.** נַעֲשֶׂה גּוּפוֹ רִאשׁוֹן לִהְיוֹת מַגָּעוֹ שֵׁנִי. וְהָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי נַעֲשֶׂה גּוּפוֹ שֵׁנִי, וְאִם נָגַע בַּתְּרוּמָה פְּסָלָהּ. אֵלּוּ מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁאָכַל חֲצִי פְּרָס, שֶׁהוּא בֵּיצָה וּמֶחֱצָה לְהָרַמְבַּ"ם וּשְׁתֵּי בֵּיצִים לְרַשִׁ"י, שֶׁזֶהוּ שִׁעוּר אֳכָלִים לִפְסֹל אֶת הַגְּוִיָּה:


האוכל אוכל ראשון – his body is made first degree [of ritual impurity] so that his contact is second-degree [of ritual impurity]. And the person who eats food that is second-degree [of ritual impurity], his body is made second-degree [of ritual impurity], but if he came in contact with heave-offering/priest-due, he invalidated it, these are from the eighteen matters that were decreed on that self-same day (i.e., when Rabban Gamaliel was deposed as President of the Sanhedrin described in Talmud Berakhot 28a – see the Bartenura commentary to Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 1, Mishnah 4 for the full list of the eighteen decrees). And specifically, that he consumed half of a loaf, which is an egg and a half worth according to Maimonides and two eggs’ worth according to Rashi. And this is the measurement of food to make the body unfit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר הָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל רִאשׁוֹן וְאֹכֶל שֵׁנִי נַעֲשֶׂה גּוּפוֹ שֵׁנִי.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּלֹא מְשַׁוִּינַן לֵיהּ לָאוֹכֵל כַּמַּאֲכָל עַצְמוֹ, וְהַאי דִּמְשַׁוֵּי לָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי שֵׁנִי וְלֹא שְׁלִישִׁי, לְפִי שֶׁמָּצִינוּ שֵׁנִי עוֹשֶׂה שֵׁנִי עַל יְדֵי מַשְׁקִין, שֶׁהַמַּשְׁקִין שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בְּשֵׁנִי הֵן נַעֲשִׂים תְּחִלָּה וְהַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶם הָוֵי שֵׁנִי, וְאִי הֲוָה מְשַׁוִּינַן לֵיהּ לָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי שְׁלִישִׁי לֹא הָיָה פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, דְּאֵין שְׁלִישִׁי עוֹשֶׂה רְבִיעִי בַּתְּרוּמָה, וַחֲכָמִים גָּזְרוּ עַל הָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, מִשּׁוּם דְּזִמְנִין אָכֵיל אֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים וְשָׁדֵי מַשְׁקִין שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה לְתוֹךְ פִּיו בְּעוֹד שֶׁאֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים בְּפִיו וּפָסֵיל לְהוּ, הִלְכָּךְ אַף עַל גַּב דִּבְאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל רִאשׁוֹן לֹא מְשַׁוִּינַן לֵיהּ לָאוֹכֵל כַּמַּאֲכָל עַצְמוֹ, בְּאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי מְשַׁוִּינַן לֵיהּ שֵׁנִי כְּמוֹ הַמַּאֲכָל, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפְסֹל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


רבי יהושע אומר האוכל אוכל ראשון ואוכל שני נעשה גופו שני – for he holds that we don’t compare for him the one who eats to be like the food itself. For that which compares the person who consumes second-degree [of ritual impurity] food to be second degree [of ritual impurity] and not third-degree [of ritual impurity, because since we found that second degree [of ritual impurity] makes someone second-degree [of ritual impurity] through liquids, for the liquids which were defiled by someone with second-degree [of ritual impurity] are made first degree [of ritual impurity] and a person who comes in contact with them is second-degree [of ritual impurity], but if we would compare to him who consumes food that is second-degree [of ritual impurity] becomes third-degree [of ritual impurity], it would not invalidate the heave-offering/priest’s due, for something that is third-degree [of ritual impurity] does not make something fourth-degree [of ritual impurity] regarding heave-offering/priest’s due, but the Sages decreed on someone who consumes food that is second degree [of ritual impurity] that he would invalidate the priest’s due/heave offering, because sometimes he consumes ritually impure foods and casts liquids of heave-offering into his mouth while the ritually impure foods are in his mouth and invalidates them, therefore, even though that when eating foods that are first-degree [of ritual impurity], we don’t compare him to one who eats like the food itself, that by consuming food that is second-degree [of ritual impurity] we compare him to be second-degree [of ritual impurity] like the food, in order to invalidate the heave-offering/priest’s due.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:3)


**שְׁלִישִׁי שֵׁנִי לַקֹּדֶשׁ.** הָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שְׁלִישִׁי נַעֲשֶׂה גּוּפוֹ שֵׁנִי לַקֹּדֶשׁ, וַעֲבִיד שְׁלִישִׁי וּרְבִיעִי בַּקֹּדֶשׁ:


שלישי שני לקודש – a person who eats food hat is third-degree [of ritual impurity], his body becomes second-degree [of ritual impurity] for Holy Things, and he makes third-degree [of ritual impurity] and fourth degree [of ritual impurity] with Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:4)


**וְלֹא שֵׁנִי לַתְּרוּמָה.** דְּאִי נָגַע בַּתְּרוּמָה לֹא פְּסָלָהּ. וּמִיהוּ אָסוּר הוּא לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה, וְהָכִי מוּכָח בַּגְּמָרָא פֶּרֶק ב'‏ דְּחֻלִּין [דַּף לד]:


ולא שני לתרומה – for if he came in contact with heave-offering, he did not invalidate it, but however, he is prohibited to consume heave-offering, and this is proven in the Gemara in Chapter Two of [Tractate] Hullin [folio 37a].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:2:5)


**בְּחֻלִּין שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה.** כְּלוֹמַר הַאי אוֹכֵל אֹכֶל רִאשׁוֹן וְאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי וּשְׁלִישִׁי דִּתְנִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין, בְּחֻלִּין שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה קָמַיְרֵי, דְּחֻלִּין גְּרֵידָא אֵין בָּהֶם שְׁלִישִׁי:


בחולין שנעשו על הרת תרומה – meaning to say this food is food of first-degree [of ritual impurity] and one who consumes food of second-degree [of ritual impurity] and third-degree [of ritual impurity] that we taught in our Mishnah, we are speaking about unconsecrated produce that was made on the purity of priest-due/heave offering, for mere unconsecrated produce does not have a third-degree [of ritual impurity].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:1)


**הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁבַּחֻלִּין טָמֵא וּמְטַמֵּא.** תְּרוּמָה. שֶׁכִּכָּר שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנָּגַע בְּרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל חֻלִּין, נַעֲשֶׂה שֵׁנִי וּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי:


הראשון שבחולין טמא ומטמא – [defiles] heave-offering/priest’s due, that a loaf of heave-offering that came in contact with first-degree [of ritual impurity] of unconsecrated foods, becomes second-degree [of ritual impurity] and makes third-degree [of ritual impurity] unfit/invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:2)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי.** שֶׁבַּחֻלִּין. פּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא, דִּשְׁלִישִׁי דִּתְרוּמָה לָא עֲבִיד רְבִיעִי, הִלְכָּךְ שְׁלִישִׁי דִּתְרוּמָה לֹא מִקְרֵי טָמֵא אֶלָּא פָּסוּל:


והשני שבחולין – makes heave-offering unfit/invalid but does not defile, for third-degree [of ritual defilement] of heave-offering does not make something fourth-degree [of ritual defilement], therefore third-degree [of ritual defilement] of heave-offering is not considered defiled, but rather unfit/invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:3)


**וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** שֶׁל חֻלִּין. הָכִי קָאָמַר, וְאִם יֵשׁ בְּאוֹתָן חֻלִּין שְׁלִישִׁי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה:


והשלישי של חולין – this is what he said and if there is in those unconsecrated foods third-degree [of ritual impurity] as, for example, that they became through the purification of heave-offering/priest’s due.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:3:4)


**נֶאֱכָל בִּנְזִיד הַדָּמַע.** בְּתַבְשִׁיל [שֶׁתְּרוּמָה] מְעֹרֶבֶת בּוֹ. וְהַתְּרוּמָה קְרוּיָה דֶּמַע, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמות כב) מְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ לֹא תְאַחֵר. וּבְפֶרֶק ב'‏ דְּחֻלִּין מוֹכִיחַ דְּדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁאֵין בִּנְזִיד הַדֶּמַע כַּזַּיִת תְּרוּמָה בִּכְדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס הוּא דְּשָׁרֵי לֵיהּ לֶאֱכֹל אֹכֶל שְׁלִישִׁי, אֲבָל אִי אִיכָּא כַּזַּיִת תְּרוּמָה בִּכְדֵי אֲכִילַת פְּרָס, אָסוּר, דְּקַיְמָא לָן הָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שְׁלִישִׁי מֵחֻלִּין שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה, נִפְסַל גּוּפוֹ מִלֶּאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה:


נאכל בנזיד הדמע (is consumed in a pottage of Holy Things)- in a cooked dish [that heave-offering] is mixed in, for the heave-offering/Terumah is called דמע/Holy Things (i.e., another name for Terumah) , as it is stated (Exodus 22:28): "מלאתך ודמעך לא תאחר [בכור בניך תתן-לי]"/You shall not put off the skimming of the first yield of your vats. [You shall give to Me the first-born among your sons],” and in the second chapter of [Tractate] Hullin (folio 34b), it proves that specifically, when there isn’t in the pottage of Holy Things an olive’s bulk of heave offering incidentally for consuming a certain quantity of bread (i.e., a piece, which is defined by the Tosefta Negaim, Chapter 7, Halakha 10 as half a loaf of which three make a Kab) that permits him to eat food which is third-degree [of ritual impurity], but if there is an olive’s bulk of heave-offering incidentally for consuming a certain quantity of bread (i.e., half a loaf), it is prohibited, for we hold that a person who consumes that which is third-degree [of ritual impurity] from unconsecrated food, that was made through the purification of Terumah/heave-offering, his body is made unfit from consuming heave-offering.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:4:1)


**הָרְבִיעִי נֶאֱכָל בִּנְזִיד הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** כְּלוֹמַר, וְאִם יֵשׁ בְּאוֹתָהּ תְּרוּמָה רְבִיעִי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ. וְכֵן הַחֲמִישִׁי נֶאֱכָל בִּנְזִיד הַקֹּדֶשׁ דְּקָתָנֵי לְקַמָּן, הָכִי קָאָמַר, וְאִם יֵשׁ בְּאוֹתוֹ קֹדֶשׁ חֲמִישִׁי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה עַל טָהֳרַת חַטָּאת שֶׁל אֵפֶר פָּרָה, דְּאָמְרִינַן בִּגְדֵי אוֹכְלֵי קֹדֶשׁ מִדְרָס לְחַטָּאת נֶאֱכָל בִּנְזִיד הַקֹּדֶשׁ, מֻתָּר לְעָרֵב בּוֹ תַּבְשִׁיל שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ טָהוֹר:


הרביעי נאכל בנזיד הקודש (And [heave offering] in the fourth degree of ritual impurity is eaten in a pottage of Holy Things) – that is to say, that if there is in that heave offering fourth degree [of ritual impurity], as, for example, that was prepared in conditions of cleanness pertaining to Holy Things, and similarly the fifth degree [of ritual impurity] that is eaten in a pottage of Holy things, as is taught further on (in Mishnah 5 of this chapter) , this is how what he said: and if there is that Holy Thing fifth-degree [of ritual impurity] as, for example, that it was prepared in conditions of cleanness of a sin-offering of the ashes of the Red Heifer, as we stated, the clothing of those who consume Holy Things are [ritual impurity imparted] through treading (usually by someone with gonorrhea, a woman with an issue, a woman after childbirth and/or a menstruating woman) for a sin-offering is eaten in a pottage of Holy Things, it is permissible to combine in it a cooked dish of pure Holy Things.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:6:1)


**הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁבְּחֻלִּין מְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקֵה חֻלִּין.** דְּכָל הַפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה מְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקִים לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה:


השני שבחולין מטמא משקה חולין – for all that invalidates/renders unfit the heave-offering/priest’s due defiles liquids to become first-degree [of ritual impurity] (see Tractate Parah, Chapter 8, Mishnah 7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:6:2)


**מְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקֵה קֹדֶשׁ.** דְּכָל הַפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ מְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקִין (קדש) לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה לַקֹּדֶשׁ:


מטמא משקה קודש – that all which invalidates/renders unfit Holy Things defiles (holy) liquids to be first degree [of ritual impurity] for Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:6:3)


**שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** כְּלוֹמַר, הָא דְּאָמְרִינַן דִּשְׁלִישִׁי שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה פּוֹסֵל וְלֹא מְטַמֵּא אֳכָלֵי קֹדֶשׁ, הָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ. אֲבָל נַעֲשֵׂית עַל טָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה, שֶׁלֹּא שְׁמָרָהוּ עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ, הַשְּׁלִישִׁי מְטַמֵּא שְׁנַיִם וּפוֹסֵל אֶחָד בַּקֹּדֶשׁ. שֶׁטָּהֳרָתָהּ שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה טֻמְאָה הִיא אֵצֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ, וַהֲרֵי זֶה הַשְּׁלִישִׁי דִּתְרוּמָה חָשׁוּב אֵצֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ כְּאִלּוּ נִטְמָא בַּשֶּׁרֶץ עַצְמוֹ:


שנעשו על טהרת הקודש (that was prepared in conditions of cleanness pertaining to Holy Things) – meaning to say, that which we said, that heave offering of third-degree [of ritual purity] makes unfit/invalidates but does not defile foods of Holy Things, these words apply when they were prepared in conditions of cleanness pertaining to Holy Things. But if it they were prepared in conditions of cleanness pertaining to heave offering/priest’s due, that it was not protected/guarded in conditions of cleanness pertaining to Holy Thins, the third-degree [of ritual impurity] defiles the second degree and makes unfit/invalidates first-degree [of ritual impurity] of Holy Things. For the ritual purity of heave-offering is impure next to Holy Things, for it is considered third-degree [of ritual impurity] of heave offering next to the Holy Things as if it was defiled by a moving creature/unclean reptile itself.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:7:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר שְׁלָשְׁתָּן שָׁוִין.** כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל, דְּלֵית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר טָהֳרָתָהּ שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה טֻמְאָה הִיא אֵצֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


ר' אליעזר אומר שלשתן שוין – as he explains further on, for Rabbi Eliezer does not hold the purity of heave-offering which is Levitical uncleanness next to Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:7:2)


**הַשְּׁלִישִׁי שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן.** לֹא מַשְׁכַּחַת שְׁלִישִׁי בְּחֻלִּין אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נַעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת תְּרוּמָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


השלישי שבכולן – third-degree of [ritual defilement] is not found with unconsecrated foods unless they were made through the purity of heave-offering. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי לֹא יַעֲשֶׂה בְּבֵית הַבָּד.** לֹא יִתְעַסֵּק לַעֲשׂוֹת שֶׁמֶן בְּבֵית הַבַּד, דְּהָא מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לְעֵיל לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר שֶׁהָאוֹכֵל אֹכֶל שֵׁנִי הוּא עַצְמוֹ נַעֲשֶׂה שֵׁנִי וּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה וּמְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקִין לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה, (דהינו השמן):


האוכל אוכל שני לא יעשה בבית הבד – he should not engage in the making of [olive] oil in the olive press, for Rabbi Yehoshua above (in Mishnah 2 of our chapter) agrees with Rabbi Eliezer that a person who consumes food that is second degree [of ritual impurity] he himself becomes second degree [of ritual impurity and invalidates the heave offering and defiles liquids to be first degree [of ritual impurity] (that is the oil).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:2)


**עַל גַּב הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


על גב הקודש – on the purification of Holy Things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:3)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ כְחֻלִּין.** וְאֵין בָּאִים לִכְלַל שְׁלִישִׁי:


הרי אלו כחולין – and they don’t come into the category of third-degree [of ritual impurity].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/2:8:4)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ כִתְרוּמָה.** וְיֵשׁ בָּהֶם שְׁלִישִׁי. וְהָנֵי תַּנָּאֵי תַּרְוַיְהוּ סְבִירָא לְהוּ חֻלִּין שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ עַל טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ לָאו כְּקֹדֶשׁ דָּמוּ, וְשֶׁאֵין לְךָ דָּבָר שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה רְבִיעִי בַּקֹּדֶשׁ אֶלָּא קֹדֶשׁ מְקֻדָּשׁ בִּלְבַד, וּפְלִיגָא אַמַּתְנִיתִין דִּלְעֵיל דְּקָתָנֵי הַשְּׁלִישִׁי שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן פּוֹסֵל לְאֳכָלֵי קֹדֶשׁ:


הרי אלו כתרומה – and they do have a third degree [of ritual impurity]. And these Tannaim/Mishnaic teachers both of them (see the previous Mishnah) hold that unconsecrated foods that were made through the purification of Holy Things are not similar to Holy Things, for there isn’t anything that makes fourth degree [of ritual impurity] other than Holy Things from Holy Things alone. And they dispute with the Mishnah above (i.e., the previous Mishnah) that teaches that “third degree [of ritual impurity] spoils foods of Holy Things”/"השלישי שבכולן ...פוסל לאוכלי קודש".



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:1)


**הָרֹטֶב וְהַגְּרִיסִין.** שֶׁהֵן מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ. שֶׁהֵן לַחִין כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן טוֹפֵחַ עַל מְנָת לְהַטְפִּיחַ:


הרוטב והגריסין שהן משקה טופח (the broth/juice/jelly of meats, and the pounded beans which moisture dripping on them/running liquid) – they are so moist when they have on them enough to moisten other objects.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:2)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ תְּחִלָּה.** דַּחֲשׁוּבִים כְּמַשְׁקִין:


הרי הן תחילה -that they are considered as a liquid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:3)


**קָרְשׁוּ הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ שְׁנִיִּים.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁנִּקְרְשׁוּ, חֲשׁוּבִים כָּאֳכָלִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בְּמַשְׁקִין:


קרשו הרי אלו שניים (if they became congealed, they are second-degree [of ritual impurity]) – for since they congealed they are considered like edibles that came in contact with liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:1:4)


**חָזְרוּ וְנִמֹּחוּ כַּבֵּיצָה מְכֻוָּן טָהוֹר.** דְּיָצָא מֵהֶן תּוֹרַת אֹכֶל וְיָרַד עֲלֵיהֶן תּוֹרַת מַשְׁקֶה, וּפָרְחָה טֻמְאָתוֹ כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנּוּ, וְאֵין לְמַשְׁקֶה זֶה מִי שֶׁיְּטַמְּאֶנּוּ, דִּמְכִי יָצָאת טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה אַמְעִיט הָאֹכֶל מִכַּבֵּיצָה, וּפָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה אֵין מְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים. אֲבָל יוֹתֵר מִכַּבֵּיצָה טָמֵא, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא, דִּמְכִי יָצָאת טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה נִטְמֵאת אוֹתָהּ טִפָּה בְּכַבֵּיצָה אֹכֶל טָמֵא, וּכְשֶׁנִּמְחָה אַחַר כָּךְ כֻּלּוֹ חָזְרָה הַטִּפָּה וְטִמְּאָה אֶת הַכֹּל:


חזרו ונמוחו כביצה מכוון טהור (if they once again dissolved – if they are the volume of an egg exactly, it is pure) – that the status of food left from them and the status of liquid descended upon them, and their defilement blossomed when they were changed, but there isn’t to this liquid anything that will defile it, for when the first drop departed, the food was reduced from that of the size of an egg’s bulk, and less than an egg’s bulk it doesn’t defile others, but more than an egg’s bulk it is impure, as the reason is explained [in the Mishnah] , for when the first drop departed , that drop was defiled with an egg’s bulk of impure food, and when all of it dissolved afterwards, the drop returned and defiled everything.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:1)


**הַשֶּׁמֶן תְּחִלָּה לְעוֹלָם.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁקָּרַשׁ אֵינוֹ יוֹצֵא מִתּוֹרַת מַשְׁקֶה:


השמן תחילה לעולם – and even though it congealed it doesn’t depart from the status of liquid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:2)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁזוּרִי אוֹמֵר אַף הַיָּיִן.** שֶׁקָּרַשׁ אֵינוֹ יוֹצֵא מִתּוֹרַת מַשְׁקֶה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁזוּרִי:


ר' שמעון שזורי אומר אף היין – [wine] that congealed does not depart from the status of liquid. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon Shezuri.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:3)


**גּוּשׁ שֶׁל זֵיתִים.** זֵיתִים הַרְבֵּה מְחֻבָּרִים יַחַד. וּבְגוּשׁ טָמֵא אַיְרֵי:


וגוש של זיתים (lump/clod of olives) – many olives that are attached together. And we are speaking about an impure lump/clod.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:4)


**כַּבֵּיצָה מְכֻוָּן טָהוֹר.** הַתַּנּוּר. דְּאֵין אֹכֶל מְטַמֵּא כְּלִי. וּמַשְׁקֶה הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַגּוּשׁ מֵחֲמַת הֶסֵּק אֵין לוֹ בַּמֶּה יְטַמֵּא, דְּמִכֵּיוָן דְּיָצָאת טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה חָסֵר לֵיהּ מִכַּבֵּיצָה. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּגּוּשׁ יוֹתֵר מִכַּבֵּיצָה, כְּשֶׁיָּצָאת טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה עֲדַיִן נִשְׁאַר בַּגּוּשׁ כַּבֵּיצָה, וְנִטְמְאָה הַטִּפָּה בַּגּוּשׁ, וְחָזְרָה לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה, וְטִמְּאָה הַטִּפָּה אֶת הַתַּנּוּר, שֶׁמַּשְׁקִין מְטַמְּאִים כְּלִי:


בביצה מכוון טהור – the oven [is pure], for food does not defile a vessel. But liquid that comes out from the clod/lump on account of heating, it has nothing for what that it defiles, for since the first drop [of liquid] departed [upon heating], it is missing from an egg’s bulk, but when there is in the clod/lump more than an egg’s bulk, when the first drop exudes there still remained in the clod/lump the equivalent of an egg’s bulk, and when the drop in the clod/lump is defiled, and it returned to being first-degree [of ritual impurity], the drop defiled the oven, for liquids defile a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:5)


**אִם הָיוּ פְרוּדִים.** אִם הָיוּ הַזֵּיתִים פְּרוּדִים, שֶׁאֵינָן גּוּשׁ:


אם היו פירודים – if the olives were separate that they are not a lump/clod.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:2:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ הֵן מֵאָה.** כְּלוֹמַר אֲפִלּוּ הֵן שִׁעוּר מֵאָה בֵּיצִים אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין לְכַבֵּיצָה לְטַמֵּא אֶת שֶׁמֶן הַיּוֹצֵא, לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה:


אפילו הן מאה – meaning to say even if they are the measurement of one-hundred eggs, they do not combine to be an egg’s bulk to defile the oil that exudes to become first-degree [of ritual defilement].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:3:1)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע בַּמַּשְׁקִין.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁדְּרָכָן בִּפְשׁוּטֵי כְּלֵי עֵץ שֶׁאֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִים, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵין כָּאן מִי שֶׁיְּטַמֵּא הַמַּשְׁקִין אֶלָּא הַזָּגִין שֶׁנָּגַע בָּהֶן טְמֵא מֵת, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא נִזְהַר מִנְּגִיעָתָן אֶלָּא מִשֶּׁיָּצָא מַשְׁקֶה, וּכְשֶׁהַמַּשְׁקִין נוֹגְעִים בַּזָּגִין כְּבָר חָסַר שִׁעוּר כַּבֵּיצָה, וְאֵין אֹכֶל מְטַמֵּא בְּפָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה:


ובלבד שלא יגע במשקין – as for example, that he pressed them with flat wooden implements that do not become defiled , for now there is nothing here that would defile the liquids other than the husk/pomace of grapes that someone defiled by a corpse had touched , for he was not careful regarding their contact other than when liquid exudes, and when the liquids come in contact with the husks/pomace of grapes there is already missing the measurement of an egg’s bulk. But food does not defile with less than an egg’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:3:2)


**יוֹתֵר מִכַּבֵּיצָה טָמֵא.** שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁיָּצְאָה טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה נִטְמֵאת בְּכַבֵּיצָה, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין מַשְׁקֶה הַנִּבְלָע בָּאֹכֶל מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה עִמּוֹ, מַשְׁלִים הוּא אֶת שִׁעוּרוֹ. דְּדִכְוָתֵיהּ אַשְׁכְּחַן בְּסָמוּךְ גַּבֵּי כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת וּמִן הַנְּבֵלָה שֶׁנִּתְמַעֲטוּ בַּחַמָּה וְהִנִּיחָן בַּגְּשָׁמִים וְתָפְחוּ, דִּגְשָׁמִים הַמֻּבְלָעִים מַשְׁלִימִין לְכַשִּׁעוּר:


יותר מכביצה טמא - that since the first drop had exuded, it became defiled with the equivalent of an egg’s bulk, for even though the liquid that is absorbed in the food is not susceptible to receive ritual defilement with it, it completes its measurement. For like it, we find adjacent (in the next Mishnah) regarding an olive’s bulk from a corpse or from a carrion that had been reduced/shrank in the sun and he placed them in the rain and they swelled up, for the rains that are absorbed/swallowed up complete to make up the measurement.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:4:1)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ טְהוֹרִים.** אַכַּבֵּיצָה אֳכָלִים וְאַכַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת וְכַזַּיִת מִן הַנְּבֵלָה וְכָעֲדָשָׁה מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ קָאֵי:


הרי אלו טהורים – an egg’s bulk they consume and it is referring to an olives’ bulk from a corpse and an olive’s bulk from a carrion and a lentil’s bulk from the a creeping insect/reptile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:4:2)


**הִנִּיחָן בַּגְּשָׁמִים וְנִתְפְּחוּ טְמֵאִין.** דְּכָל הַשִּׁעוּרִים בָּתַר גּוֹדְלַיְהוּ אָזְלִינַן. וּמֵהָכָא קַיְמָא לָן דְּאֵין דִּחוּי אֵצֶל אִסּוּרִים:


הניחן בגשמים ונתפחו טמאין – for all of the measurement that we follow are after their size/greatness. And from here, we hold that there is no suspension/removal of an object from its purpose regarding prohibitions.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:1)


**אִם טְמֵאוֹת טְמֵאוֹת.** כִּי הָא דִּתְנַן לְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק הַשֶּׁרֶץ וְהַצְּפַרְדֵּעַ [מִשְׁנָה ז'] נָגַע בְּאֶחָד בַּלַּיְלָה וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם חַי אִם מֵת וּבַשַּׁחַר עָמַד וּמְצָאוֹ מֵת, חֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִים, שֶׁכָּל הַטֻּמְאוֹת כִּשְׁעַת מְצִיאָתָן. וְדַוְקָא בִּמְקוֹם מְצִיאָתָן, לְמַעוֹטֵי נָגַע בְּאֶחָד בַּלַּיְלָה בְּזָוִית זוֹ וּלְמָחָר מְצָאוֹ מֵת בְּזָוִית אַחֶרֶת וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּזֶה:


אם טמאות טמאות – like that which is taught further on in the Mishnah in the Chapter "השרץ והצרפדע" [Tractate Taharot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 7]: “If one touched someone at night, and it is not known if he is alive or dead, and in the morning he arose and found him dead,… the Sages declare unclean….for all unclean things [are adjudged] as they are at the time that they are found.” And specifically, in the place where they are found, to exclude if he touched someone at night in this corner and on the morrow found him dead in another corner and other things similar to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:2)


**אִם מְכֻסּוֹת מְכֻסּוֹת.** כְּגוֹן כְּלִי חֶרֶס הַמֻּקָּף צָמִיד פָּתִיל, לֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהָיָה הַמֵּת בָּאֹהֶל הָיָה מְגֻלֶּה וְעַכְשָׁיו מְכֻסֶּה, אוֹ אִם מְצָאוֹ מְגֻלֶּה לֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהָיָה הַמֵּת (באהל) הָיָה מְכֻסֶּה וּמֻקָּף צָמִיד פָּתִיל:


אם מכוסות מכוסות – as, for example, an earthenware vessel that is surrounded by an airtight lid , we don’t say that perhaps at the time that the corpse was in the tent, it was uncovered and now it is covered, or if they found it uncovered, we don’t say that perhaps at the time that the corpse (was in the tent) it was covered and surrounded by an airtight lid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:3)


**מַחַט שֶׁנִּמְצֵאת מְלֵאָה חֲלֻדָּה.** שֶׁמְּעַכֶּבֶת אֶת הַתְּפִירָה, דַּחֲלֻדָּה כָּזוֹ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ מִידֵי טֻמְאָתָהּ:


מחט שנמצאת מלאה חלודה (a needle that was found filled with mold/rust) – that prevents the sewing, for mold/rust like this purifies it from its defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:5:4)


**אוֹ שְׁבוּרָה טְהוֹרָה.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא כְּשֶׁהָיְתָה שְׁלֵמָה נִטְמְאָה וּכְשֶׁיְּתַקְּנוּהָ וְתַחֲזֹר שְׁלֵמָה תַּחֲזֹר לְטֻמְאָתָהּ הַיְשָׁנָה. וְהַיְנוּ פֵּרוּשָׁא דְּאִם טְהוֹרוֹת טְהוֹרוֹת דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל:


או שבורה טהורה - and we don’t say that perhaps that it when it was whole it was defiled, and when he would repair it and it would return whole, it would return to its earlier/older defilement, and this is the explanation – that if they are pure, they are pure, as is taught above.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:6:1)


**בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא וְהַאי מָבוֹי רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הוּא, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי כֵּיוָן דִּסְפֵקָא הָוֵי בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל, כְּגוֹן חֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן, סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר, דְּהָכִי יָלְפִינַן מִקְּרָאֵי דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא ז] וְהַבָּשָׂר אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע בְּכָל טָמֵא לֹא יֵאָכֵל, מַשְׁמַע וַדַּאי טָמֵא לֹא יֵאָכֵל, סָפֵק טָמֵא יֵאָכֵל, וּכְתִיב כָּל טָהוֹר יֹאכַל בָּשָׂר, וַדַּאי טָהוֹר הוּא דְּיֹאכַל, הָא סָפֵק טָמֵא סָפֵק טָהוֹר לֹא יֹאכַל, אֶלָּא לָאו שְׁמַע מִנַּהּ כָּאן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל כָּאן שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל:
