בחזקת טהרה – for even though that regarding doubtful ritual impurity in the private domain is considered as doubtfully impure and this alleyway is the private domain, nevertheless since the doubt is in a matter where it the temperament/disposition [of the participants] is lacking to be able to be asked, as for example, a deaf-mute, imbecile and/or a minor child, he is considered pure in his status of doubt, for this we derive from Scriptural verses, as it is written (Leviticus 7:19): “Flesh that touches anything impure shall not be eaten.” This implies that something that is definitely impure shall not be eaten; if it is doubtfully impure it may be eaten, as it is written (Leviticus 7:19): “Only he who is pure may eat such flesh.” Someone who is definitely pure may eat it, but if he is doubtfully impure/doubtfully pure, he shall not eat it. But is it not that we learn from it here that when he has awareness/understanding to be interrogated, [as opposed to] when he doesn’t have awareness/understanding to be interrogated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:6:2)


**וְכָל הַפִּקֵּחַ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל. אִם לֹא יָדַע אִם הָלַךְ בִּמְקוֹם טֻמְאָה אִם לָאו, בְּחֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה הוּא. דִּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא, מִסּוֹטָה יָלְפִינַן, דִּסְפֵקָא הִיא אִם נִטְמֵאת אִם לָאו, וְסָפֵק זֶה בְּמָקוֹם סְתִירָה הוּא דְּהַיְנוּ רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וְאָמַר רַחֲמָנָא וְנִסְתְּרָה וְהִיא נִטְמָאָה, וְסוֹטָה יֵשׁ בָּהּ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל אִם נִטְמְאָה אִם לָאו:


וכל הפקח – that he has awareness/understanding to be interrogated. If he does not know if he walked in a place of defilement or not, he is under the presumption of defilement. For someone of doubtful defilement in the private domain, his [presumption of] doubt is impure which we derive from [the case of] the Sotah/woman suspected by her husband of being unfaithful, and there is a doubt if she is defiled or not and this doubt is in a place of a married woman’s hiding with a man under suspicious circumstances, that is, in the private domain, and the All-Merciful stated (Numbers 5:13): “and she keeps secret the fat that she has defiled herself without being forced,” but a woman suspected by her husband of being unfaithful has awareness/understanding to be interrogated if she has been defiled or not.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:1)


**וְאֵין הַשּׁוֹשַׁנִּים אֶלָּא בִמְקוֹם הַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיִּקַּח הַשּׁוֹשַׁנִּים אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יִכָּנֵס לְבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת:


ואין השושנים אלא במקום טומאה – for it is impossible that he (i.e., the young child) would take the lilies unless he entered into the cemetery.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:2)


**טָהוֹר.** כֵּיוָן דְּאִיכָּא לְמִתְלֵי בְּאַחֵר, שֶׁמָּא לִקְּטָן אַחֵר וּנְתָנָן לוֹ, דְּתִינוֹק אֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל וּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר:


טהור – since it is possible to be placed beside him by another [person], perhaps another child picked/gathered them and gave them to him, for a child lacks awareness/understanding to be interrogated and anything where there is suspicion] of doubt [concerning him] is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:3)


**וְכֵן חֲמוֹר.** שֶׁהָיָה עוֹמֵד בִּמְקוֹם טָהֳרָה וְלֹא יָדְעִינַן אִם נִכְנַס בִּמְקוֹם טֻמְאָה:


וכן חמור – that was standing in a place of ritual purity and we do no know if it entered into the place of defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:7:4)


**כֵּלָיו טְהוֹרִים.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל:


כליו טהורים – because it lacks awareness/knowledge to be interrogated.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:1)


**תִּינוֹק שֶׁנִּמְצָא בְצַד עִסָּה.** תִּינוֹק טָמֵא שֶׁנִּמְצָא בְּצַד עִסָּה טְהוֹרָה וַחֲתִיכָה שֶׁל עִסָּה זוֹ בְּיָדוֹ:


תינוק שנמצא בצד עיסה – a ritually impure child that is found at the side of started dough and a piece of this started dough was in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:2)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַהֵר.** רַבִּי מֵאִיר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּחָיֵשׁ לְמִעוּטָא וְסָבַר דְּרֹב תִּינוֹקוֹת מְטַפְּחִים בְּעִסָּה, וְשֶׁמָּא לָקַח הוּא עַצְמוֹ אֶת הַבָּצֵק וְטִמֵּא אֶת הָעִסָּה, וּמִעוּט אֵין מְטַפְּחִים וְשֶׁמָּא אָדָם טָהוֹר לָקַח וְנָתַן לוֹ, וְעִסָּה זוֹ בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה עוֹמֶדֶת, סְמֹךְ מִעוּטָא לַחֲזָקָה וְאִתְּרַע לַהּ רֻבָּא, וְהָוֵי פַּלְגָּא וּפַלְגָּא וְטָהוֹר לְעִנְיַן תִּינוֹק שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל:


ר' מאיר מטהר – Rabbi Meir according to his reasoning who took into consideration the minority (i.e., the possibility of the rarer cases) and held that the majority of small children slap the started dough, and perhaps he himself took the risen dough and defiled the started dough, but the minority [of young children] do not slap but perhaps a ritually pure person took it and gave it to him, this started dough remains in the presumption of ritual purity, for this minority is near to a presumption and the majority was impaired/weakened, and it was half-and-half and that is ritually pure regarding a young child who lacks the awareness/knowledge to be interrogated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִים.** לְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְּלֹא חָיְשֵׁי לְמִעוּטָא, וְרֹב תִּינוֹקוֹת מְטַפְּחִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטמאים – according to their rationale that they are not concerned about a minority, for most young children slap [the dough]. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:4)


**נְקִירַת.** נְקָבִים נְקָבִים יֵשׁ בָּעִסָּה מֵחֲמַת הַתַּרְנְגוֹלִים שֶׁנִקְּרוּ בָּהּ:


נקירת – there are small incisions/pickings in the started dough on account of the hens that pecked in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:5)


**שֶׁיְּנַגְּבוּ אֶת פִּיהֶם בָּאָרֶץ.** דֶּרֶךְ הַתַּרְנְגוֹלִים לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁתוּ מְנַגְּבִין פִּיהֶן בָּאָרֶץ, הִלְכָּךְ אִם יֵשׁ קַרְקַע מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּין מַשְׁקִין לַכִּכָּרוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁיּוּכְלוּ לְנַגֵּב בָּהּ פִּיהֶן לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁתוּ, הַכִּכָּרוֹת טְהוֹרִים. וְאִם לָאו, טְמֵאִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִטְמְאוּ בְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים שֶׁהָיָה בְּפִיהֶן שֶׁל תַּרְנְגוֹלִים. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְשֶׁיֵּשׁ קַרְקַע מַפְסֶקֶת מְסַפְּקָא לָן אִם נִגְּבוּ פִּיהֶן אִם לֹא, הָוֵי סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל, וּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר:


שינגבו את פיהן בארץ – it is the manner of hens after they drank [water], they dry their mouths on the ground. Therefore, if there is land that forms a partition between the liquids to the loaves [of dough] in order that they can dry their mouths on it after they drank, the loaves [of dough] are ritually pure. But if not, they are impure, for they were defiled with impure liquids that were in the mouths of the hens. But even though that when the land forms a partition, we are doubtful if their mouths were dried or not, this is a doubtful defilement in a matter that there isn’t awareness/knowledge to be interrogated, and its doubt is [nevertheless deemed] ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 3:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/3:8:6)


**שֶׁהוּא פִקֵּחַ.** וְאֵין דַּרְכּוֹ לִשְׁתּוֹת כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא מוֹצֵא לֶאֱכֹל, שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר כָּל שָׁעָה מַשְׁקִין מְצוּיִין וְאֵין כָּל שָׁעָה אֳכָלִין מְצוּיִין. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב:


שהוא פיקח – for it is not his manner to drink all the time that he finds food [to consume]. For he (i.e., Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov) says that whenever liquids are found but food is not always found. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:1:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵק.** כִּכָּר לְבֵין הַמַּפְתְּחוֹת. לֹא שְׁנָא זָרַק כִּכָּר טָהוֹר לְבֵין מַפְתְּחוֹת טְמֵאוֹת, וְלֹא שְׁנָא זָרַק מַפְתְּחוֹת טְמֵאוֹת לְבֵין הַכִּכָּרוֹת, וְלֹא נוֹדַע אִם נָגַע הַכִּכָּר הַטָּהוֹר בַּמַּפְתְּחוֹת הַטְּמֵאוֹת, טָהוֹר הַכִּכָּר, דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְהַאי תַּנָּא זוֹרֵק כְּמִי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל דָּמֵי:


הזורק. ככר לבין המטפחות – there is no difference whether he threw a ritually pure loaf among ritually impure keys, or whether he threw impure keys among the [ritually pure] loaves, and it is not known if the loaf came in contact with/touched the ritually impure keys, the loaf is ritually pure, for the [anonymous] Tanna/teacher of this Mishnah holds that someone who throws is like one who lacks the awareness/knowledge to be interrogated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:1:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כִּכָּר לְבֵין הַמַּפְתְּחוֹת טָמֵא.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה זוֹרֵק כְּמִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל דָּמֵי, וּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא. מִיהוּ כְּשֶׁזָּרַק [מַפְתֵּחַ טָמֵא בֵּין כִּכָּרוֹת], טָהוֹר, הוֹאִיל וְהַטֻּמְאָה אֵינָהּ נָחָה בַּמָּקוֹם אֶלָּא עוֹבֶרֶת עַל הַטָּהוֹר, סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וְכִכָּר וּמַפְתְּחוֹת דְּנָקַט בְּמַתְנִיתִין, הוּא הַדִּין לְכָל שְׁאָר דְּבָרִים הַנִּזְרָקִים, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּאוֹרְחַיְהוּ דֶּאֱינָשֵׁי לִזְרֹק כִּכָּר וּמַפְתְּחוֹת זֶה לָזֶה, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי נָקַט הָנֵי:


ר' יהודה אומר ככשר לבין המפתחות טמא – for Rabbi Yehuda holds that a person who throws (whether impure keys or loaves) has awareness/knowledge to be interrogated, and his [status of] doubt makes him ritually impure. However, when he threw [a ritually impure key among the loaves], it (i.e., the loaf) is ritually pure, since the defilement does not come to rest in a place but rather passes over the ritually pure [loaf], its doubt is [presumed to be] ritually pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda. And the [ritually pure] loaf and the [ritually impure] keys that our Mishnah uses, the same law applies for all the rest of the things that are thrown, but because it is the practice of people to throw a loaf and keys one to the other, because of that, [the Mishnah] took this [example].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:2:1)


**סָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וַאֲפִלּוּ אָדָם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל אוֹחֵז בַּטֻּמְאָה. שֶׁאֵין לַטֻּמְאָה מָקוֹם שֶׁתְּהֵא נָחָה בּוֹ, שֶׁכָּל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא בְּפִי הַחֻלְדָּה אוֹ בְּפִי הַכֶּלֶב אֵינָהּ חֲשׁוּבָה נָחָה בַּמָּקוֹם, אֶלָּא טֻמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת הִיא, הִלְכָּךְ סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר, וַאֲפִלּוּ אוֹחֵז בַּטֻּמְאָה הָאָדָם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל:


ספק נגע ספק לא נגע ספיקו טהור – and even in the private domain, and even regarding an individual who has awareness/knowledge to be interrogated who is holding the defilement/defiling object, because the defilement has no place that it rests upon, for all the while that it is in the mouth of the weasel or in the mouth of the dog it is not considered to be resting in any place, but rather it is passing defilement, therefore, its matter of doubt is deemed to be ritually pure, and even if the one who is holding the defilement/defiling object has awareness/knowledge to be interrogated.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:3:1)


**הָיוּ מְנַקְּרִין בָּהֶן.** אִם הִשְׁלִיכוּ הַחֻלְדָּה אֶת הַשֶּׁרֶץ, וְהַכֶּלֶב אֶת הַנְּבֵלָה, מִפִּיהֶם עַל הָאָרֶץ וְהָיוּ מְנַקְּרִים בָּהּ, הֲרֵי יֵשׁ מָקוֹם לַטֻּמְאָה וּסְפֵקָהּ טָמֵא:


היו מנקרין בהן – if the weasel cast off the creeping animal or the dog [cast off] the carrion that is in their mouths onto the ground and they would peck at it, there is a place for the defilement and its manner of doubt is ritually impure.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:1)


**סְפֵק אָדָם.** סָפֵק הֶאֱהִיל עַל הָאָדָם:


ספק אדם – there is doubt if it (i.e., the raven with an olive’s bulk of a corpse in its mouth) overshadowed over a person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:2)


**טָמֵא.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל. וְאֵינוֹ טָהוֹר מִטַּעֲמָא שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בְּפִי הָעוֹרֵב וְהִיא טֻמְאָה עוֹבֶרֶת, דְּכָל הַמַּאֲהִילִין חֲשׁוּבוֹת יֵשׁ לַטֻּמְאָה מָקוֹם, וַאֲפִלּוּ הַנִּזְרָקִין:


טמא – for he has awareness/knowledge to be interrogated, and he is not ritually pure for the reason that the defilement [of the olive’s bulk of a corpse] is in the mouth of the raven and it is passing defilement , and everything that overshadows is considered that there is defilement in the palce, and even that which is thrown.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:3)


**סְפֵק כֵּלִים.** סָפֵק הֶאֱהִיל עַל הַכֵּלִים:


ספק כלים – there is doubt whether it (i.e., the raven) overshadowed [with the olive’s bulk of a corpse in its mouth] over the vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:4)


**טָהוֹר.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶן דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל:


טהור – for they lack awareness/knowledge to be interrogated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:5)


**הַמְמַלֵּא בַעֲשָׂרָה דְלָיִים.** זֶה אַחַר זֶה:


הממלא בעשה דליים – one after another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:6)


**וְנִמְצָא שֶׁרֶץ בְּאַחַד מֵהֶן הוּא טָמֵא וְכֻלָּן טְהוֹרִין.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁאֵין [לַדְּלִי] אֳגָנִים. אֲבָל יֵשׁ לָהּ אֳגָנִים, כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִים, דְּשֶׁמָּא הַשֶּׁרֶץ הָיָה בָּרִאשׁוֹן, וְהַאי דְּלֹא נָפַל עִם הַמַּיִם אֵימוּר אֳגָנִים עִכְּבוּהוּ:


ונמצא שרץ באחד מהן הוא טמא וכלן טהורים – and these words [refer to the situation] where [the bucket] lacks basins, all of them are ritually impure, for perhaps the creeping animal was the first [inside], and that which didn’t fall in with the water, we state that the basins prevented it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:4:7)


**הָעֶלְיוֹן טָהוֹר.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא מִן הָעֶלְיוֹן נָפַל לַתַּחְתּוֹן:


העליון טהור – [but we are not concerned that perhaps it was from the upper one] it (i.e., the creeping animal) fell to the lower one (i.e., vessel).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:1)


**עַל סְפֵק בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** כְּגוֹן תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנִּכְנְסָה לְבֵית הַפְּרָס, דְּהוּא שָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר וְסָפֵק אִם יֵשׁ בָּהּ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה מִן הַמֵּת:


על ספק בית הפרס - heave offering/priest’s due that entered into an area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse, that this is a field in which a grave was plowed up and it is doubtful if there within a bone a barley-corn’s bulk from the corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:2)


**וְעַל סְפֵק עָפָר הַבָּא מֵאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים.** דְּכָל עָפָר הַבָּא מֵאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים מְסַפְּקִינַן לֵיהּ בְּרָקָב שֶׁל מֵת. וּשְׁנֵי אֵלֶּה, אֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים וּבֵית הַפְּרָס, גָּזְרוּ בָּהֶם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְטַמְּאִים בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא:


ועל ספק עפר הבא מארץ העמים – that dust that comes from the lands of the nations, we have doubts regarding the mass of earth from a grave containing parts of a decayed human body. And it is taught concerning the land of the nations/ארץ העמים and the area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse/בית הפרס, they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed that they would defile through contact and through lifting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:3)


**וְעַל סְפֵקָן שֶׁל בִּגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ.** דְּכֻלְּהוּ מְסַפְּקָא לָן אִם יָשְׁבָה עֲלֵיהֶן אִשְׁתּוֹ נִדָּה:


ועל ספיקן של בגדי עם הארץ – for regarding all of them we are in doubt if his menstruating wife sat upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:4)


**וְעַל סְפֵק כֵּלִים הַנִּמְצָאִים.** דְּלֹא יָדְעִינַן אִם טְמֵאִים הֵן:


ועל ספק כלים הנמצאים – for we don’t know if they are ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:5)


**וְעַל סְפֵק רֻקִּין.** דְּשֶׁמָּא שֶׁל זָב וְזָבָה הֵן, אוֹ דְּנִדָּה וְיוֹלֶדֶת:


ועל ספק רוקין – for perhaps they are of a person with gonorrhea or a woman with flux, or that of a menstruating woman or someone who just gave birth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:6)


**מֵי רַגְלֵי אָדָם שֶׁהֵן כְּנֶגֶד מֵי רַגְלֵי בְהֵמָה.** רְבוּתָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן, דְּלֹא אָמְרִינַן מִדְּהָנֵי דִּבְהֵמָה הָנֵי נַמִּי דִּבְהֵמָה, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִיכָּא תְּרֵי סְפֵקֵי לְקֻלָּא, סָפֵק דִּבְהֵמָה סָפֵק דְּאָדָם, וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם תִּמְצֵי לוֹמַר דְּאָדָם, שֶׁמָּא דְּאָדָם טָהוֹר. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ עַל פִּי הַתּוֹסֶפְתָּא, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ מֵי רַגְלֵי אָדָם טָמֵא עִם מֵי [רַגְלֵי] בְּהֵמָה חֲצִי בַּחֲצִי בְּשָׁוֶה, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד מֵי רַגְלֵי בְּהֵמָה, וּמְסַפְּקָא לָן אִם בָּטְלוּ מַרְאֵיהֶן שֶׁל מֵי רַגְלֵי אָדָם וְיִהְיוּ כֻּלָּן טְהוֹרִין, אוֹ לֹא בָּטְלוּ וְיִהְיוּ כֻּלָּן טְמֵאִין:


מי רגלי אדם שהן כנגד מי רגלי בהמה – it teaches us a noteworthy point, and we don’t say that since these (i.e. urine) are from beasts, those also are from beasts, and even though there are two doubts (i.e., a compound uncertainty) for a leniency, the doubt regarding beasts [and] the doubt regarding a human being, and even if you can assume that [both] are from a human being, perhaps, the person is pure. And Maimonides explains through reference to the Tosefta (see Tractate Makhshirin, Chapter 3, Halakha 13 and Tractate Taharot, Chapter 5, Halakha 2), as for example, that the urine of a ritually impure man was mixed with the urine of a beast in equal measure, and this is nearby the urine of cattle, and we have a doubt if the appearance of the human urine was nullified and that all of them are ritually pure, or that they weren’t nullified and all of them are ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:7)


**עַל וַדַּאי מַגָּעָן שֶׁהוּא סְפֵק טֻמְאָתָן.** יוֹדֵעַ וַדַּאי שֶׁנָּגְעָה בָּהֶן תְּרוּמָה, אֲבָל אֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ אִם הֵן טְמֵאִים אוֹ לָאו, כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא סְפֵקָא דְּכֻלְּהוּ:


על ודאי מגען שהוא ספק טומאתן (because of certainly touching them which is a manner of doubt in respect to their [imparting] uncleanness)- he knows with certainty that priest’s due/heave offering came in contact with them, but he doesn’t know if they are ritually impure or not, as we have explained the doubt in all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:5:8)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד תּוֹלִין.** עַל סְפֵק מַגָּעָן. וְלֹא שׂוֹרְפִים מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוֵי סְפֵק סְפֵקָא לְקֻלָּא, סָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם תִּמְצֵי לוֹמַר נָגַע, סָפֵק טְמֵאִים סָפֵק טְהוֹרִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּאָמַר עַל סְפֵק מַגָּעָן שׂוֹרְפִין:


וחכמים אומרים ברשות היחיד תולין – on the doubt of their contact, and we don’t burn them because it is a compound uncertainty which is ruled as a leniency, there is a doubt whether it touched and there is a doubt whether it did not touch, and even if you can assume that it came in contact, there is doubt of whether they are ritually impure or ritually pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi who stated that on doubt of their contact we burn them [in the private domain].



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:1)


**אֶחָד טָמֵא.** לָאו טָמֵא וַדַּאי שֶׁהוּא יָדוּעַ דְּשֶׁל זָב אוֹ שֶׁל זָבָה, דְּאִם כֵּן בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִשְׂרוֹף כִּשְׁאָר כָּל סְפֵק טֻמְאָה, אֶלָּא אֶחָד טָמֵא שֶׁהוּא מִסְּתָם רֻקִּין, וְאֶחָד טָהוֹר דְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁהוּא שֶׁל אָדָם טָהוֹר. וְכֵיוָן דַּאֲפִלּוּ נָגַע וַדַּאי בְּטָמֵא זֶה שֶׁהוּא סְתָם רֻקִּין אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן, הַשְׁתָּא דְּאִיכָּא סָפֵק נָגַע בַּטָּהוֹר, תּוֹלִין בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד בֵּין אַמַּגָּע בֵּין אַמַּשָּׂא בֵּין אַהֶסֵּט בֵּין בְּלַח בֵּין בְּיָבֵשׁ. וְלֹא יָבֵשׁ כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁאֵין יָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר וּלְהַשְׁרוֹת כְּמוֹת שֶׁהָיָה, דְּהַהוּא טָהוֹר גָּמוּר אֲפִלּוּ וַדַּאי דְּזָב, אֶלָּא דְּיָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר וּלְהַשְׁרוֹת לִכְמוֹת שֶׁהָיָה:


אחד טמא – it is not definitively impure that it is known that it (i.e., the two drops of spittle) are from a person with gonorrhea or of a woman with a flux, for if so (i.e., that it is definitely known that it is impure), in the private domain, it is burnt like the other things that are doubtfully impure, but rather, one is ritually impure from mere spittle and one is pure for it is known that it is from a ritually pure person, and since that even if definitely touched the impure [spittle],that which is mere spittle is not [impure] other than according to the Rabbis, here where there is doubt of whether it touched the pure [spittle], we suspend it in the private domain, whether through contact or whether through carrying or whether through movement , whether it is moist or dry. But not so dry that one is not able to return and to permit it as it was, for this is completely pure even if it (i.e., the spittle) is definitely from someone with gonorrhea, but that it is able to be return and to be permitted in the form that it was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:2)


**וְעַל מַגָּעָן בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בִּזְמַן שֶׁהֵם לַחִים.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא יִדְבַּק מֵהֶן כָּל שֶׁהוּא בְּגוּף הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶן, וְיֵצֵא מִכְּלַל סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְדִין רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד יֵשׁ לוֹ כְּשֶׁנִּדְבַּק כָּל שֶׁהוּא בְּגוּף הָאָדָם. אֲבָל רֹק יָבֵשׁ שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִדְבָּק, אִם נָגַע בּוֹ הָוֵי סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר:


ועל מגען ברה"ר בזמן שהן לחים – it is impossible that something from them would not cleave/attach a little bit on the body of the one who touches them, and it leaves from the status of doubtfully impure in the public domain, and it has the law of the private domain when it is attached a little bit on the body of a person. But dry spittle which is not attached, if he touched it, it is doubtful impurity in the public domain, where his manner of doubt is considered ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:3)


**וְעַל מַשָּׂאָן בֵּין לַחִין בֵּין יְבֵשִׁין.** דִּכְשֶׁנִּתְלְשָׁה הַטֻּמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְנִשֵּׂאת עַל גַּבֵּי אָדָם, לָא חֲשִׁיבָא עוֹד טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, אֶלָּא הֲרֵי הִיא כְּטֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


ועל משאן בין לחין בין יבשין - when the defilement becomes detached in the public domain and is carried by a person, it is not considered any longer as defilement in the public domain, but it is defilement in the private domain as we have stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:4)


**הָיָה רֹק יְחִידִי.** מֵהָרֻקִּין הַנִּמְצָאִים, סָפֵק שֶׁל אָדָם טָמֵא סָפֵק שֶׁל אָדָם טָהוֹר:


היה רוק יחידי- from the spittle that is found, it is doubtful whether it is of a ritually impure person or of a ritually pure person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:5)


**וְנָגַע בּוֹ וּנְשָׂאוֹ וְהִסִּיטוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נָגַע בַּתְּרוּמָה:


ונגע בו ונשאו והסיטו – even in the public domain, and afterwards it touched heave offering/priest’s-due.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:6:6)


**שׂוֹרְפִים עָלָיו אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה.** כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישָׁא, עַל וַדַּאי מַגָּעָן שֶׁהוּא סְפֵק טֻמְאָתָן שׂוֹרְפִין אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


שורפין עליו את התרומה – as is taught in the first clause of the Mishnah, if it had definite contact which is doubtful defilement, we burn the priest’s due/heave-offering.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:7:1)


**סְפֵק מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים.** סָפֵק אִם נָפְלוּ לְמִקְוֶה זֶה מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים וּפְסָלוּהוּ, אוֹ לֹא נָפְלוּ. אוֹ שֶׁרָאִינוּ שֶׁנָּפְלוּ בּוֹ מַיִם שְׁאוּבִין, סָפֵק אִם הָיָה בָּהֶן שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר כְּדֵי לִפְסֹל אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה, אוֹ לֹא הָיָה בָּהֶן כַּשִּׁעוּר. וְכֻלְּהוּ שְׁאָר סְפֵקוֹת מְפֹרָשִׁים לְקַמָּן בְּפִרְקִין:


ספק מים שאובים – it is doubtful if the drawn waters fell into this Mikveh/ritual bath and invalidated it or they didn’t fall, or that we saw that drawn waters fell into it, that there is a doubt if there were three LOG which is the measure to invalidate the Mikveh, or that there weren’t in them according to this measure. And all of the rest of the doubts are explained further in [the Mishnayot] in our chapter.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:1)


**סְפֵק טֻמְאָה צָפָה עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם.** סָפֵק אִם נָגַע בְּטֻמְאָה שֶׁצָּפָה עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם אוֹ לֹא נָגַע:


ספק טמאה צפה על פני המים – there is a doubt if it (i.e., the water) touched the uncleanness floating on the surface of the water or it didn’t touch it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:2)


**בֵּין בַּכֵּלִים בֵּין בַּקַּרְקַע.** בֵּין שֶׁהַמַּיִם בְּכֵלִים בֵּין שֶׁהַמַּיִם בַּקַּרְקַע, טָהוֹר, דַּחֲשִׁיב כְּאִלּוּ אֵין מָקוֹם לַטֻּמְאָה:


בין בכלים בין בקרקע – whether the water was in vessels or whether the water was on the ground, it is ritually pure, for it is considered as if there is no place for uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:3)


**בַּכֵּלִים טָמֵא.** דַּחֲשִׁיבֵי כְּאִלּוּ יֵשׁ מָקוֹם לַטֻּמְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


בכלים טמא – for they are considered as if there is a place for uncleanness. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:4)


**סְפֵק יְרִידָתוֹ טָמֵא.** שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ אָדָם כְּשֶׁיּוֹרֵד לִבְרֵכָה שֶׁל מַיִם, שֶׁכָּל דָּבָר שֶׁבַּמַּיִם בָּא עָלָיו, מֵחֲמַת הַהֶסֵּט שֶׁמַּתְחִיל לִרְחֹץ אֶת עַצְמוֹ. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁעוֹלֶה מִן הַמַּיִם, הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁבַּמַּיִם מִתְרַחֲקִים מִמֶּנּוּ וְהוֹלְכִים לְצִדֵּי הַבְּרֵכָה:


ספק ירידתו טמא (if the doubt has to do with his descent into the water) – for it is manner of a person when he descends to a pond of water that everything that is in the water comes upon him, on account of the movement that when he begins to wash himself. But when he ascends from the water, the things that are in the water distance themselves from him and go to the sides of the pond.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:8:5)


**אֲפִלּוּ אֵין שָׁם אֶלָּא מְלֹא הָאָדָם וְהַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁהַמָּקוֹם צַר בְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי טָהוֹר, קָסָבַר דְּטֻמְאָה צָפָה אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה. כָּךְ מָצָאתִי. אֲבָל רַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא יָדַע בְּוַדַּאי שֶׁנָּגַע בַּטֻּמְאָה, טָהוֹר. נִרְאֶה שֶׁאִם הָיָה בְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִגַּע, טָמֵא:


אפילו אין שם אלא מלא האדם והטומאה – that the place is narrow in the path for it is impossible for him that he wouldn’t touch it, nevertheless he is ritually pure, for he (i.e., Rabbi Yossi) holds that the uncleanness that floats does not defile. Such is what I found. But Maimonides explains that all the while that he didn’t know with certainty that he touched uncleanness, he is ritually pure. It appears that if he was on the path where it is impossible for him that he doesn’t touch it, he is ritually impure.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:9:1)


**סְפֵק מַשְׁקִים לִטַּמֵא.** מַשְׁקִים טְהוֹרִים שֶׁנּוֹלַד בָּהֶן סָפֵק אִם קִבְּלוּ טֻמְאָה אִם לָאו, סְפֵקָן טָמֵא, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל:


ספק משקים ליטמא – ritually pure liquids that a doubt appeared on them if they were susceptible to receive defilement or not, their doubt is ritually impure, as we will explain further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:9:2)


**לְטַמֵּא טָהוֹר.** וְאִם הַמַּשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין וְיֵשׁ לָנוּ סָפֵק אִם טִמְּאוּ הַדָּבָר הַטָּהוֹר אִם לָאו, סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר:


לטמא טהור – and if the liquids are ritually impure and we have a doubt if they defiled the ritually pure thing or not, its manner of doubt is that it is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:9:3)


**וּזְרָקָהּ לְבֵין הַכִּכָּרוֹת כוּ'‏ סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ סָפֵק אִם לְאַחַר שֶׁנָּח הַמַּקֵּל נָגְעוּ הַמַּשְׁקִים בַּכִּכָּרוֹת אוֹ לֹא נָגְעוּ. דְּאִי בִּשְׁעַת זְרִיקָה, הֲוֵי לֵיהּ טֻמְאָה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ מָקוֹם, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּשֶׁרֶץ דְּאוֹרַיְתָא סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה נִזְרְקָה. וְאִם תֹּאמַר אַכַּתִּי תֵּפּוּק לֵיהּ [דְּטָהוֹר] אֲפִלּוּ בְּשֶׁרֶץ מִשּׁוּם דְּאֵין בּוֹ דַּעַת לִשָּׁאֵל. וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר דְּאִיכָּא לְאוֹקְמֵי מַתְנִיתִין כְּשֶׁאָדָם אוֹחֵז הַכִּכָּרוֹת:


וזרקה לבין הככרות כו' ספיקו טהור – as for example that we had a doubt if after the staff came to rest the liquids touched the loaves [of bread] or didn’t touch them. For if it was at the time of throwing, it had defilement that has no place, and even with a creeping animal according to the Torah, its manner of doubt is ritually pure, since the defilement is thrown. But if you say that yet I will derive (that it is ritually pure) even with a creeping animal because it does not have awareness/knowledge to be interrogated. But one can say, that it is possible to establish that our Mishnah is dealing with a case when a person is holding the loaves.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:10:1)


**סְפֵק מַשְׁקִין לְאֳכָלִין טָמֵא.** קָסָבַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי טֻמְאַת מַשְׁקִין לְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, דְּדָרֵישׁ קְרָא וְכָל מַשְׁקֶה אֲשֶׁר יִשָּׁתֶה בְּכָל כְּלִי יִטְמָא, לְטַמֵּא אֶת אֲחֵרִים, הִלְכָּךְ סְפֵק מַשְׁקִין לְטַמֵּא אֳכָלִין, טָמֵא:


ספק משקין אוכלין טמא – for Rabbi Yossi holds that defilement of liquids to defile others according to the Torah, for he expounds on the Biblical verse (Leviticus 11:34): “as to any liquid that may be drunk, it shall become impure if it was inside any vessel,” to defile others, therefore, something that is a matter of doubt in respect to liquids, if it has to do with foods, it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:10:2)


**וּלְכֵלִים טָהוֹר.** דְּטֻמְאַת מַשְׁקִין לְטַמֵּא כֵּלִים מִדְּרַבָּנָן, מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא דְּפָלֵיג עֲלֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי דָּרֵישׁ וְכָל מַשְׁקֶה אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁתֶּה בְּכָל כְּלִי יִטְמָא, אֲבָל לֹא לְטַמֵּא אֶת אֲחֵרִים, וְטֻמְאַת מַשְׁקִין לְטַמֵּא אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִין דְּרַבָּנָן, הִלְכָּךְ סְפֵק טֻמְאַת מַשְׁקִין לְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ולכלים טהור – for the defilement of liquids to defile vessels is according to the Rabbis, from the eighteen matters that [the Sages] decreed on that day (when Rabban Gamaliel was removed as head of the Sanhedrin – see Tractate Berakhot 28a – see the Bartenura commentary on Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 1, Mishnah 4). For the first Tanna/teacher that disputes on that of Rabbi Yossi who expounds (Leviticus 11:34): “as to any liquid that may be drunk, it shall become impure if it was inside any vessel” but not to defile others, for the defilement of liquids to defile foods and liquids is according to the Rabbis, therefore a matter of doubt of the defilement of liquids to defile others is ritually pure. And the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:1)


**סְפֵק יָדַיִם לִטַּמֵא.** מִי שֶׁהָיוּ יָדָיו טְהוֹרוֹת וְסָפֵק נָגַע בְּאֳכָלִים טְמֵאִין אוֹ בְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין וְנִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו, סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע וְלֹא נִטְמְאוּ יָדָיו:


ספק ידים ליטמא - a person whose hands were ritual pure and it was doubtful if they touched ritually impure foods or ritually impure liquids and his hands were defiled, and it is doubtful if he didn’t touch and his hands were not defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:2)


**וּלְטַמֵּא.** הָיוּ יָדָיו טְמֵאוֹת, סָפֵק נָגַע בְּאֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִין וְטִמְּאָן, סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע:


ולטמא – his hands were ritually impure, it is a matter of doubt if he touched foods and liquids and defiled them, and it is doubtful that he did not touch them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:3)


**וְלִטָּהֵר.** הָיוּ יָדָיו טְמֵאוֹת וְטִהֲרָן, סָפֵק נִטְהֲרוּ כָּרָאוּי אִם לָאו. בְּכָל הַסְּפֵקוֹת הַלָּלוּ, סְפֵקָן טָהוֹר:


וליטהר- his hands were ritually impure and he purified them, it is doubtful if they were purified properly or not, in all of these matters of doubt, their doubt is deemed ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:4)


**סְפֵק רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, טָהוֹר, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ צִבּוּר עוֹשִׂים אֶת הַפֶּסַח בְּטֻמְאָה, אִם טֻמְאָה וַדָּאִית הֻתְּרָה לַצִּבּוּר, קַל וָחֹמֶר סְפֵק טֻמְאָה:


ספק רשות הרבים – it is matter of doubt of defilement in the public domain, they are considered ritually pure, for such we found concerning a community that makes the Passover sacrifice in ritual defilement, if it is definitive defilement, it was permissible to the community, as an a fortiori inference it a manner of doubt of defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:5)


**סְפֵק דִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים.** כְּגוֹן כָּל הָנָךְ דַּחֲשִׁיב, דְּכֻלְּהוּ מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר דָּבָר שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם, וּמְפֹרָשִׁין הֵן בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת פֶּרֶק קַמָּא:


ספק דברי סופרים - as for example, all of these that are considered, and all of them are from the eight things that they (i.e., the Sages) decreed on that day (i.e., the day when Rabban Gamaliel was deposed as head of the Sanhedrin -see Tractate Berakhot 28a, and they are explained in Tractate Shabbat in the first chapter [Mishnah 4 – see the Bartenura commentary).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:6)


**אֲבָל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא אַב הַטֻּמְאָה וְהוּא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים.** כְּגוֹן דַּם תְּבוּסָה, וְנָכְרִים שֶׁעֲשָׂאוּם כְּזָבִים, וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּאֵלּוּ, אִם נִסְתַּפֵּק אִם נָגַע בָּהֶן אִם לֹא נָגַע:


אבל דבר שהוא אב הטומאה והוא מדברי סופרים – as for example, דם תבוסה/blood that flows from a person at the time of his death or afterwards (the legal status of this blood is that of a corpse itself and is therefore an ultimate primary source of ritual impurity) and heathens that made themselves like those with gonorrhea, and similar things to this, if there is a doubt if he touched them or did not touch them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:11:7)


**סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא.** וּמִיהוּ אַב הַטֻּמְאָה מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים שֶׁהוּא עַצְמוֹ מִסָּפֵק, כְּגוֹן בֵּית הַפְּרָס דְּאִיכָּא סְפֵקָא אִם יֵשׁ בּוֹ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה אִם לָאו, בְּהָא תְּנַן לְעֵיל דְּעַל וַדַּאי מַגָּעָן שׂוֹרְפִין, וְעַל סְפֵק מַגָּעָן אֵין שׂוֹרְפִין, דַּהֲוֵי סְפֵק סְפֵקָא:


ספיקו טמא – but however a primary source of ritual impurity that is from the words of the Scribes (i.e., Rabbinic) that it itself is from doubt, as for example the בית הפרס/area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or corpse (i.e., a field containing a grave that was unintentionally plowed and the bones may have been strewn throughout the field, and a grave containing a grave the location of which is unknown), if there is in it a bone the bulk of a barleycorn or not. In this, it is taught in the Mishnah above (see Mishnah 5) that when their contact is definite, we burn them, but on their doubtful contact we don’t burn them, for this is a ספק ספיקא/compound uncertainty [which is ruled with leniency].



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:1)


**זוֹ טָהֳרַת פְּרִישׁוּת.** דִּתְנַן, בִּגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ מִדְרָס לַפְּרוּשִׁים, וּבִגְדֵי פְּרוּשִׁים מִדְרָס לְאוֹכְלֵי תְּרוּמָה. וְאִם נִסְתַּפֵּק אֶחָד מִן הַפְּרוּשִׁים שֶׁאוֹכְלִין [חֻלֵּיהֶן] בְּטָהֳרָה, אִם נָגַע בְּבִגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ אִם לֹא נָגַע, וְכֵן אוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנִּסְתַּפֵּק אִם נָגַע בְּבִגְדֵי הַפְּרוּשִׁים וְנִטְמָא בָּהֶן לַתְּרוּמָה, סְפֵקָן טָהוֹר:


זו טהרת פרישות – as it is taught in the Mishnah [Tractate Hagigah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 7]: “The clothing of those who are not careful in their observance of the laws of ritual purity/עם הארץ is in the status of מדרס/Levitical uncleanness arising from a person with gonorrhea immediate contact caused by treading, and the clothing of Pharisees are considered Midras with regard to those who consume heave offering.” But if there is doubt regarding one of the Pharisees who consume [their unconsecrated food] in ritual purity, if he came in contact with the clothes of a person who is not careful in his observance of the laws of ritual purity or didn’t come in contact with it, and similarly, a person who eats heave-offering (i.e., a Kohen) and there is doubt if he had contact with the clothing of the Pharisees and the heave-offering was defiled, their manner of doubt is considered ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:2)


**סְפֵק שְׁרָצִים.** הִשְׁלִיךְ שֶׁרֶץ בֵּין הַכִּכָּרוֹת, סָפֵק נָגַע בַּכִּכָּרוֹת בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁעָבַר עֲלֵיהֶן, סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, אִם מָצָא אוֹתוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ, הֲרֵי הַכִּכָּרוֹת כִּשְׁעַת מְצִיאָתָן וּסְפֵקָן טָהוֹר:


ספק שרצים – he cast a creeping animal between the loaves [of bread], it is doubtful if it touched the loaves at the time that it passed over them, it is doubtful that it did not touch [them]. If he found it that it didn’t touch [the loaves], behold the loaves are like the time that he found them, and their matter of doubt is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:3)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִזְקַק לַטֻּמְאָה.** בְּפֶרֶק ה'‏ דִּנְגָעִים מְפָרֵשׁ כֵּיצַד:


עד שלא נזקק לטומאה (before it had been subjected to a ruling of uncleanness) – In Chapter Five of [Tractate] Negaim (Mishnayot 4-5) it explains how [this is the case].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:4)


**סְפֵק נְזִירוּת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר הֲרֵינִי נָזִיר אִם יֵשׁ בַּכְּרִי הַזֶּה מֵאָה כּוֹר, הָלַךְ וּמְצָאוֹ שֶׁנִּגְנַב אוֹ שֶׁאָבַד, סָפֵק הָיָה בּוֹ, סָפֵק לֹא הָיָה בּוֹ, מֻתָּר לִשְׁתּוֹת יַיִן וּלְהִטַּמֵּא לְמֵתִים:


ספק נזירות – as, for example, that he said: Behold I am a Nazirite if there is in this heap/pile one hundred KOR, if he went and found that it had been stolen or that he lost it, it is doubt that it had it [and it is doubtful that it didn’t have it, it is permitted [for him] to drink wine and to become defiled with corpses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:5)


**סְפֵק בְּכוֹרוֹת.** סָפֵק אִם הוּא פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם אִם לָאו:


ספק בכורות – it is doubtful if it (i.e., the child or the animal) is the firstborn [of its mother] or not.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:6)


**אֶחָד בְּכוֹרֵי אָדָם.** פָּטוּר מִלָּתֵת חָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים לַכֹּהֵן:


אחד בכורי אדם – except from giving five Selaim (i.e., coins) to a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:7)


**אֶחָד בְּכוֹרֵי בְהֵמָה טְמֵאָה.** פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר:


ואחד בכורי בהמה טמאה – the first born of a donkey


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:12:8)


**שֶׁהַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה.** דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ לַכֹּהֵן, אַיְתֵּי רְאָיָה דְּהוּא בְּכוֹר וּשְׁקוֹל:


שהמוציא מחבירו עליו הראיה – that he said to the Kohen: “Bring proof that he is a first born and pay the head-tax.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:13:1)


**סְפֵק קָרְבָּנוֹת.** כֵּיצַד, הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלֶיהָ סְפֵק חָמֵשׁ לֵידוֹת וְכוּ': **מְבִיאָה קָרְבָּן אֶחָד.** חַטַּאת הָעוֹף הַבָּא עַל הַסָּפֵק:


ספק קרבנות – how so? The woman who has a doubt of five miscarriages that were in doubt. מביאה קרבן אחד – a sin-offering of a fowl that comes for a doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:13:2)


**וְאוֹכֶלֶת בַּזְּבָחִים.** שֶׁהַקָּרְבָּן הַזֶּה לִגְמֹר טָהֳרָתָהּ בָּא וַהֲרֵי הוּא כִּטְבִילָה, שֶׁאִם נִטְמֵאת הָאִשָּׁה כַּמָּה טֻמְאוֹת, טְבִילָה אַחַת עוֹלָה לְכֻלָּן, אַף קָרְבָּן זֶה כֵּן:


ואוכלת בזבחים – that this sacrifice comes to compete her ritual purity and it is like ritual immersion, for if the woman was defiled several defilements, one ritual immersion counts for all of them, even this sacrifice as well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 4:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/4:13:3)


**וְאֵין הַשְּׁאָר עָלֶיהָ חוֹבָה.** לֹא הִצְרִיכוּהָ חֲכָמִים לַהֲבִיאָן. שֶׁאַף הָאֶחָד בְּקֹשִׁי הִתִּירוּ לְהַקְרִיב סְפֵק מְלִיקַת חֻלִּין לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, אֶלָּא כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהּ תַּקָּנָה לִטָּהֵר לֶאֱכֹל בַּקָּדָשִׁים:


ואין השאר עליה חובה – the Sages did not require her to bring them (the other sacrifices), for even the one, with difficulty, they permitted to bring a doubtfully pinched unconsecrated offering to the Altar, but in order to make for her an ordinance to be ritually pure to eat Holy Things.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:1)


**הַשֶּׁרֶץ.** שֶׁרֶץ מְטַמֵּא בְּמַגָּע וְלֹא בְּמַשָּׂא:


השרץ (creeping thing) – the creeping animal defiles through contact but not by lifting (see Tractate Kelim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 as well as Leviticus 11:29-30).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:2)


**צְפַרְדֵּעַ.** טָהוֹר. וּלְפִי שֶׁצְּפַרְדֵּעַ דּוֹמֶה לְצָב וְטָעוּ בֵּיהּ, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי נָקַט צְפַרְדֵּעַ:


צפרדע – a ritually pure [frog], but since it is similar to a species of lizard and they (i.e., the Rabbis) erred concerning it, because of this, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took [the word] צפרדע/frog.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:3)


**כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת.** מְטַמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל. וּנְבֵלָה אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה בְּאֹהֶל:


כזית מן המת -defiles in a tent. But a carrion does not defile in a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:4)


**עֶצֶם מִן הַמֵּת.** מְטַמֵּא בְּהֶסֵּט, וְעֶצֶם דִּנְבֵלָה טָהוֹר:


עצם מן המת – [a bone from a corpse] defiles through movement, but the bone of a carrion is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:5)


**גּוּשׁ מִבֵּית הַפְּרָס וְגוּשׁ מֵאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים.** מְטַמְּאִים בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין בְּאֹהֶל:


גוש מבית הפרס וגוש מארץ העמים – they defile through contact and by lifting/carrying but do not defile in a tent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:6)


**שְׁנֵי שְׁבִילִין אֶחָד טָמֵא.** שֶׁאֶחָד מֵהֶן קֶבֶר מֻטָּל בּוֹ לְרָחְבּוֹ, וּמְמַלֵּא אֶת כֻּלּוֹ, וְאֵין עוֹבֵר בּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יַאֲהִיל:


שני שבילין אחד טמא – that one of them is a grave lying width-wise and fills all of it, and he doesn’t pass through it so that he will not be overshadowed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:7)


**הֶאֱהִיל עַל אַחַד מֵהֶן.** עַל כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת וְכַזַּיִת מִן הַנְּבֵלָה קָאֵי:


האהיל על אחד מהן – we are referring to on an olive’s bulk of a corpse or on an olive’s bulk of a carrion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:8)


**הִסִּיט אֶחָד מֵהֶן.** [עַל עֶצֶם מִן הַמֵּת וְעֶצֶם מִן הַנְּבֵלָה, וְכֵן] אַגּוּשׁ מֵאֶרֶץ טְהוֹרָה וְגוּשׁ מִבֵּית הַפְּרָס וְגוּשׁ מֵאֶרֶץ הָעַמִּים קָאֵי, דִּמְטַמְּאִין בְּהֶסֵּט:


הסיט אחד מהם – [on a bone from the corpse or a bone from the carrion, and similarly] a clod of earth from a ritually pure land and a clod of earth from an area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse/בית הפרס and a clod of earth from the land of the nations (i.e., outside the Land of Israel), that defile through movement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:9)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְטַמֵּא.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דִּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר הָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁעָשָׂה טָהֳרוֹת, כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין לָהֶם תַּקָּנָה כְּשֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ. אֲבָל לְגַבֵּי גַּבְרָא לְהַצְרִיכוֹ טְבִילָה וְהַזָּאָה, סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּצָרִיךְ טְבִילָה וְהַזָּאָה, שֶׁמָּא הַיּוֹם וּמָחָר יִתְבָּרֵר הַדָּבָר שֶׁנִּטְמָא:


ר' עקיבא מטמא – for Rabbi Akiba holds that defilement in the public domain – its manner of doubt is pure – these words apply when he made/prepared pure things, since they lack any remedy when they are defiled. But concerning a person who is caused to need ritual immersion [in a Mikveh] and sprinkling [of ashes], he holds that he requires ritual immersion and sprinkling, lest today or tomorrow the matter becomes verified that he was defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:1:10)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ גַּבְרָא דְּאִית לֵיהּ תַּקַּנְתָּא לֹא מַצְרְכִינַן לֵיהּ טְבִילָה וְהַזָּאָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטהרים – for even a person who has a remedy, we don’t we require him to undergo ritual immersion and sprinkling. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:2:1)


**אֶחָד שֶׁאָמַר נָגַעְתִּי בָזֶה.** אַהַשֶּׁרֶץ וְהַצְּפַרְדֵּעַ דְּרֵישָׁא קָאֵי, דִּצְפַרְדֵּעַ דּוֹמָה לְצָב וְטָעוּ בֵּיהּ:


אחד שאמר נגעתי בזה – it refers to the frog and the creeping animal of the first clause [found in Mishnah 1 of this chapter], for the frog is similar to a species of lizard and they (i.e., the Rabbis) erred concerning it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:2:2)


**שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לַהֲלֹךְ.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְאָדָם לִמְנֹעַ עַצְמוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יֵלֵךְ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, הִלְכָּךְ סְפֵק שְׁבִילִין טָהוֹר. אֲבָל מַגָּע אֶפְשָׁר לְאָדָם שֶׁלֹּא לִגַּע, הִלְכָּךְ כִּי מְסַפְּקָא לֵיהּ אִי נָגַע אִי לֹא נָגַע, סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה לֹא כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וְלֹא כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


שדרך בני אדם להלוך – that it is impossible for a person to prevent himself that he should not walk in the public domain, therefore a manner of doubt regarding pathways is ritually pure. But [regarding] touching/contact it is possible for a person not to touch it, therefore, if he has doubt whether he touched it or not, his manner of doubt is ritual impurity. But the Halakha is neither according to Rabbi Akiba or Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:3:1)


**הִזָּה וְשָׁנָה.** הִזָּה בַּשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְשָׁנָה בַּשְּׁבִיעִי:


הזה ושנה – he sprinkled on the third [day] and repeated it on the seventh [day].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:3:2)


**אֵלּוּ וָאֵלּוּ תְּלוּיוֹת.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּבִשְׁנַיִם שֶׁהָלְכוּ מוֹקְמִינַן כָּל חַד בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה, כְּדִתְנַן לְקַמָּן, הָכָא גַּבֵּי חַד בִּתְרֵי זִמְנֵי לֹא שַׁיָּךְ לְטַהֵר בַּקַּיָּמוֹת, דַּאֲפִלּוּ בִּשְׁנַיִם כְּשֶׁבָּאוּ לִשָּׁאֵל בְּבַת אַחַת, טְמֵאִין. וְחַד אֲפִלּוּ בָּזֶה אַחַר זֶה כְּבַת אַחַת דָּמֵי:


אלו ואלו תלויות – even though that in the two [paths] that they walked, we establish that each one is in the presumption of ritual purity, as is taught in the Mishnah further on (Mishnah 5), here concerning one [person] in two times, it doesn’t belong to ritually purify with those which remain, for even with two when they came to interrogate him at the same time, they are ritually impure. And the other, even with one after the other is considered like at the same time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:3:3)


**וְהַשְּׁנִיּוֹת יִשָּׂרֵפוּ.** דְּוַדַּאי טָמֵא הָיָה כְּשֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ:


השניות ישרפו – for certainly it was ritually impure when they were made.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:5:1)


**אִם נִשְׁאֲלוּ.** כָּל אֶחָד לַחֲכָמִים עַל סְפֵקוֹ בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, אֵין בֵּית דִּין יְכוֹלִין לְטַמְּאָן, דְּהָא קַיְמָא לָן סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר. וְאִם בָּאוּ בְּבַת אַחַת, הוֹאִיל וּבְהוֹרָאָה אַחַת יֵשׁ לָנוּ לוֹמַר טְמֵאִין אַתֶּם אוֹ טְהוֹרִים אַתֶּם, אִי אֶפְשָׁר לָנוּ לוֹמַר טְהוֹרִים אַתֶּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֶחָד וַדַּאי טָמֵא:


אם נשאלו – each of by the Sages on his manner of doubt by himself, no Jewish court is able to defile them, for we hold that regarding a manner of doubt in the public domain, his manner of doubt is ritually pure, but if they came at one time, since that with one decision we are able to say, “you [both] are ritually impure or “you are [both] ritually impure, it is impossible for us to say “you [both] are ritually pure” since one is definitely impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:5:2)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ טְמֵאִין.** לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ רַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה כְּשֶׁבָּאוּ לִשָּׁאֵל בְּבַת אַחַת, דְּדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל טְמֵאִין. וּכְשֶׁבָּאוּ לִשָּׁאֵל זֶה אַחַר זֶה דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל טְהוֹרִים. עַל מָה נֶחְלְקוּ, עַל הַבָּא לִשָּׁאֵל עָלָיו וְעַל חֲבֵרוֹ, דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְדַמֵּי לֵיהּ לְזֶה אַחַר זֶה, וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְדַמֵּי לֵיהּ לְבַת אַחַת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר בין כך ובין כך טמאין – Rabbi Yossi and Rabbi Yehuda did not disagree when they came to be interrogated at the same time, for everyone states that they are ritually impure. But when they came to be interrogated one after another, everyone states that they are ritually pure. On what did they disagree? One when one comes to be interrogated him and on his fellow, for Rabbi Yehuda compares this to one coming after the other, whereas Rabbi Yossi comes it to simultaneously. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:6:1)


**אָכַל אַחַד מֵהֶן.** תָּנָא טֻמְאָה דְּאוֹרַיְתָא וְקָתָנֵי טֻמְאָה דְּרַבָּנָן. וּפְלוּגְתַּיְהוּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי הָכָא נַמִּי בְּבָא לִשָּׁאֵל עָלָיו וְעַל חֲבֵרוֹ, כִּפְלוּגְתַּיְהוּ לְעֵיל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אכל אחד מהן – it (i.e., the Mishnah) taught defilement according to the Torah and it [also] teaches defilement according to the Rabbi. And the dispute of Rabbi Yehuda and Rabbi Yossi here also is when one comes to be interrogated upon and on his neighbor, like the dispute above (in Mishnah 5). And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:1)


**עַל רֻקּוֹ שׂוֹרְפִים.** שֶׁעַל רֻקִּים הַנִּמְצָאִים שׂוֹרְפִים אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, כְּדִתְנַן בְּפִרְקִין דִּלְעֵיל, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן סָפֵק, וְלֹא אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר רֻבָּא:


על רוקו שורפים – that on the spittle that is found, we burn the heave-offering/priest’s due as it is taught in the Mishnah in our chapter above (Tractate Taharot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 5), and even though they are a matter of doubt, we don’t follow after the majority.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:2)


**וְעַל בְּגָדָיו הוֹלְכִים אַחַר הָרֹב.** אִם רֹב אַנְשֵׁי אוֹתָהּ הָעִיר זָבִים וְזָבוֹת, שׂוֹרְפִין:


ועל בגדיו הולכים אחר הרוב - if the majority of the people of that city are people with gonorrhea/זבים and זבות/women with a flux, we burn [the priest’s due/heave-offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:3)


**כֵּלָיו טְמֵאִין מִדְרָס.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּבְיָשַׁב וּבָא אֶחָד וְדָרַס הוֹלְכִין אַחַר הָרֹב, יָשֵׁן שָׁאנֵי, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא רֹב בְּנֵי אָדָם דָּרְסוּ עַל בְּגָדָיו וְאֶחָד מֵהֶן זָב אוֹ זָבָה:


כליו טמאין מדרס – although that when he sat and another came and tread, we go after the majority. Sleeping is different, for we are concerned that perhaps the majority of people tread on his clothing and one of them was a man with gonorrhea or a woman with a flux.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין.** כֵּיוָן דַּהֲוֵי סָפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, טָהוֹר, שֶׁמָּא לֹא דָּרַס כְּלָל:


וחכמים מטהרין – since there was a doubtful defilement in the public domain, he is ritually pure, perhaps no one tread at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:5)


**נָגַע בְּאֶחָד בַּלַּיְלָה.** בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דַּוְקָא מַיְרֵי:


נגע באחד בלילה – we are speaking specifically in the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:7:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** אִם רָאָהוּ חַי מִבָּעֶרֶב אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבָּא שַׁחֲרִית וּמְצָאוֹ שֶׁמֵּת, מוֹדִים חֲכָמִים לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר, וּכְשֶׁלֹּא רָאָהוּ חַי פְּלִיגֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטמאין – if he saw him from the evening even though he came in the morning and found that he had died, the Sages agree with Rabbi Meir that he is ritually pure, but if he saw him living, there is a dispute. But the Halakha is according to the Sagesץ



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:1)


**שׁוֹטָה אַחַת בָּעִיר.** וְדַוְקָא אִשָּׁה שׁוֹטָה, אֲבָל אָדָם שׁוֹטֶה לֹא, דְּאִשָּׁה רְגִילָה בְּנִדּוּת, וְאֵין אָדָם רָגִיל בְּזִיבוֹת:


שוטה אחת בעיר – and specifically a woman imbecile, but not a male imbecile. For a woman is regularly in a menstruating status, whereas a male is not normally having a flux.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:2)


**אוֹ נָכְרִית.** וְהוּא הַדִּין לְנָכְרִי, דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא אֵין חִלּוּק בֵּין נָכְרִי לְנָכְרִית, וְכֻלָּן טְהוֹרִין, וְרַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ עֲלַיְהוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כְּזָבִים. וְכֵן כּוּתִית אֵין חִלּוּק בֵּין כּוּתִי לְכוּתִית לְמַאן דְּאָמַר כּוּתִים גֵּרֵי אֲרָיוֹת הֵן, וְגַם הָאִידְנָא גָּזְרוּ עֲלַיְהוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כְּנָכְרִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם:


או נכרית – and the same law applies regarding a heathen/gentile, for according to the Torah, there is no distinction between a male and a female, and all of them are ritually pure, but the Rabbis decreed upon them that they would be like those with a flux. And similarly, regarding a female Cuthean (i.e., a member of the sect of Samaritans), there is no distinction between a male Cuthean to a female Cuthean according to the one who says that Cutheans are lion-proselytes (i.e., proselytes from mere fear – referring to II Kings, Chapter 17, verse 25), and even today, they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed concerning them that they would be like heathens regarding all their matters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:3)


**כָּל הָרֻקִּין שֶׁבָּעִיר טְמֵאִים.** שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לַהֲלֹךְ בְּכָל הָעִיר:


כל הרוקין שבעיר טמאים – for it is their manner to walk throughout the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:4)


**אוֹ שֶׁיָּשְׁבָה עִמּוֹ בִּסְפִינָה.** דִּמְטַמְּאָה מִדְרָס אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בְּגָדֶיהָ נוֹגְעִים, כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ג'‏ דְּזָבִים:


או שישבה עמו בספינה – that she defiles through treading/leaning (i.e., Midras – Levitical uncleanness arising from a person with gonorrhea’s immediate contact) even though her clothing does not touch, as is taught in the beginning of the third chapter of [Tractate] Zavim (Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:8:5)


**אִם מַכִּירָתוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹכֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה כֵּלָיו טְהוֹרִים.** דְּאִם הָיְתָה נִדָּה אוֹ זָבָה לֹא הָיְתָה דּוֹרֶסֶת עַל בְּגָדָיו וְלֹא נִכְנֶסֶת עִמּוֹ בִּסְפִינָה:


אם מכירתו שהוא אוכל בתרומה כליו טהורים – that if she was a menstruating woman or a woman with a flux she would not tread on his clothing nor enter with him on the ship.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:9:1)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַמֵּא.** שֶׁאִם הֱבִיאוּהוּ שְׁנַיִם לִידֵי מִיתָה חֲמוּרָה, לֹא יְבִיאוּהוּ לִידֵי הַקַּל:


ר' מאיר מטמא – that if two [witnesses] brought him to a grave death [as punishment], they would not bring him to [bring] the lenient sacrifice (see also the reasoning of Rabbi Meir in Tractate Kritot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:9:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אָדָם נֶאֱמָן עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ.** דִּמְתָרְצִינַן דִּבּוּרֵיהּ, מַאי לֹא נִטְמֵאתִי דְּקָאָמַר, לֹא עָמַדְתִּי בְּטֻמְאָתִי אֶלָּא טָבַלְתִּי, וְהוֹאִיל וְכֵן, אָדָם נֶאֱמָן עַל יְדֵי עַצְמוֹ:


וחכמים אומרים אדם נאמן על פי עצמו – that we provide the answer through his word. What is “I was not defiled” that he stated? I didn’t stand in my defilement but rather I ritually immerse, and since this is the case, a person is believed concerning himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/5:9:3)


**בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד טָמֵא בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים טָהוֹר.** דַּחֲשִׁיב כִּשְׁאָר סְפֵק טֻמְאָה דְּעָלְמָא:


ברה"י טמא ברה"ר טהור – that he is considered like the rest of merely doubtful matters of defilement.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:1)


**מָקוֹם שֶׁהָיָה רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְנַעֲשָׂה רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** כְּגוֹן בִּקְעָה, דְּבִימוֹת הַגְּשָׁמִים רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וּבִימוֹת הַחַמָּה רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


מקום שהיה רשות היחיד ונעשה רשות הרבים – as, for example a valley, plane, that in the rainy season it is the private domain, and in the days of summer heat it is the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:2)


**הַמְסֻכָּן.** מִי שֶׁנִּתְעַלֵּף וְאֵין יָדוּעַ מִמֶּנּוּ אִם חַי אִם מֵת:


המסוכן – a person who fainted/swooned and it is not known from him if he is living or dead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:3)


**וְהֶחֱזִירוּהוּ לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד.** וְשָׁם רָאוּהוּ שֶׁמֵּת:


והחזירוהו לרה"י – and there they saw him that he died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:4)


**כְּשֶׁהוּא בִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא.** וּמְטַמְּאִינַן מִסָּפֵק כָּל אָדָם וְכֵלִים שֶׁהָיוּ עִמּוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה. וְאָדָם וְכֵלִים שֶׁהָיוּ עִמּוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְאַחַר שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּהוּ מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, טְהוֹרִין, דִּסְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הוּא, וּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר:


כשהוא ברה"י ספיקו טמא – and we declare him impure, from doubt every person and vessels that were with him in the private domain when he was there at first. But people and vessels that were with him in the public domain after they removed him from the private domain are ritually pure. For it is manner of doubtful defilement in the public domain and his manner of doubt is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:1:5)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מַפְסָקֶת.** כֵּיוָן דְּחָשְׁבִינַן לֵיהּ כְּחַי לְאַחַר שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּהוּ מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּמְטַהֲרִינַן לְכֵלִים וְאָדָם שֶׁהָיוּ עִמּוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דְּשֶׁמָּא חַי הוּא, אֵיךְ נַחְשְׁבֵהוּ מֵת לְמַפְרֵעַ קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּצָא מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, עַל כָּרְחָךְ אֵין יְכוֹלִין לְטַמֵּא מִסָּפֵק אָדָם וְכֵלִים שֶׁהָיוּ עִמּוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה, הוֹאִיל וּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּהוּ שָׁם בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה מַפְסֶקֶת שֶׁאַתָּה מַחֲשִׁיבוֹ כְּחַי וּמְטַהַרְתְּ אָדָם וְכֵלִים שֶׁהָיוּ עִמּוֹ שָׁם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון אומר רשות הרבים מפסקת (the public domain intervenes) – since we consider him as living after they removed him from the private domain to the public domain, and we declare ritually pure vessels and people that were with him in the public domain for perhaps he is alive, how can we consider him dead retroactively before he left from the private domain, by force we are not able to defile from doubt a person or vessels that were with him in the private domain originally, since the public domain that they eventually took him out there intervenes and you must consider him as living and purify a person and vessels that were with him. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:1)


**הַטָּמֵא עוֹמֵד.** מְצֹרָע עוֹמֵד תַּחַת הָאֹהֶל אוֹ תַּחַת הָאִילָן:


הטמא עומד – a leper stands underneath the tent or underneath the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:2)


**וְטָהוֹר עוֹבֵר.** דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם, סָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, אוֹ סָפֵק הֶאֱהִיל הָאֹהֶל אוֹ הָאִילָן עַל שְׁנֵיהֶם וְנִטְמָא הַטָּהוֹר, סָפֵק לֹא הֶאֱהִיל וְלֹא נִטְמָא. אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה הַטָּהוֹר עוֹמֵד וְהַמְּצֹרָע עוֹבֵר. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, כְּגוֹן הַשֶּׁרֶץ בְּפִי הַחֻלְדָּה וְהַנְּבֵלָה בְּפִי הַכֶּלֶב, עָבְרוּ בֵּין הַטְּהוֹרִים, אוֹ הַטְּהוֹרִים עָבְרוּ בֵּינֵיהֶן, חֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים אִם סָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין לַטֻּמְאָה מָקוֹם, כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק הַזּוֹרֵק טֻמְאָה. וּלְפִי פֵּרוּשׁ זֶה, סָפֵק הֶאֱהִיל סָפֵק לֹא הֶאֱהִיל לֵיכָּא לְאוֹקְמָא אַהַךְ רֵישָׁא דְּטָמֵא עוֹמֵד וְטָהוֹר עוֹבֵר, דִּבְטֻמְאַת אֹהֶל אֵין חִלּוּק בֵּין יֵשׁ מָקוֹם לַטֻּמְאָה בֵּין אֵין מָקוֹם לַטֻּמְאָה, כִּדְהוֹכַחְנוּ לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק הַזּוֹרֵק טֻמְאָה:


וטהור עובר – on the path there, it is doubtful if he touched [the impure leper] or didn’t touch, or it is doubtful if the tent overshadowed him or the tree [overshadowed] over both of them and the ritually pure person was defiled, [and] it is doubtful if it didn’t overshadow and he wasn’t defiled. Or the ritually pure person was standing and the leper was passing. Another explanation, as for example, the creeping animal is in the mouth of the weasel or the carrion in the mouth of the dog passed between the ritually pure individuals or the ritually pure individuals passed between them, the Sages declare ritually pure if there is doubt if he touched or a doubt that he didn’t touch, because the defilement doesn’t have a place, as is taught above in the Chapter "הזורק טומאה" /He who throws defilement [Tractate Taharot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 3] (see also Tractate Negaim, Chapter 13, Mishnah 7). But according to this explanation, if he doubtfully overshadowed or doubtfully did not overshadow, it is impossible to establish to this first clause [of the Mishnah] that the impure person is standing and the ritually pure person is passing by, for regarding the defilement of a tent, there is no distinction between whether there is a place for defilement or whether there isn’t place for defilement, as we proved above in the chapter "הזורק טומאה"/He who throws defilement (Tractate Negaim, Chapter 13, Mishnah 7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:3)


**טֻמְאָה בִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְטָהֳרָה בִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** כְּגוֹן חֲנוּת הַפְּתוּחָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, סָפֵק נִכְנַס סָפֵק לֹא נִכְנַס:


טומאה ברה"י וטהרה ברה"ר – as for example, a store that is open to the public domain, it is doubtful whether he entered or whether he didn’t enter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:2:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מְטַמֵּא.** דְּסָבַר סָפֵק בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְרַבִּים הַמְשֻׁתָּפִים בּוֹ, בָּתַר רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד אָזְלִינַן וּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי, בָּתַר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אָזְלִינַן וּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ר' יהושע מטמא – for he holds that doubtfully in the private domain and many are engaged with him, we follow after the private domain and his manner of doubt is ritually impure. But the Rabbis hold that we follow after the public domain and his manner of doubt is ritually pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:3:1)


**עָלָה לְרֹאשׁוֹ.** רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל אִילָן חָשׁוּב רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד:


עלה לראשו – [he ascended] to the top of the tree, which is considered the private domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:3:2)


**הִכְנִיס יָדוֹ לְחוֹר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ טֻמְאָה.** הַחוֹר חָשׁוּב רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָאָדָם עוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִכְנִיס יָדוֹ לַחוֹר, כְּעוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד דָּמֵי. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהָאִילָן וְהַחוֹר לֹא הֲווּ רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד אֵצֶל שַׁבָּת, הֲווּ רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד אֵצֶל טֻמְאָה:


הכניס ידו לחור שיש בו טומאה – the hole is considered the private domain, and even though the person is standing in the public domain, since he put his hand into the hole, it is like he is standing in the private domain. But although the tree and the hole were not the private domain regarding Sabbath [law], they are the private domain regarding ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:3:3)


**חֲנוּת שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא וּפָתוּחַ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** הַוְיָא כָּל הַחֲנוּת כֻּלָּהּ כְּטֻמְאָה הַמֻּנַּחַת בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁאִם הָיָה שֶׁרֶץ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים סָפֵק נָגַע בּוֹ סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר, הָכָא נַמִּי סָפֵק נִכְנַס לַחֲנוּת זֶה סָפֵק לֹא נִכְנַס, סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר. אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ שְׁתֵּי חֲנֻיּוֹת אַחַת טְהוֹרָה וְאַחַת טְמֵאָה, סָפֵק לַטָּמֵא נִכְנַס סָפֵק לַטָּהוֹר נִכְנַס, סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא, דְּהַוְיָא לֵיהּ סְפֵק טֻמְאָה בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד:


חנות שהוא טמא ופתוח לרה"ר – it would the entirety of all of the store is like the defilement that is placed in the public domain, and just as if there was a creeping animal in the public domain, it is doubtful if he touched it, doubtful if he didn’t touch it, his manner of doubt of ritually pure, here also, if he doubtfully entered into this store or doubtfully didn’t enter into it, his manner of doubt is ritually pure. But if there were two stores, one ritually pure and one ritually impure, it is doubtful whether he entered into the impure store or whether he entered into the ritually pure one, his manner of doubt is ritually impure, for it would be him a doubtful defilement in the private domain.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:4:1)


**סְפֵק בִּיאָה טָהוֹר.** מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעִינַן דּוּמְיָא דְּסוֹטָה, דְּוַדַּאי נִסְתְּרָה אֶלָּא דְּסָפֵק נָגַע סָפֵק לֹא נָגַע, דְּלֹא יָדְעִינַן אִם נִטְמֵאת, וְקַרְיֵהּ רַחֲמָנָא טְמֵאָה, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ד) וְנִסְתְּרָה וְהִיא נִטְמָאָה. אֲבָל סְפֵק בִּיאָה טָהוֹר:
