ספק ביאה טהור – because we require something similar to the woman accused of being unfaithful to her husband (i.e., Sotah), for certainly she keeps it secret, but rather if he doubtfully touched it, or doubtful did not touch [the defiling agent], for we don’t know if she was defiled, and the All-Merciful calls her defiled, as it is written (Numbers 5:13): “and she keeps secret the fact that she has defiled herself,” but a case of doubt concerning entry is considered ritually pure.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:5:1)


**בִּקְעָה.** שָׂדוֹת שֶׁל אֲנָשִׁים הַרְבֵּה, וּבִימוֹת הַגְּשָׁמִים שֶׁהֵן חֲרוּשׁוֹת וּזְרוּעוֹת יֵשׁ לָהֶן דִּין רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, לְפִי שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם נִזְהָרִים מִלְּהַלֵּךְ עַל גַּבֵּי הַזְּרָעִים שֶׁלֹּא יַפְסִידוּ:


בקעה – fields belonging to many people, and in the rainy reason when they are plowed and sown, it has the law of being the private domain, because people are careful from walking on the seeds so that they don’t become destroyed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:5:2)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר מְטַהֵר.** דַּהֲוֵי לֵיהּ סְפֵק בִּיאָה, וְאֵין כָּל הַבִּקְעָה חֲשׁוּבָה כְּשָׂדֶה אַחַת, לְפִי שֶׁהַשָּׂדוֹת חֲלוּקִים בַּמֵּצָרִים שֶׁלָּהֶם:


ר' אלעזר מטהר – for this is for him doubtful entry, and not all valleys are considered like one field, because the fields are divided with their narrow boundaries.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:6:1)


**עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר לֹא נָגַעְתִּי.** לָאו דַּוְקָא שֶׁיֹּאמַר בָּרִי לִי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַעְתִּי, דְּהוּא הַדִּין הֵיכָא דְּאִיכָּא מִידִי לְמִתְלֵי בֵּיהּ שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע:


עד שיאמר לא נגעתי – not exactly that he would say it is evident to me that I did not touch it, and the same law applies where there is a matter to compare to him that he didn’t touch it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:6:2)


**שְׁבִילֵי בֵית גִּלְגּוּל.** מָקוֹם עָגֹל וְעוֹלֶה וְיוֹרֵד, וְאֵין רֹב עַם יְכוֹלִין לָלֶכֶת בְּאוֹתָן שְׁבִילִין:


שבילי בית גלגול (pathways of a field full of hills and depressions) – a round place that ascends and descends, and most of the people are not able to walk on these pathways.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:6:3)


**רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לַשַּׁבָּת.** לָאו רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד מַמָּשׁ, אֶלָּא כַּרְמְלִית נִינְהוּ. וְאַמַּאי קָרֵי לַהּ רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְכֵן הָא דִּתְנַן בְּסָמוּךְ, הַבִּקְעָה בִּימוֹת הַחַמָּה רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לְשַׁבָּת, לָאו רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד מַמָּשׁ, אֶלָּא כַּרְמְלִית. וְהַשְׁתָּא כָּל הָנֵי גַּוְנֵי דִּתְנַן הָכָא דַּהֲווּ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, מַיְרֵי כְּגוֹן דְּלֵיכָּא תְּלָתָא בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנּוֹלַד הַסָּפֵק, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי כֵּיוָן דְּרַבִּים מְצוּיִין שָׁם, כִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דָּמֵי. דְּאִי בִּדְאִיכָּא תְּלָתָא בְּהַהִיא שַׁעְתָּא שֶׁנּוֹלַד הַסָּפֵק בָּהּ, אֲפִלּוּ בְּחַדְרֵי חֲדָרִים טָהוֹר, דְּיָלְפִינַן מִסּוֹטָה דַּהֲוֵי רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֵיכָּא תַּמָּן אֶלָּא תְּרֵי, בּוֹעֵל וְנִבְעֶלֶת:


רשות היחיד לשבת – it is not actually the private domain, but they are a Karmelit (an intermediate domain – between a private domain and a public domain – any open area larger than four square handbreadths that is not a public thoroughfare – including fields, seas, rivers, alleyways and lanes). But why call it a private domain? Because it is not the public domain, as will be taught in the Mishnah shortly, the valley during the dry season is the private domain regarding Shabbat, but it is not actually a private domain, but rather a Karmelit. But now all of these paths are taught in the Mishnah here are the public domain, we are speaking, for example, that they lack three [people] at the time that the doubt emerged, and even here, since many are found there, it is like the public domain. For if there are three [people] at the time that the doubt emerged regarding, even in the innermost of rooms, he is ritually pure, as we derive from the Sotah/the wife suspected of unfaithfulness towards her husband, it is a private domain, because there aren’t there other than two [people], the male initiating intercourse (i.e., the adulterer) and the woman having sexual intercourse.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:7:1)


**וּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לַטֻּמְאָה.** דְּבִימוֹת הַחַמָּה שְׁכִיחֵי אֱינָשֵׁי דְּאָזְלֵי בָּהּ לִקְצֹר וְלָדוּשׁ וְלַחֲרֹשׁ הַקַּרְקַע, לְזָרְעָהּ לַשָּׁנָה הַבָּאָה:


רשות הרבים לטומאה – for during the dry season many people are found and they go in it to reap and to thresh and to plow the ground, to seed it for the coming year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:7:2)


**בִּימוֹת הַגְּשָׁמִים.** שֶׁהִיא זְרוּעָה, אֵין אָדָם נִכְנָס לָהּ:


בימות הגשמים – that it is sown, a person doesn’t enter into it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:8:1)


**בָּסִילְקִי.** פְּלַטְיָא גְּדוֹלָה. וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק ב'‏ דְּיוֹמָא [דַּף כד] לִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית כְּמִין בָּסִילְקִי גְּדוֹלָה הָיְתָה:


בסילקי (basilica, a building with colonnades for holding courts, meeting place for merchants, exchange) – a large wide street/open place. And this is what we said in Chapter two of [Tractate] Yoma [folio 24b] the chamber of hewn stones was like a large basilica/building with colonnades for holding courts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:8:2)


**וְרוֹאֶה אֶת הַנִּכְנָסִין וְאֶת הַיּוֹצְאִין.** דְּכִי רוֹאֶה הַנִּכְנָסִים וְהַיּוֹצְאִים חֲשִׁיבָא רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד טְפֵי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ורואה את הנכנסים ואת היוצאים- for when he sees those who enter and those who leave it is considered more a private domain. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:9:1)


**הַפָּרוֹן.** דּוֹמֶה לְבָסִילְקִי, וְיֵשׁ לוֹ שְׁנֵי פְּתָחִים מְכֻוָּנִים זוֹ כְּנֶגֶד זוֹ:


הפרון (a building with a gateway leading from street to street/forum) – it is similar to a basilica. And it has two openings directly opposite each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:9:2)


**וְכֵן הַצְּדָדִין.** מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן לַפָּרוֹן, חֲשִׁיבֵי נַמִּי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, כֵּיוָן שֶׁרַבִּים מִשְׁתַּמְּשִׁין בְּאוֹתָן צְדָדִין:


וכן הצדדין – from here and from there to the building with a gateway leading from street to street/forum. It is also considered the public domain, because many use those sides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:9:3)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר הַצְּדָדִין רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד.** שֶׁאֶמְצַע הַפָּרוֹן בִּלְבַד עָשׂוּי לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בּוֹ רַבִּים, וְלֹא הַצְּדָדִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


ר' מאיר אומר הצצדין רשות היחיד – the middle of the forum alone is made for many people to use and not the sides. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/6:10:1)


**הָאִסְטְוָנִית.** מָקוֹם צַר כְּאַמָּה רֹחַב, גָּבוֹהַּ אַרְבָּעָה אוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה טְפָחִים, עוֹמֵד לִפְנֵי הַחֲנֻיּוֹת שֶׁהַסּוֹחֲרִים יוֹשְׁבִים שָׁם:


האסטוונית (a system of colonnades, colonnade with double rows) – a narrow place like a cubit wide, four or five handbreadths high, standing in front of the stores where the merchants sit there.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:1)


**הַקַּדָּר שֶׁהִנִּיחַ אֶת קְדֵרוֹתָיו.** מוֹכֵר הַקְּדֵרוֹת שֶׁהוּא חָבֵר, שֶׁהִנִּיחַ קְדֵרוֹתָיו בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּרְגִילִין לָתֵת בְּצִדֵּי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אֲבָנִים גְּדוֹלוֹת אוֹ יְתֵדוֹת לְהַרְחִיק הָעֲגָלוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיקוּ הַכְּתָלִים, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהַיְתֵדוֹת הַלָּלוּ דּוֹחֲקוֹת אֶת רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְזֶה הִנִּיחַ קְדֵרוֹת אֶצְלָם, דָּחַק גַּם הוּא אֶת הַדֶּרֶךְ יוֹתֵר, וְהָעוֹבְרִין מִתְחַכְּכִים בָּהֶן וּבִגְדֵיהֶם עוֹבְרִים עַל פִּי אֲוִירָן, וּבִגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ טְמֵאִין הֵן וּמְטַמְּאִין אֶת הַחִיצוֹנִיּוֹת:


הקדר שהניח את קדרותיו (the potter who left his pots) – a person who sells pots who is a Haver/a member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse, who left his pots in the public domain, and they would customarily place large stones or pegs on the sides of the public domain to distance the wagons so that the walls would not damage them, and since these pegs press/squeeze the public domain, and this person who left his pots near them also pressed the path more, and the passers-by rub themselves on it and their clothing passes over their airspace, and the clothing of those who are not careful in their observance of Levitical laws of ritual purity/the עם הארץ/commonfolk are impure and defile the outermost ones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:2)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים בְּמֻתָּרוֹת.** שֶׁהָעוֹבְרִים וְשָׁבִים מַסִּיטִין אוֹתָן:


במה דברים אמורים במותרות (under what circumstances – when they are untied)- when those who pass by and return shake them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:3)


**אֲבָל בַּאֲגוּדוֹת.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַסִּיטָן, הַכֹּל טָהוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אבל באגודות (but when they are tied up) – that it is impossible to shake them, everything is ritually pure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:1:4)


**שֶׁלֹּא מָסַר לוֹ אֶלָּא שְׁמִירַת הַמַּפְתֵּחַ.** וְלֹא סָמְכָא דַּעְתֵּיהּ לְהִכָּנֵס, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא נִתְפָּס עָלָיו כְּגַנָּב:


שלא מסר לו אלא שמירת המפתח – for he did not rely upon him to enter, because he would be arrested/made responsible as a thief.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:2:1)


**יָשֵׁן וּמְצָאוֹ עֵר הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** כָּל מַה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת טָמֵא, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא כְּשֶׁהֵקִיץ מִשְּׁנָתוֹ נָגַע בַּכֵּלִים שֶׁבַּבַּיִת שֶׁלֹּא רָאָה אוֹתָם מִתְּחִלָּה כְּשֶׁהָיָה יָשֵׁן, שֶׁכֵּן דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְמַשְׁמֵשׁ וְלִבְדֹּק בְּמֶה שֶּׁלֹּא רָאוּ מֵעוֹלָם. אֲבָל עֵר וּמְצָאוֹ עֵר, לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, שֶׁכְּבָר רָאָה כָּל אֲשֶׁר בַּבַּיִת כְּשֶׁהָיָה עֵר:


ישן ומצאו ער הבית טמא – everything that is in the house is ritually impure, for we are concerned that perhaps when he woke up from his sleep he touched utensils that are in the house for he didn’t see them initially when he was asleep, for it is the manner of people to touch/feel and check that which they had never seen. But someone who is awake and he was found awake, we are not concerned, for he already saw everything that is in the house while he was awake.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:2:2)


**עַד מְקוֹם שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לִפְשֹׁט אֶת יָדוֹ.** כָּל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְהַגִּיעַ לָהֶם כְּשֶׁיּוֹשִׁיט יָדוֹ מִן הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָיָה יָשֵׁן שָׁם, חָיְשִׁינַן לְהוּ שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בָּהֶם, אֲבָל לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הָלַךְ בַּבַּיִת וְנָגַע בַּכֵּלִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


עד מקום שהוא יכול לפשוט את ידו – all the vessels that he is able to reach them when he stretches forth his hand from the place where he was sleeping, we are concerned lest he touched them, but we are not concerned that perhaps he walked in the house and touched vessels. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:3:1)


**הַמַּנִּיחַ אֻמָּנִים וְכוּ'.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאֻמָּנִין סָמְכוּ דַּעְתַּיְהוּ לְמַשְׁמֵשׁ כָּל אֲשֶׁר בַּבַּיִת, וְסָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא דִּבְהָא מוֹדוּ חֲכָמִים לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר דְּכָל הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְּאֻמָּנִין פְּלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ וְאָמְרֵי דְּאֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא עַד מְקוֹם שֶׁהֵן יְכוֹלִין לִפְשֹׁט אֶת יָדָם וְלִגַּע. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


המניח אומנים וכו' – because artisans rely upon touching/feeling everything that is in the house, and I might think that in this, the Sages agree with Rabbi Meir that the entire house is ritually impure, it comes to tech us that even with artisans, the Rabbis dispute him and state that there is no impurity other than up to the place where they are able to stretch forth their hands and to touch. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:1)


**אֵשֶׁת חָבֵר.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמֶנֶת עַל הַטָּהֳרוֹת, דְּאֵשֶׁת חָבֵר הֲרֵי הִיא כְּחָבֵר:


אשת חבר – who is trustworthy on matters of ritual purity for the wife of a Haver is like a Haver.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:2)


**שֶׁהִנִּיחָה לְאֵשֶׁת עַם הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ נֶאֱמֶנֶת, דְּאֵשֶׁת עַם הָאָרֶץ וּבָנָיו כְּמוֹתוֹ, עַד שֶׁיְּקַבְּלוּ עֲלֵיהֶם דִּבְרֵי חֲבֵרוּת:


שהנייה לאשת עם הארץ – who is not trustworthy, for the wife of a commoner/who is not careful regarding the Levitical laws of ritual purity and tithes and his children are like hi, until they accept upon themselves the words of those who are careful with the observance of the Levitical laws of ritual purity and tithing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:3)


**פָּסְקָה הָרֵחַיִם.** גָּמְרָה טְחִינָתָהּ וּפָסְקָה מִלִּטְחֹן. דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא עֲסוּקָה בִּמְלַאכְתָּהּ בָּרֵחַיִם אֵינָהּ הוֹלֶכֶת וּמְמַשְׁמֶשֶׁת בַּבַּיִת:


פסקה רחים – she completed her grinding and stopped milling. For all the time that she is busy with her work on the millstones, she does not go and examine about the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:4:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים וְכוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים וכו' – And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:1)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא רוֹאֶה.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁבַּעַל הַבַּיִת יָכוֹל לִרְאוֹת הַנִּכְנָסִין בַּבַּיִת וְהַיּוֹצְאִין מִן הַבַּיִת:


בזמן שהוא רואה – at the time when the owner of the house is able to see those who enter the house and those who depart from the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:2)


**הָאֳכָלִין וְהַמַּשְׁקִין וְכוּ'‏ טְמֵאִין.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בָּהֶן הַשּׁוֹמֵר הַזֶּה שֶׁהוּא עַם הָאָרֶץ:


האוכלין והמשקין וכו' טמאין – for we are concerned that perhaps this guard touched them since he is a commoner/not careful in his observance of the laws of Levitical impurity and tithing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:3)


**אֲבָל הַמִּשְׁכָּבוֹת וְהַמּוֹשָׁבוֹת וְכוּ'‏ טְהוֹרִין.** שֶׁאֵין עַם הָאָרֶץ עוֹשֶׂה מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּהֶסֵּט, דְּלֹא גָּזְרוּ אֶלָּא עַל טֻמְאַת מַגָּעוֹ בִּלְבַד:


אבל המשכבות והמושבות וכו' טהורין – for a commoner does not perform acts of lying or sitting and doesn’t defile through shaking, for they (i.e., the Rabbis) did not decree other than on the defilement of his contact alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:4)


**וְלֹא אֶת הַיּוֹשְׁבִים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאִם עוֹמֵד רוֹאֵהוּ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה אֶת הַיּוֹשֵׁב, הַכֹּל טָמֵא:


ולא את היושבים – even though that if he stands he sees him, since he doesn’t see the person who is sitting, everything is ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:5)


**אֲפִלּוּ מוּבָל.** אֲפִלּוּ הָיָה הַשּׁוֹמֵר הַזֶּה שֶׁהוּא עַם הָאָרֶץ, מוּבָל, שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לֵילֵךְ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מוֹבִילִים אוֹתוֹ:


אפילו מובל (even someone immobilized) – even if this guard/watchman who is a commoner/not careful in his observance of the laws of Levitical purity and tithing is immobilized, that he is unable to walk unless they carry him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:6)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ כָּפוּת.** קָשׁוּר:


ואפילו כפות – and tied up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:5:7)


**הַכֹּל טָמֵא.** שֶׁלֹּא מְטַמְּאִינַן לְהוּ מִשּׁוּם מִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נִכְנַס שָׁם נָכְרִי אוֹ אִשָּׁה נִדָּה וְיָשְׁבָה וְהִסִּיטָה וְטִמְּאָה אֶת הַכֹּל:


הכל טמא – for we don’t defile them because of the lying or sitting of a commoner, but rather because we are concerned that perhaps a heathen or a menstruating woman enters there and she sat or shook something and defiled everything.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:1)


**הַגַּבָּאִים שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ לַבַּיִת.** [יִשְׂרְאֵלִים] שֶׁהֵן גַּבָּאִין שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ לִגְבּוֹת מִיַּד יִשְׂרָאֵל כֶּסֶף גֻּלְגָּלְתָּא וּמִסִּים וְאַרְנוֹנִיּוֹת, וְהֵן עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ, וְנִכְנְסוּ לְבֵיתוֹ לַעֲבֹט עֲבוֹטוֹ:


הגבאים שנכנסו לבית (of Israelites) -they are tax collectors of the king to collect from the hand of an Israel the money of their capitation tax and the taxes and taxes from crops and other farmer’s produces delivered in kind, and they are commoners/not careful in the observance of the laws of Levitical purity and tithing, and they entered into his house to seize his pledge.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:2)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** כָּל כְּלֵי הַבַּיִת טָמֵא, לְפִי שֶׁמְּחַפְּשִׂין בְּכָל הַבַּיִת:


הבית טמא – all the vessels of the house are ritually impure because they search throughout the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:3)


**אִם יֵשׁ עִמָּהֶן גּוֹי נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר לֹא נִכְנָסְנוּ.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם אֵין עִמָּהֶן נָכְרִי הֵן יוֹתֵר וְיוֹתֵר נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא נִכְנַסְנוּ. אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם סֵיפָא נָקַט לַהּ, דְּקָתָנֵי אֲבָל אֵין נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר נִכְנַסְנוּ וְלֹא נָגַעְנוּ, דְּדַוְקָא אִם יֵשׁ עִמָּהֶם נָכְרִי אֵין נֶאֱמָנִים, דְּמִרְתְּתֵי מִן הַנָּכְרִי שֶׁמָּא יַעֲנִישֵׁם אִם לֹא יְחַפְּשׂוּ כָּל הַבַּיִת וְעַל כָּרְחָךְ הֵם מְמַשְׁמְשִׁין כָּל אֲשֶׁר בַּבַּיִת. אֲבָל אִם אֵין עִמָּהֶם נָכְרִי, נֶאֱמָנִים אַף בָּזֶה לוֹמַר נִכְנַסְנוּ אֲבָל לֹא נָגַעְנוּ:


אם יש עמהן גוי נאמנים לומר לא נכנסנו – and all the more so if there isn’t a heathen with them, they are more and more believed to state, “we have not entered.” But because of the concluding phrase of the clause of the Mishnah, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took it (i.e., these words), as it is taught: “but they are not believed to state – we entered, but didn’t touch,” for specifically if there is a heathen with them they are not believed, for they are frightened/trembling from the heathen lest he punish them if they don’t search through the entire house, and by force they feel touch/handle everything that is in the house. But if there is no heathen with them, they are believed even in this to state, “we entered but we didn’t touch [anything].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:6:4)


**אִם יֵשׁ עִמָּהֶן נָכְרִי אוֹ אִשָּׁה.** שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עַל הַנָּכְרִים לִהְיוֹת כַּזָּבִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם. וְהָאִשָּׁה, שֶׁמָּא הִיא נִדָּה אוֹ זָבָה:


אם יש עמהן גוי או אשה – that they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed on the heathens to be like people with gonorrhea/זבים for all of their matters. And the woman, perhaps she is a menstruating woman/נדה or a זבה/woman with a flux.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:1)


**אוֹדְיָארִין.** הַבַּלָּנִין בַּעֲלֵי הַמֶּרְחֲצָאוֹת יֵשׁ לָהֶן חַלּוֹנוֹת חַלּוֹנוֹת, וְכָל מִי שֶׁנִּכְנָס לַמֶּרְחָץ נוֹתֵן בְּגָדָיו כָּל אֶחָד בְּחַלּוֹן וְנוֹעֵל עָלָיו בַּדֶּלֶת שֶׁבַּחַלּוֹן:


אודיארין (store-keeper) – a bathing attendant – who receives a small coin as a fee/keeper of the clothes at the baths, the owners of the bathhouses which have many windows/apertures and anyone who enters to the bathhouse places his clothes in a window and he (i.e., the bathing attendant) locks them up with a door that is in the aperture/window.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:2)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה מְטַהֵר.** מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁנָּעַל הַדֶּלֶת בִּפְנֵי הַחַלּוֹן. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יִגַּע שָׁם אָדָם טָמֵא, מֵאַחַר שֶׁנִּתְפָּס עָלָיו כְּגַנָּב:


רבי אליעזר בן עזריה מטהר – from the time that he looks the door in front of the window/aperture. And we are not concerned that perhaps a ritually impure person will touch there, after he is seized as a thief.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:3)


**עַד שֶׁיִּתֶּן לְתוֹכוֹ מַפְתֵּחַ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנּוֹתֵן הַמַּפְתֵּחַ בְּיַד הַבַּלָּן שֶׁהוּא עַם הָאָרֶץ, הָא תְּנַן לְעֵיל הַמּוֹסֵר מַפְתֵּחַ לְעַם הָאָרֶץ הַבַּיִת טָהוֹר:


עד שיתן לו המפתח – and even though he places the key in the hand of the bathing attendant who is a commoner/not stringent in the observance of the Levitical laws of purity and tithes, for it is taught in the Mishnah (a text which I could not find) above (note: we have a teaching in the Tosefta Tractate Taharot, Chapter 8, Halakha 1 that teaches the reverse: "המוסר מפתח לעם הארץ הבית טמא"/He who gives over the key to a commoner, the house is ritually impure – the exact reverse of what the Bartenura commentary is pointing out and which agrees with the view of the Sages): “He who gives over the key to a commoner, the house is ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:4)


**אוֹ עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה לוֹ סִימָן.** לְהַכִּיר בּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע אָדָם שָׁם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


או עד שיעשה לו סימן – to recognize that no person touched there. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:5)


**הַמַּנִּיחַ אֶת כֵּלָיו.** כֵּלִים שֶׁבָּצַר בָּהֶם בְּטָהֳרָה בְּשָׁנָה זוֹ, מַנִּיחַ אוֹתָם לִבְצֹר בָּהֶם בְּטָהֳרָה לְשָׁנָה הַבָּאָה:


המניח את כליו – vessels that he cut grapes with in ritual purity in this year, he leaves them to cut grapes with them in ritual purity in the following year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:6)


**בְּנָכְרִי כֵּלָיו טְהוֹרִין.** גָּרְסִינַן. כְּלוֹמַר אִם הִנִּיחָן אֵצֶל נָכְרִי אֵין בָּהֶם מִשּׁוּם אִסּוּר יֵין נֶסֶךְ, דְּלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא לְקָחָן הַנָּכְרִי בְּלֹא יְדִיעַת יִשְׂרָאֵל לַעֲשׂוֹת בַּגַּת:


בנכרי כליו טהורין – we have this reading, meaning to say, if he left them with a heathen, there is no prohibition of wine poured as a libation/יין נסך associated with them, for we are not concerned that perhaps a Canaanite took them without the knowledge of the Israelite to make [wine] with them in the vat for wine-pressing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:7:7)


**וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** דְּחוֹשֵׁשׁ לָהֶן מִשּׁוּם טֻמְאַת מַגָּע, טְמֵאִים אִם הִסִּיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מֵהֶן, עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר בְּלִבִּי הָיָה לְשָׁמְרָם. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים דְּלֹא גָּרְסֵי בְּנָכְרִי, אֶלָּא הַמַּנִּיחַ כְּלִי מִגַּת זוֹ לְגַת הַבָּאָה כֵּלָיו טְהוֹרִים, וּמַיְרֵי בְּכֹהֵן הַלּוֹקֵחַ יַיִן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ וּמַנִּיחַ כֵּלָיו אֶצְלוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּקַּח מִמֶּנּוּ לַגַּת הַבָּאָה, וְהוֹאִיל וְכֵלִים שֶׁל כֹּהֵן הֵן, מִרְתַּת עַם הָאָרֶץ וְלֹא נָגַע בְּהוּ. אֲבָל אִם הַכֵּלִים הֵן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁרוֹצֶה לַעֲשׂוֹת יֵינוֹ בְּטָהֳרָה, לֹא מִזְדְּהַר בְּהוּ עַם הָאָרֶץ כֻּלֵּי הַאי, וְחָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא נָגַע בָּהֶן אָדָם טָמֵא, אִם הִסִּיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מֵהֶן, עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר בְּלִבִּי הָיָה לְשָׁמְרָם:


ובישראל – for he worries/is concerned about them because of defilement through contact, they are ritually impure if his attention was diverted from them, until he says: It was my intention (literally, in my heart) to guard them. But there are books in which we do not have the reading [of the word]: "בנכרי"/with a heathen, but rather a person who leaves a vessel from this vintage to the next vintage, his vessels are ritually pure, and we are dealing with a Kohen who purchases wine of heave-offering/priest’s due from a commoner/who is not careful in his observance of the laws of Levitical purity or tithes and leaves his vessels with him in order that he can purchase from him during the next vintage, for since the vessels belong to a Kohen, the commoner trembles and doesn’t touch them. But if the vessels are of an Israelite who desires to make his wine in a state of ritual purity, a commoner is not all that careful with them, and we are concerned that perhaps a ritually impure person touched them, if his attention was diverted from them, until he states: “it was my intention to guard them.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:1)


**מִי שֶׁהָיָה טָהוֹר.** כֹּהֵן שֶׁהָיָה טָהוֹר לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה, וְהִסִּיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מִלֶּאֱכֹל עוֹד בַּתְּרוּמָה:


מי שהיה טהור – a Kohen who was ritually pure to consume heave-offering and his mind was diverted/he was discarding from his mind to consume any more heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:2)


**שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ טְמֵאִים פּוֹרְשִׁים מִמֶּנּוּ.** הַטְּמֵאִים שֶׁיּוֹדְעִים בּוֹ שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר, פּוֹרְשִׁים מִמֶּנּוּ, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בְּאָדָם טָמֵא וְטִמְּאָהוּ:


שדרך טמאים פרושים ממנו – the ritually impure people that know of him that he is ritually pure, separate from him, therefore, we are not concerned that he came in contact with a ritually impure person and he defiled him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁהִסִּיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מטמאין – for after his mind was diverted/he was discarding from his mind [to consume any more heave offering]. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:4)


**הָיוּ יָדָיו טְהוֹרוֹת.** בְּהָא מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דַּאֲפִלּוּ אָמַר יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי שֶׁלֹּא נִטְמְאוּ יָדַי:


היו ידיו טהורות – in this Rabbi Yehuda agrees that even if he said: “I know that I was my hands were not ritually defiled.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:8:5)


**יָדָיו טְמֵאוֹת.** וּפוֹסְלִין אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, מֵאַחַר שֶׁהִסִּיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מִלֶּאֱכֹל, לְפִי שֶׁהַיָּדַיִם עַסְקָנִיּוֹת הֵן וְנָגַע וְלָאו אַדַּעְתֵּיהּ:


ידיו טמאות – and they ritually defile the heave-offering, for after he diverted his mind/discarded from his mind to eat [any more heave-offering], because the hands are busy/preoccupied and they touched [impure things] unintentionally.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/7:9:1)


**גַּרְגְּרָנִיּוֹת.** זוֹלְלוֹת וּמִתְאַוּוֹת לְמַאֲכָל. תַּרְגּוּם כִּי הָיִיתִי זוֹלֵלָה (איכה א), אֲרֵי הֲוֵיתִי גַרְגְּרָנִית. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


גרגניות (unable to resist temptation/gluttonous) – they despise/disregard and are desirous for food. It is the Aramaic translation of (Lamentations 1:11): “How abject/what a glutton I have become.” I have become a glutton/bibber like a lion. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:1:1)


**הַדָּר עִם עַם הָאָרֶץ.** מֻקָּפוֹת צָמִיד פָּתִיל. טְמֵאוֹת, מִשּׁוּם אִשְׁתּוֹ נִדָּה, שֶׁמָּא הִסִּיטָה וְיָשְׁבָה וְטִמְּאָה אֶת הַכֹּל:


הדר עם עם הארץ. מוקפות צמיד פתיל – they are ritually impure because his wife is a menstruating woman, lest she shook it and sat and defiled everything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:1:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְטַהֵר בְּתַנּוּר.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַסִּיטוֹ, דִּסְתָמוֹ מְחֻבָּר קְצָת, שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לוֹ טְפֵלָה לְחַבְּרוֹ בָּאָרֶץ. וּמַאן דִּמְטַמֵּא סָבַר גָּזְרִינַן מְחֻבָּר אַטּוּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְחֻבָּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה מטהר בתנור – because it is impossible to shake it, for it is merely attached a bit, and they make it secondary to attach it to the ground. But the one who defiles it (i.e., Rabbi Yossi) holds that we make the degree that something attached is because of that which is not attached. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:1)


**הַמַּפְקִיד כֵּלִים אֵצֶל עַם הָאָרֶץ טְמֵאִים טְמֵא מֵת.** וּצְרִיכִין הַזָּאָה שְׁלִישִׁי וּשְׁבִיעִי:


המפקיד כלים אצל עם הארץ טמאים טמא מת – and requiring sprinkling on the third and seventh days (see Numbers 19:19).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:2)


**אִם מַכִּירוֹ.** אִם הָעַם הָאָרֶץ מַכִּיר בַּמַּפְקִיד שֶׁהוּא כֹּהֵן אוֹכֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה, זָהִיר עַל הַפִּקָּדוֹן שֶׁלּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יְטַמְּאֶנּוּ בְּמֵת, וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ הַזָּאָה:


אם מכירו – if the commoner recognizes the depositor that he is a Kohen who consumes heave-offering/priest’s due, he is careful regarding his deposit that it will not be defiled by a corpse, and it doesn’t require sprinkling [on the third and seventh days].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:3)


**אֲבָל טְמֵאִין מִדְרָס.** מִשּׁוּם אִשְׁתּוֹ נִדָּה שֶׁמָּא יָשְׁבָה עֲלֵיהֶן, דְּאֵינוֹ נִזְהָר מִטֻּמְאַת אִשְׁתּוֹ:


אבל טמאים מדרס – because his wife is a menstruating woman, lest she sat upon them, for he is not careful from the defilement of his wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:4)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא רוֹצֶצֶת.** שֶׁהַכִּסּוּי דּוֹחֵק הַבְּגָדִים מֵחֲמַת שֶׁהִיא מְלֵאָה בְּיוֹתֵר:


בזמן שהיא רוצצת (at the time when it is tightly packed) – that the covering presses the clothing on account of the fact that it is too full.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:5)


**טְמֵאִים מִדְרָס.** הַבְּגָדִים. דִּכְשֶׁאִשְׁתּוֹ יוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל הַתֵּבָה אוֹ נִשְׁעֶנֶת עָלֶיהָ, מַכְבֶּדֶת עַל הַבְּגָדִים:


טמאים מדרס – the clothes [are defiled through treading]. For when his wife sits upon the box [full of clothing] or leans upon it, she weighs down upon the clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:6)


**אִם אֵינָהּ רוֹצֶצֶת טְמֵאִין מַדָּף.** כְּלוֹמַר טֻמְאָה קַלָּה, לְטַמֵּא אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין, כְּמוֹ עַל גַּבּוֹ שֶׁל זָב שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּגָדִים:


אם אינה רוצצת טמאין מדף (if it is not tightly packed, they are unclean with indirect contact for conferring ritual impurity) -meaning to say, a lighter defilement, to defile foods and liquids, like on top of a person with gonorrhea who does not defile clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:2:7)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַמַּפְתֵּחַ בְּיַד בְּעָלִים.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הִסִּיטָה:


אע"פ שמפתח ביד בעלים – for we are concerned lest he shook it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:3:1)


**הַמְאַבֵּד בַּיּוֹם וּמָצָא בַיּוֹם טָהוֹר.** דְּמִסְּתָמָא אִם הָיָה אָדָם מוֹצְאוֹ וְנוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ הָיָה מַגְבִּיהוֹ, כְּדֶרֶךְ מוֹצֵא אֲבֵדָה. אֲבָל בַּלַּיְלָה, אֲפִלּוּ שָׁעָה אַחַת דְּלֹא חֲזִי לֵיהּ, חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בּוֹ אָדָם טָמֵא וְלֹא רָאָהוּ. וּמַתְנִיתִין בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר אַיְרֵי, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן בַּלַּיְלָה טְמֵאִים מִדְרָס וּטְמֵא מֵת. אֲבָל בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא, בֵּין בַּיּוֹם בֵּין בַּלַּיְלָה טְמֵאִים. וְהָכִי אִיתָא בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


המאבד ביום ומצא ביום טהור – for something undefined if a person would find it and touch/come in contact with it, he would lift it up, in the manner that he finds a lost object. But at night, even for one hour that he can’t see it, we are concerned lest a ritually impure person touched it and he did not see it. But our Mishnah is dealing with the public domain, and its manner of doubt is assumed to be ritually pure but even so at night, they are ritually impure by Midras/ritual impurity through treading or by someone defiled by a corpse. But in the private domain, its manner of doubt is ritually impure, whether during the day or whether at night, and this is brought in the Tosefta (Tractate Taharot, Chapter 9, Halakha 10).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:3:2)


**בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים טְהוֹרִים בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד טְמֵאִין.** דְּאֵין כָּאן אֶלָּא סָפֵק שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בָּהֶן נָכְרִי אוֹ עַם הָאָרֶץ, הִלְכָּךְ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר, טְהוֹרִים. בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד דִּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא, טְמֵאִים:


ברשות הרבים טהורים ברשות היחיד טמאים – We don’t have here other than a manner of doubt, lest a heathen or a commoner/who is not careful in his observance of the laws of Levitical purity or tithing touched them. Therefore, in the public domain, where there is a matter of doubt of it being ritually pure, it is ritually pure, whereas in the private domain, where there is a matter of doubt of it being ritually impure, they are ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:3:3)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֻנַּח בִּרְשׁוּת עַם הָאָרֶץ.** וְכָל מַה שֶּׁבְּבֵית עַם הָאָרֶץ הָוֵי טְמֵא מֵת וְצָרִיךְ הַזָּאָה:


מפני שהונח ברשות עם הארץ – and everything that is in the house of common is considered having corpse defilement and requires sprinkling [on the third and seventh days – see Numbers 19:19].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:4:1)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָיוּ גַנָּבִים.** כְּלוֹמַר, כֵּיוָן דְּהִנִּיחוֹ נָעוּל וּמְצָאוֹ פָּתוּחַ, וַדַּאי גַּנָּבִים הָיוּ שָׁם וּפְתָחוּהוּ, וְהַאי דְּלֹא מְטַמְּאִינַן לֵיהּ, דְּאֵמוּר נִמְלְכוּ וְהָלְכוּ לָהֶן וְלֹא נָגְעוּ בְּכָל מַה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מפני שהיו גנבים – meaning to say, since they left it locked and found it open, certainly thieves were there and opened it, and that we don’t defile him, for it is stated that they changed their mind and went away and didn’t touch anything that was in the house. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:5:1)


**שֶׁנִּכְנְסָה שֶׁלֹּא בִרְשׁוּת.** וּמִרְתְּתָא שֶׁלֹּא תִּתָּפֵס כְּגַנָּב וּמְמַהֶרֶת לָצֵאת, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלְּצֹרֶךְ בְּנוֹ אוֹ בְּהֶמְתּוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת נִכְנְסָה:


שנכנסה שלא ברשות – out of trembling that she will not be caught like a thief and she runs to leave, and even though she entered for the need of his (i.e., the Haver/the member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse) son or the beast of the owner of the house.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:1)


**כֹּל הַמְיֻחָד לְמַאֲכַל אָדָם.** כְּגוֹן סְתָם אֳכָלִין:


כל המיוחד למאכל אדם – as, for example, mere/undefined foods.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:2)


**טָמֵא.** טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין עַד שֶׁיִּפָּסֵל מֵאֲכִילַת הַכֶּלֶב:


טמא – the ritual impurity of foods [is impure] until it becomes invalid/unfit from being the food for a dog.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:3)


**עַד שֶׁיְּיַחֲדֶנּוּ לָאָדָם.** דְּיֵשׁ חִלּוּק בֵּין אַפּוֹקֵי טֻמְאָה מִנֵּיהּ, לַאֲתוֹיֵי לֵיהּ טֻמְאָה:


עד שייחדנו לאדם – that there is a separation between removing defilement from it and bringing defilement to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:4)


**גּוֹזָל שֶׁנָּפַל לְגַת.** כְּשֶׁלֹּא נָפַל לַגַּת לֹא פְּלִיגֵי רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי וְרַבָּנָן, דְּנִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר בַּכְּרַכִּים לֹא בַּעְיָא מַחֲשָׁבָה לְשַׁוּוֹיַהּ אֹכֶל, מִשּׁוּם דְּאִיכָּא רֹב עַם הַבָּאִים לַשּׁוּק וְאָכְלֵי נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר. וּבַכְּפָרִים דְּלֵיכָּא רֹב עַם בָּעֵי מַחֲשָׁבָה, כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק קַמָּא. כִּי פְלִיגֵי כְּשֶׁנָּפַל לַגַּת וּמֵת בַּכְּרַכִּים, רַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי אַף עַל גַּב דְּבַכְּרַכִּים לֹא בָּעֵי מַחֲשָׁבָה, הֵיכָא דְּנָפַל לַגַּת מְאָסַתּוּ גִּתּוֹ וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי וּבָעֵי מַחֲשָׁבָה. וְרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי סָבַר לֹא מְאָסַתּוּ גִּתּוֹ וַהֲרֵי הוּא כִּשְׁאָר נִבְלַת עוֹף טָהוֹר דְּלֹא בַּעְיָא מַחֲשָׁבָה בַּכְּרַכִּים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי:


גוזל שנפל לגת – when it didn’t fall into the vat, thee is no dispute [between] Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri and the Rabbis. For the carrion of a ritually pure bird in the cities that doesn’t require intention to declare it food, because there a lot of people with those who come to the marketplace and consume the carrion of a pure bird. But in the villages where there isn’t a lot of people, it requires intention, as is explained in chapter 1 (see Mishnah 1). Where they disagree is where it (i.e., the bird) fell into the vat and died in the cities, the Rabbis hold that even though that in the cities it doesn’t require intention/giving thought to it, where it fell into the vat, his vat made it detestable and it is not appropriate/fit and requires intention/giving thought to it, whereas Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri holds that his vat did not make it rejected, and it is like the rest of the carrion of ritually pure birds that don’t require intention/giving thought to it in the cities. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:6:5)


**חִשַּׁב עָלָיו חֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן.** לְהַאֲכִילוֹ לְנָכְרִי:


חישב עליו חרש שוטה וקטן – to feed it to Canaanites.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:1)


**אֲחוֹרֵי כֵלִים שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בְמַשְׁקִים.** הַאי דְּנָקַט אֲחוֹרֵי כֵּלִים, מִשּׁוּם דְּקִילּוּ, שֶׁכְּלִי שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ אֲחוֹרָיו בְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, אֲחוֹרָיו טְמֵאִין, תּוֹכוֹ אָזְנָיו אֳגָנָיו וְיָדָיו טְהוֹרִין, מִשּׁוּם דְּטֻמְאַת מַשְׁקִים לְטַמֵּא כֵּלִים מִדְּרַבָּנָן הֵקֵלּוּ בָהּ. אֲבָל אִם נִטְמָא תּוֹכוֹ בְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, נִטְמָא כֻּלּוֹ:


אחורי כלים שנטמאו במשקין – that [the Mishnah] took/used the term, "אחורי כלים"/the outer sides of utensils, is because they were lenient, for a vessel whose outer part was ritually defiled with ritually impure liquids, its outer sides are ritually impure, but its inside – its handles, its rims/borders and its hands are ritually pure because the ritually impurity of liquids to defile vessels is from the Rabbis, they were lenient concerning it. But if its inside was defiled by impure liquids, all of it is defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:2)


**מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין.** אֲפִלּוּ דְּחֻלִּין:


מטמאים את המשקין – even that which is non-consecrated [liquids].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:3)


**וְאֵין פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הָאֳכָלִין.** וַאֲפִלּוּ דִּתְרוּמָה:


ואין פוסלין את התרומה – and even that of heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר מְטַמְּאִין אֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין וּפוֹסְלִין אֶת הָאֳכָלִים.** רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ יָלֵיף (בנדה דף ז:) דַּאֲחוֹרֵי כֵּלִים פּוֹסְלִים אֳכָלֵי תְּרוּמָה קַל וָחֹמֶר מִטְּבוּל יוֹם, מַה טְּבוּל יוֹם שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין, דְּלֹא גָּזְרוּ רַבָּנָן עַל טְבוּל יוֹם שֶׁיְּהֵא מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי פּוֹסֵל אֳכָלִים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וּבָא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ וְטָהֵר וְאַחַר יֹאכַל מִן הַקֳּדָשִׁים, אֵינוֹ טָהוֹר לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה עַד שֶׁיַּעֲרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ, דִּקְרָא בִּתְרוּמָה מִשְׁתַּעֵי. אֲחוֹרֵי כֵּלִים שֶׁמְּטַמְּאִין אֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין, אֵינוֹ דִּין שֶׁיְּהוּ פּוֹסְלִין אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּלֹא חָיֵשׁ לְהַאי קַל וָחֹמֶר, מִשּׁוּם דַּאֲחוֹרֵי כֵּלִים שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ בְּמַשְׁקִין דְּרַבָּנָן, וּטְבוּל יוֹם דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, וּדְרַבָּנָן מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא אֵין דָּנִין קַל וָחֹמֶר:


ר' יהושע אומר מטמאין את המשקין ופוסלין את האוכלים – Rabbi Yehoshua derives (in Tractate Niddah 7b) that the outer sides of utensils defile foods of heave-offering through an a fortiori/Kal VaHomer from the law of a person who immersed himself that day/טבול יום. Just as a person who immersed himself that day does not defile liquids and the Sages did not decree on a person who immersed himself that day that he defiles the liquids, nevertheless, he defiles the foods of heave-offering, as it is written (Leviticus 22:7): “As soon as the sun sets, he shall be pure; and afterward he may eat of the sacred donations, [for they are his food],” for he is not ritually pure to eat heave-offering until his sun has set, for the Biblical verse is speaking about heave-offering/Terumah. The outer sides of vessels that defile the liquids, does it not follow that they would invalidate the heave-offering? But Rabbi Eliezer did not concern himself with this a fortiori/Kal VaHomer, because the outer sides of utensils that are defiled by liquids is according to the Rabbis, and the person who immersed himself that day/טבול יום is from the Torah, and that which is Rabbinic, we don’t make an a fortiori [argument] on something from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:5)


**שִׁמְעוֹן אֲחִי עֲזַרְיָה.** לְפִי שֶׁעֲזַרְיָה עָסַק בִּפְרַקְמַטְיָא וְהָיָה מְסַפֵּק מָזוֹן לְשִׁמְעוֹן אָחִיו שֶׁיַּעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה, לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרָא עַל שֵׁם עֲזַרְיָה אָחִיו:


שמעון אחי עזריה – because Azariah dealt with practical maters and he would supply food to his brother Shimon who engaged in Torah study, therefore he is called by the name of Azariah, his brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:7:6)


**לֹא כָךְ וְלֹא כָךְ.** לֹא מְטַמְּאִין מַשְׁקֵה חֻלִּין וְלֹא פּוֹסְלִין אֳכָלֵי תְּרוּמָה, אֲבָל מְטַמְּאִין מַשְׁקֵה תְּרוּמָה, וְאוֹתָן מַשְׁקִין שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה מְטַמְּאִין אֶחָד בְּאֳכָלֵי תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ שְׁנִיִּים, וְאוֹתָן אֳכָלִים פּוֹסְלִים אֳכָלִים אֲחֵרִים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה. וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמְרֵי הָאֳכָלִים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה לְמַשְׁקִין שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, מְטַמְּאֶיךָ לֹא טִמְּאוּנִי, כְּלוֹמַר אֲחוֹרֵי כְּלִי הַמְּטַמְּאֶךָ לֹא טִמְּאוּנִי, דְּלֹא פָּסְלֵי אֳכָלֵי תְּרוּמָה, וְאַתָּה טִמֵּאתָנִי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


לא כך ולא כך – we do not defile unconsecrated liquids nor do we invalidate/make unit food of heave-offering, but we do defile liquid have-offering, and those liquids of heave-offering, we defile at first degree of ritual impurity with foods of heave-offering that became second-degree of ritual impurity, and those foods invalidate/make unit other foods of heave-offering. And this is that we state that foods of heave-offering to liquids of heave-offering, “things that defile you do not defile me,” meaning to say, “the outer sides of a vessel that defiled you do not defile me, for they do not invalidate foods of heave offering, bur you defile me.” But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:1)


**שֶׁהִיא קְטַפְרֵס.** שֶׁצִּדָּהּ אֶחָד נָמוּךְ כְּעֵין מִדְרוֹן:


שהיא קטפרס ([a kneading trough] that lay on an incline/sloping downward) – that one side is low, similar to a slope.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:2)


**וּמַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ מִלְּמַטָּן.** בַּצַּד הַנָּמוּךְ, וְהַיְנוּ בָּצֵק שֶׁבְּלִילָתָהּ רַכָּה וּמַשְׁקֶה שֶׁלָּהּ טוֹפֵחַ, אֲבָל מִלְּמַעְלָה בְּלִילָתָהּ עָבָה וְאֵין מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ:


משקה טופח מלמטן (running liquid is from below) – on the low side, that is the [unclean] dough that its mixture is soft and its liquid drips, but from above, its mixture is thick and its liquid doesn’t drip.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:3)


**שָׁלֹשׁ חֲתִיכוֹת.** בֵּין שְׁלָשְׁתָּן כַּבֵּיצָה, אַחַת טוֹפֵחַ וּשְׁתַּיִם אֵינָן טוֹפֵחַ, סְדוּרוֹת זוֹ אֵצֶל זוֹ, וְהַטּוֹפַחַת מִלְּמַטָּה, וְהַשְׁתָּא הָאֶמְצָעִית נוֹגַעַת בַּטּוֹפַחַת, וְהָעֶלְיוֹנָה נוֹגַעַת בָּאֶמְצָעִית:


שלש חתיכות – between the three [pieces of dough] is like an egg’s bulk, one dripping and two that do not drip, they are arranged one next to the other, and the one that drips is below, and now the middle one touches the dripping one and the upper one touches the middle one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:4)


**אֵינָן מִצְטָרְפוֹת.** לְכַבֵּיצָה לְטַמֵּא הַמַּשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ שֶׁלְּמַטָּה וְיַחֲזֹר הַמַּשְׁקֶה וִיטַמֵּא אֶת הָעֲרֵבָה, דִּנְגִיעָה אֵינָהּ מְחַבֶּרֶת אֶת הַבָּצֵק, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נוֹשְׁכוֹת כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא (משנה ז) מִקְרָצוֹת נוֹשְׁכוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ:


אינן מצטרפות – to be an egg’s bulk to defile the liquid that is flowing from below and the liquid returns and defiles the kneading trough, for contact does not connect the dough unless they stick together as is taught in the Mishnah in the First Chapter [of Tractate Taharot] (Mishnah 7)"מקרצות נושכות זו את זו" /pieces of dough – separated from the main dough in the trough - stick to each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:5)


**שְׁתַּיִם מִצְטָרְפוֹת.** אִם לֹא הָיוּ אֶלָּא שְׁתֵּי חֲתִיכוֹת בָּצֵק, הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ וְשֶׁלְּמַעְלָה הֵימֶנָּה הַנּוֹגַעַת בָּהּ, וּבֵין שְׁתֵּיהֶן כַּבֵּיצָה, מִצְטָרְפוֹת לְטַמֵּא הַמַּשְׁקֶה וְיַחֲזֹר וִיטַמֵּא אֶת הָעֲרֵבָה:


שתים מצטרפות – if there weren’t other than two pieces of dough, the bottom one that has liquid flowing and the one that is above it that touches it, and if there is between of them an egg’s bulk, they combine to defile the liquid and return and defile the kneading trough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ שְׁתַּיִם אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הָיוּ רוֹצְצוֹת מַשְׁקֶה.** לְעוֹלָם אֵין מַשְׁקֶה מְחַבֵּר עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה מַשְׁקֶה דּוֹחֵק וְעוֹמֵד בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַחֲתִיכוֹת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אפילו שתים אין מצטרפין אא"כ היו רוצצות משקה – always the liquid does not connect until the liquid will be pressing and standing between the two pieces [of dough]. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:7)


**וְאִם הָיָה מַשְׁקֶה עוֹמֵד.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם לֹא הָיָה קְטַפְרֵס אֶלָּא אֶשְׁבֹּרֶן, וְהַמַּשְׁקֶה עוֹמֵד בֵּין חֲתִיכוֹת הַבָּצֵק, אֲפִלּוּ כָּל שִׁעוּר הַבֵּיצָה שֶׁבַּבָּצֵק פֵּרוּרִים דַּקִּין כְּעֵין הַחַרְדָּל מְפֹרָדִים זֶה מִזֶּה, הַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁעוֹמֵד מְחַבְּרָן וּמִצְטָרְפִין לְכַבֵּיצָה, וּמְטַמְּאִין הַמַּשְׁקֶה וְחוֹזֵר הַמַּשְׁקֶה וּמְטַמֵּא אֶת הָעֲרֵבָה:


ואם היה משקה עומד (and if it was standing liquid) – meaning to say, there wasn’t a sloping/inkling, but rather a cavity for the reception of water/a pond, and the liquid stands between the pieces of dough, even all the measure of an the egg’s bulk that is in the dough are thin crumbs similar to the mustard seed that are separated one from the other, the standing liquid attaches them and combines them to be an egg’s bulk, and defiles the liquid and the liquid returns and defiles the kneading trough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:8:8)


**אֹכֶל פָּרוּר.** אֹכֶל שֶׁהוּא חָלוּק לְפֵרוּרִים פֵּרוּרִים דַּקִּין אֵינוֹ מִצְטָרֵף, דְּאֵין הַמַּשְׁקֶה מְחַבְּרָן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי דּוֹסָא:


אוכל פרור – food that is divided into thin crumbs does not combine, for the liquid does not attach them. And the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Dosa.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:1)


**כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִשִּׁיקָהּ לַמִּקְוֶה.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁחִבֵּר קָצֶה אֶחָד מִן הַמַּקֵּל בַּמִּקְוֶה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הִטְבִּילוֹ כֻּלּוֹ, טָהֲרוּ כָּל הַמַּשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁוֹ הָאַחֵר, דִּקְטַפְרֵס חִבּוּר:


כיון שהשיקה למקוה – since the end of one of the sticks was attached in the Mikveh, even though he didn’t immerse all of it, he purified all the impure liquids that are on the other head, for the incline/sloping is an attachment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:2)


**עַד שֶׁיַּטְבִּיל אֶת כֻּלָּהּ.** דְּקָסָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן קְטַפְרֵס אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר:


עד שיטביל את כולה – for the Rabbis hold that an incline/sloping is not an attachment/connection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:3)


**הַנִּצּוֹק.** קִלּוּחַ צִנּוֹר הַבָּא מִלְּמַעְלָה לְמַטָּה בִּזְקִיפָה כְּעַמּוּד:


הניצוק (an uninterrupted flow of a liquid – poured from vessel to vessel) – the continuous flow/jet of a water pipe/spout that comes from above to below with erection like a pillar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:4)


**קְטַפְרֵס.** מַיִם הַבָּאִים מִלְּמַעְלָה לְמַטָּה דֶּרֶךְ מִדְרוֹן שֶׁל תֵּל גָּבוֹהַּ:


קטפרס – water from above to below through the sloping of a high hill.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:5)


**וּמַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ.** וְאֵין בּוֹ טוֹפֵחַ עַל מְנָת לְהַטְפִּיחַ, שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כָּל כָּךְ מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁאִם טָפַח בָּהֶן יָדוֹ עָלָה בָּהּ מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁיּוּכַל לְהַטְפִּיחַ דָּבָר אַחֵר:


ומשקה טופח – it doesn’t have moistness to moisten other objects, for it doesn’t have in it so much liquid for if he struck his hand in them, liquid would come up that would be able to moisten something else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:6)


**אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר לֹא לַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁאִם מַשְׁקִים תְּלוּשִׁין הֵן וְנָגַע טָמֵא בַּתַּחְתּוֹן, הָעֶלְיוֹן טָהוֹר. וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם חָלָק, אִם מַשְׁקִין מִכָּאן וּמַשְׁקִין מִכָּאן וּמַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ בְּאֶמְצַע, נָגַע טָמֵא בְּמַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ, מַשְׁקִין שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן טְהוֹרִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמְּחֻבָּרִים לוֹ:


אינו חיבור לא לטומאה - for if the liquids are detached and an impure thing came in contact with the bottom part, the top part is ritually pure. And similarly for flowing liquid, and even in a smooth place, if there are liquids from here and liquids from there and liquid flowing in the middle, if an impure person touched with flowing liquids, the liquids from here and from there are ritually pure, even though they are attached/connected to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:7)


**וְלֹא לַטָּהֳרָה.** כְּגוֹן גֻּמָּא שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה, וְנִצּוֹק אוֹ מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ מְחֻבָּר לְגֻמָּא זוֹ וּלְמִקְוֶה כָּשֵׁר, אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר, וְהַטּוֹבֵל בְּגֻמָּא זוֹ לֹא עָלְתָה לוֹ טְבִילָה:


ולא לטהרה – as for example, a hole that doesn’t have forty Seah and an uninterrupted flow of a liquid or flowing liquid that is attached to this hole/indentation or to a Kosher ritual bath/Mikveh, it is not a connection, for a person who immerses in this hole/indentation, his immersion did not count for him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:8)


**אֶשְׁבֹּרֶן.** מָקוֹם עָמֹק שֶׁנִּקְווּ בּוֹ מַיִם:


אשבורן – a deep place where water is gathered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 8:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/8:9:9)


**חִבּוּר לַטֻּמְאָה וְלַטָּהֳרָה.** וְאִם נִטְמְאוּ קְצָתָן נִטְמְאוּ כֻּלָּן. וְכֵן אִם נִתְחַבְּרוּ קְצָת מֵהֶן לַמִּקְוֶה וְטָהֲרוּ, טָהֲרוּ כֻּלָּן:


חיבור לטומאה ולטהרה – for if part of them were defiled, all of them were defiled, and similarly, if part of them were attached to a Mikveh/ritual bath and they were purified, all of them were purified.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:1)


**זֵיתִים מֵאֵימָתַי מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה.** לִהְיוֹת מֻכְשָׁרִים בַּשֶּׁמֶן הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן:


זיתים מאימתי מקבלין טומאה – to be fit for Levitical uncleanness with the oil that is coming out from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:2)


**זֵעַת הַמַּעֲטָן.** כְּלִי שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בּוֹ אֶת הַזֵּיתִים וְהֵן נִכְמָרוֹת שָׁם כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ רְאוּיִים לְהוֹצִיא שֶׁמֶן. לְשׁוֹן עֲטִינָיו מָלְאוּ חָלָב [אִיּוֹב כא]:


זיעת המעטן (the – intended - exudation produced by lying in the vat/exude the sweat of the vat) – a vessel that they place the olives in and they shrink by effect of the heat there in order to the appropriate to remove the oil. The language of (Job 21:24): “His pails are full of milk.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא זֵעַת הַקֻּפָּה.** דְּקֻפָּה אֵינָהּ מַחֲזֶקֶת הַמַּשְׁקִין, וְהַמַּשְׁקֶה הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן הוֹלֵךְ לְאִבּוּד, וְלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ, וְאֵין מַכְשִׁיר הַזְּרָעִים לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא מַשְׁקֶה דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) וְכִי יֻתַּן מַיִם עַל זֶרַע, כְּתִיב יִתֵּן וְקָרִינַן יֻתַּן, יֻתַּן דּוּמְיָא דְּיִתֵּן, מַה יִּתֵּן דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ אַף יֻתַּן דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ:


אבל לא זיעת הקופה (but not the – unwelcome – exudation originating in the pile or basket) – for the basket does not hold the liquids, for the basket that comes out from hem goes to waste and it is not appropriate for him, and it doesn’t make the seeds susceptible to receive ritual impurity other than liquid that is appropriate for it, as it is written (Leviticus 11:38): “but if water is put on the seed”/"וכי יתן-מים על-זרע" – is written יתן (defectively), but we read it as "יותן"/”yuttan” (written fully), [the word] יותן is similar to יתן. Just as "יתן" is satisfactory, so also ”יותן" is satisfactory.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:4)


**שִׁעוּר זֵעָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים.** דְּמַשְׁקֶה הַיּוֹצֵא תּוֹךְ שְׁלֹשָׁה אֵינוֹ חָשׁוּב שֶׁמֶן אֶלָּא מֹחַל בְּעָלְמָא וְאֵינוֹ מַכְשִׁיר, שֶׁאֵין מַכְשִׁירִים לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא שִׁבְעָה מַשְׁקִין בִּלְבַד, וְהֵן יַיִן מַיִם שֶׁמֶן דְּבַשׁ חָלָב טַל דָּם:


שיעור זיעה שלשה ימים (the measure/prescribed time of sweat is three days) – for liquid that goes out within three days is not considered oil but rather a mere thin secretion and doesn’t make it susceptible [for ritual impurity], for they don’t make it susceptible to receive ritual impurity other than the seven liquids (see Tractate Makhshrin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 4) alone, and they are wine, water, olive oil, honey, milk, dew and blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:5)


**מִשֶּׁיִּתְחַבְּרוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה זֶה לָזֶה.** דֶּרֶךְ הַזֵּיתִים מִתְחַבְּרִים זֶה לָזֶה כְּשֶׁשָּׁהוּ בַּמַּעֲטָן:


משיתחברו שלשה זה לזה – for it is the manner of olives that are attached one to another when they remain in the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:1:6)


**מִשֶּׁתִּגָּמֵר מְלַאכְתָּן.** מְלֶאכֶת הַכְנָסָתָן. דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ לְהָבִיא עוֹד זֵיתִים מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר לְהוֹסִיף עַל אֵלּוּ, אֵין הַזֵּעָה מַכְשִׁירָתָן, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ. וּמִשֶּׁתִּגָּמֵר מְלַאכְתָּן, אֲפִלּוּ בֶּן יוֹמוֹ מַכְשִׁיר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל, שֶׁחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים כִּדְבָרָיו:


משתגמר מלאכתן (after the work of preparing them is completed) – the work of bringing them. For all the time that it is intention to bring in more olives from another place to add to these, the sweat does not make them susceptible [for receiving ritual impurity], because it is not satisfactory for him. But when the work of preparing them is completed, even of that day, it makes it susceptible [to receive ritual impurity], even for that one day, it makes it (i.e., the olives) susceptible [to receive ritual impurity]. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Gamaliel, for the Sages state according to his words.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:1)


**גָּמַר מִלִּמְסֹק.** מִלִּלְקֹט זֵיתָיו:


גמר מלמסוק – from harvest his olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:2)


**אֲבָל עָתִיד לִקַּח.** לִקְנוֹת זֵיתִים מִן הַשׁוּק וּלְהוֹסִיף עַל אֵלּוּ:


אבל עתיד ליקח – to purchase olives from the market and to add on to these.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:3)


**גָּמַר מִלִּקַּח.** שֶׁאֵין בְּדַעְתּוֹ לִקְנוֹת עוֹד זֵיתִים, אֲבָל רוֹצֶה לִלְווֹת זֵיתִים מֵאֲחֵרִים וּלְהוֹסִיף עַל אֵלּוּ:


גמר מליקח – that he doesn’t have any intention to purchase more olives, but wishes to borrow olives from others and to add on to these.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:4)


**אֵרְעוֹ אֵבֶל אוֹ מִשְׁתֶּה.** וּלְפִיכָךְ לֹא הוֹסִיף עֲלֵיהֶן, אֲבָל דַּעְתּוֹ לְהוֹסִיף כְּשֶׁיַּעֲבֹר הָאֵבֶל:


אירעו אבל או משתה – and therefore he didn’t add to them, but it is his intention to add [to them] when the mourning period will pass.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:5)


**טְהוֹרִין.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הֻכְשְׁרוּ. כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל דְּאָמַר מִשֶּׁתִּגָּמֵר מְלַאכְתָּן:


טהורין – because they were not made fit for Levitical uncleanness. According to Rabban Gamaliel (see previous Mishnah) who stated: “After the work of preparing them is completed.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:6)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא מְקוֹם מַגָּעָן.** אֵין מֻכְשָׁר אֶלָּא מְקוֹם נְפִילַת הַמַּשְׁקֶה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, נָפְלוּ עַל הַזֵּיתִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין, לֹא נִטְמָא מֵהֶן אֶלָּא הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בָּהֶן הַמַּשְׁקִין טְמֵאִים בִּלְבַד:


אין טמא אלא מקום מגען – it is not fit to receive Levitical uncleanness other than the place of the falling liquid. Another explanation: if they (i.e., the water) on these olives whose work of preparing them was not completed the liquids are impure, nothing is defiled from them other than the olives that the impure liquids them alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:2:7)


**הַמֹּחַל הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן.** הַמַּיִם הַיּוֹצְאִים מִן הַזֵּיתִים קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן אֵין לָהֶן דִּין מַשְׁקִין, וְאֵין מַכְשִׁירִין אֶת הָאֳכָלִין, וְהֵן עַצְמָן אֵין מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה. וּמִפְּנֵי מָה טָהוֹר הַמֹּחַל הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַזֵּיתִים עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹצֶה בְּקִיּוּמָן:


המוחל היוצא מהן (the thin secretion that exudes from them) – the water that exudes from the olives prior to their work of preparing them being completed, they do not have the status of law of liquids, and they do not make eatables susceptible [to receive ritual defilement], and they themselves do not receive ritual defilement. And because just as the thin secretion/sap that exudes from the olives is ritually pure until the work of preparing them is completed because he doesn’t desire their existence.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:1)


**נָפְלוּ עֲלֵיהֶן מַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן וְהֻכְשְׁרוּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, הֲרֵי כָּל הַזֵּיתִים טְמֵאִין וַאֲפִלּוּ אוֹתָן שֶׁלֹּא נָגְעוּ בָּהֶן הַמַּשְׁקִין הַטְּמֵאִין, שֶׁהַמַּשְׁקֶה שֶׁיָּצָא מֵהֶן לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ הֻכְשְׁרוּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה הוּא מִתְעָרֵב בְּאֵלּוּ הַמַּשְׁקִין הַטְּמֵאִין וּמְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מֵהֶן, וּמְטַמֵּא אַחַר כָּךְ כָּל שְׁאָר הַזֵּיתִים:


נפלו עליהן משקין טמאין – after the work of preparing them is completed and they are susceptible to receive ritual defilement, all of the olives are ritually impure and even those that the impure liquids did not touch, for the liquid that exudes from them after the work of preparing them is completed for through it, they became susceptible to receive ritual defilement he combines with those impure liquids and receives defilement from them, and afterwards they defile all of the rest of the olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:2)


**הַמֹּחַל הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן.** מִן הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן:


המוחל היוצא מהן – (the thin secretion/sap that exudes from them) from the olives whose work of preparing them has been completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַהֵר.** דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב מֹחַל מַשְׁקֶה לֹא לְהַכְשִׁיר הַזְּרָעִים וְלֹא לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה כִּשְׁאָר מַשְׁקִים:


ר' אליעזר מטהר – for he (i.e., Rabbi Eliezer) does not consider the thin secretion/sap a liquid neither to make susceptible the seeds [to receive ritual defilement] and not to receive defilement like the rest of the liquids (see Tractate Makhshirin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַמְּאִין.** דְּמֹחַל חָשׁוּב מַשְׁקֶה בֵּין לְהַכְשִׁיר בֵּין לְטַמֵּא, כְּשֶׁיּוֹצֵא מִן הַזֵּיתִים לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן:


וחכמים מטמאין – for the thin secretion/sap is considered a liquid whether to make susceptible [to receive ritual defilement] or whether to defile when it exudes from the olives after the work of preparing them is completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:3:5)


**הַמֹּחַל הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַבּוֹר.** לְאַחַר שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין הַשֶּׁמֶן מִן הַבּוֹר, נִשְׁאָר בְּקַרְקָעִית הַבּוֹר מֹחַל כְּמוֹ מַיִם. וְקָאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דַּעֲלֵיהּ אִפְלִיגוּ רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבָּנָן, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לָא חֲשִׁיב לֵיהּ מַשְׁקֶה, וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא אֶפְשָׁר לִהְיוֹת מֹחַל בְּקַרְקָעִית הַבּוֹר בְּלֹא צִחְצוּחֵי שֶׁמֶן, חֲשִׁיבֵי מַשְׁקֶה כְּמוֹ הַשֶּׁמֶן. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים, וּכְמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ פְּלוּגְתַּיְהוּ תַּנָּא קַמָּא, וְלֹא כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


המוחל היוצא מן הבור – after they remove the oil from the cistern, there remains a thin secretion/sap on the floor of the cistern like water, and as Rabbi Shimon states that it is upon this that Rabbi Eliezer and the Rabbis disagree, Rabbi Eliezer does not consider it liquid, but the Rabbis hold that since it is not possible to have a thin secretion/sap on the floor of the cistern without dilution of oil, it is considered liquid like the oil. And the Halakha is according to the Sages, and like the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] and not like Rabbi Shimon explained it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:1)


**הַגּוֹמֵר אֶת זֵיתָיו.** לְאַחַר שֶׁעָבַר זְמַן הַגִּתּוֹת וְהַבַּדִּים. דְּבִשְׁעַת הַגִּתּוֹת וְהַבַּדִּים הָא תְּנַן בַּחֲגִיגָה שֶׁהַכֹּל נֶאֱמָנִים עַל הַתְּרוּמָה בִּשְׁעַת הַגִּתּוֹת וְהַבַּדִּים:


הגומר את זיתיו – after the time of the vats and the olive press have passed. For at the time of the vats and the olive press, it is taught in the Mishnah in [Tractate] Hagigah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 4 that everyone is believed [that in Judea] regarding the heave-offering/priest’s due at the time of the vats and olive presses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:2)


**וְשִׁיֵּר** [צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר יְשַׁיֵּר וְכֵן הוּא בִּסְפָרִים יְשָׁנִים]:


ושייר (and left aside) –[one must say, "ישייר" /and there should remain, and similarly it is found in old manuscripts].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:3)


**קֻפָּה אַחַת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנָּה תְּרוּמָה וּמַעֲשֵׂר בְּטָהֳרָה וְיִתְּנֶנָּה [לְעֵינֵי הַכֹּהֵן, שֶׁהַכֹּהֵן] עַצְמוֹ יִרְאֶה שֶׁגָּמַר עַתָּה אֶת זֵיתָיו, שֶׁאִם לֹא יִתְּנֶנָּה לְעֵינֵי הַכֹּהֵן, הוּא אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן לוֹמַר שֶׁשְּׁמָרָן בְּטָהֳרָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁעָבַר זְמַן הַגִּתּוֹת וְהַבַּדִּים. וְאִית סְפָרִים דְּגָרְסֵי לְעָנִי הַכֹּהֵן, מִשּׁוּם דִּתְרוּמָה מֻעֶטֶת כָּזוֹ שֶׁמַּפְרִישִׁים לְאַחַר הַגִּתּוֹת וְהַבַּדִּים אֵין רְגִילִין לָתֵת אוֹתָהּ אֶלָּא לְכֹהֵן עָנִי, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי תָּנֵי כֹּהֵן עָנִי, וְהוּא הַדִּין דְּמָצֵי לָתֵת אוֹתָהּ לְכֹהֵן עָשִׁיר:


קופה אחת – in order that he would separate heave-offering from it and [first-tithe] from it in ritual purity [and give it in the presence of the Kohen, that the Kohen] himself will see that he now has finished working with his olives, for if he doesn’t give it in the presence of the Kohen, he will not be believed to state that he guarded them in ritual purity, since the time of the vats and olive presses have passed. But there are books where we have the reading "לעני הכהן"/to a poor person who is a Kohen, because small amounts of heave-offering like this that he separates after [the time has passed] of the vats and the olive presses, it is not customary to give it other than to an indigent Kohen, because of this, it (i.e., this manuscript of the Mishnah) teaches: "כהן עני"/an indigent/poor Kohen, and the same law applies when he is able to give to a wealthy Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:4)


**יוֹלִיךְ לוֹ אֶת הַמַּפְתֵּחַ מִיָּד.** וּמְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ בְּמַאי דְּאָמַר שֶׁעַתָּה גָּמַר זֵיתָיו:


יוליך לו את המפתח מיד – and we believe him with what he says that now he has completed [working with] his olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:4:5)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** שֶׁל גְּמַר לְקִיטַת הַזֵּיתִים, אִם הֵבִיא לוֹ אֶת הַמַּפְתֵּחַ, מְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וּבְתוֹסֶפְתָּא (פרק י) מַשְׁמַע דְּמִיָּד דְּקָאָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה, הַיְנוּ כָּל אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם:


ר' שמעון אומר מעת לעת – of the completion of his harvesting of the olives, if he brought him the key, we believe him. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehudah. But in the Tosefta (Tractate Taharot, Chapter 9, Halakha 10) in implies that when Rabbi Yehuda said "מיד"/immediately, that is all of the entire day.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:1)


**הַמַּנִּיחַ זֵיתִים בַּכּוֹפֵשׁ.** גָּרְסִינַן. וְהוּא סַל גָּדוֹל שֶׁמַּנִּיחִים בּוֹ זֵיתִים לִכְמֹר:


המניח זיתים בכותש (he who places olives in a basket in which olives are kept for softening) – We have the reading. And it is is a large basket that they place the olives into to shrink them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:2)


**שֶׁיִּמְתֹּנוּ.** שֶׁיִּתְרַכְּכוּ וְתִהְיֶה בָּהֶן לַחְלוּחִית. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁגּוֹרְסִים בַּכּוֹתֵשׁ, שֶׁמַּנִּיחִים אוֹתָם בַּמַּכְתֶּשֶׁת יָמִים רַבִּים כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּעָשׂוּ רָפִים וְרַכִּים וְיִהְיוּ נוֹחִין לְהִכָּתֵשׁ:
