שימתונו – that they will be softened and they will be moist. And there are books where we have the reading of "בכותש"/in an olive basket, that we place them in a cavity for many days in order that they will become loose and soft and they will be easy to press.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:3)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מֻכְשָׁרִים.** בַּמֹּחַל הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן, שֶׁכְּבָר נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן:


הרי אלו מוכשרים – with thin secretion/sap that exudes from them, for the work of preparing them is completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:4)


**בֵּית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אֵין מֻכְשָׁרִים.** דְּלֹא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן עֲדַיִן:


The School of Hillel states that they are not susceptible [to receive ritual defilement] for the work of preparing them is not yet completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:5)


**הַפּוֹצֵעַ זֵיתִים.** מְמַעֲכָן בְּיָדַיִם מְסֹאֲבוֹת:


הפוצע זיתים (he who crushes olives)- he who crushes them with unwashed hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:5:6)


**טִמְּאָן.** דְּיָדַיִם מְסֹאֲבוֹת פּוֹסְלוֹת אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, וְכָל הַפּוֹסֵל אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה מְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקִין לִהְיוֹת תְּחִלָּה. וְהֵן מֻכְשָׁרִים בִּפְצִיעָתוֹ, דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בַּמַּשְׁקֶה דְּטָיֵיף עֲלַיְהוּ, שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ כָּךְ הֵן מַרְבִּים טַעַם:


טימאן- for unwashed hands invalidate/make unfit the heave-offering, and all who invalidate the heave-offering defiles liquid to be first-degree [of ritual impurity]. And they are made fit for Levitical uncleanness through their crushing, for it is satisfactory for him with liquid that drips on them, and through this, they increase the taste.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:1)


**לְגַרְגְּרָם.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶן גַּרְגְּרִים. כְּלוֹמַר לַעֲשׂוֹת שֶׁכָּל גַּרְגֵּר וְגַרְגֵּר יִתְיַבֵּשׁ בַּחַמָּה וְיִתְנַגֵּב:


לגרגרם – to make of them shriveled olives. Meaning to say, to make that each and every berry will wither in the sun and dry out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ הֵן רוּם אַמָּה.** דִּכְשֶׁנִּתְיַבְּשׁוּ הָעֶלְיוֹנוֹת, הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת מַזִּיעוֹת וּמִתְלַחְלְחוֹת בַּמַּשְׁקֶה, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הוֹאִיל וְדַעְתּוֹ שֶׁיִּתְנַגְּבוּ, אֵינָן מֻכְשָׁרִים. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים לְגַרְגְּרָן, לְמַתְּקָן בַּחַמָּה כְּדֵי לְסָפְתָם בְּמֶלַח וּלְאָכְלָן. וּלְפִי שֶׁאֵין מִתְמַתְּקִין כֻּלָּם בְּבַת אַחַת, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי נָקַט לְגַרְגְּרָן, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁיִּקַּח הַגַּרְגְּרִים הַמִּתְמַתְּקִין רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן וְיֹאכְלֵם. וְגוֹרְסִין אֲפִלּוּ הֵן רוּם אַמָּה הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מֻכְשָׁרִים, כְּלוֹמַר אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁהֵן גְּבוֹהִים אַמָּה דְּהַשְׁתָּא אוֹתָן הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת אֵינָן מִתְמַתְּקוֹת, הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מֻכְשָׁרִים בַּמַּשְׁקֶה הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן, דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ שֶׁיַּזִּיעוּ שָׁם, דַּחֲזוּ לַאֲכִילָה טְפֵי:


אפילו הן רום אמה – that when the upper ones wither, the lowest ones from the moisture and the moistness of the liquid, nevertheless, since it is his intention that they dry out, they are not made fit for Levitical uncleanness . And there are those who explain "לגרגרן"/to shrivel them, to sweet them in the sun in order that make an incision in them with salt and to consume them. And because they all do not become sweet at once, because of this, it (i.e., the Mishnah) takes [the term]: "לגרגרן"/to make of them shriveled olives, that is to say that he takes the shriveled olives that sweeten one by one and consumes them, and we have the reading: "אפילו הן רום אמה"/even if they are piled a cubit in height, they are fit for Levitical uncleanness, meaning to say, even that they are a cubit high for now those lowest ones are sweetened, they are made fit for Levitical uncleanness by liquids that exude from them. For it is satisfactory for him that they sweat there, that it appears better for consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:3)


**שֶׁיִּלְקוּ.** שֶׁיִּתְמַעֲכוּ וְיִתְפַּצְּעוּ:


שילקו (that they might putrefy) – that they will be crushed and will be burst.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:4)


**וְעָתִיד לְהַעֲלוֹתָן לַגַּג.** אַחַר כָּךְ לְמַתְּקָן שָׁם בַּחַמָּה:


לעתיד להעלותן לגג – afterwards to sweeten them there in the sun.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:5)


**נְתָנָן בַּגַּג שֶׁיִּפְתְּחֵם.** שֶׁיִּתְבַּקְּעוּ הַזֵּיתִים וְיִמְלְחֵם:


נתנן בגג שיפתחם – that the olives will burst open and will salt them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:6)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מֻכְשָׁרִים.** דְּכָל הָנֵי נִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּזֵעַת הַשֶּׁמֶן הַנִּפְלָט:


הרי אלו מוכשרים – for all these it is satisfactory to him with the sweating of the oil that is saved.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:7)


**עַד שֶׁיְּשַׁמֵּר.** שֶׁיְּתַקֵּן שׁוֹמֵרָה לְגַגּוֹ. סֻכָּה מִקָּנִים וְקַשׁ שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בְּרֹאשׁ הַגַּג לָשֶׁבֶת שָׁם לִשְׁמֹר:


עד שישמר – that he will prepare to guard it on its roof. He covers it from reeds and straw that they make at the top of the roof to sit there to guard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:6:8)


**אֵינָן מֻכְשָׁרִים.** דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ בַּזֵּעָה שֶׁיּוֹצֵאת מֵהֶן:


אינן מוכשרים – for it is not satisfactory to him with the moisture that exudes from them.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:1)


**רָצָה לִטֹּל מֵהֶם בַּד אֶחָד.** זֵיתִים שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן וְלֹא הֻכְשְׁרוּ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה דַּעְתּוֹ לִקַּח וּלְהוֹסִיף עֲלֵיהֶן כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין, וּבָא לִקַּח מֵהֶן שִׁעוּר מֻעָט שֶׁמְּשִׂימִים בְּבֵית הַבַּד בְּפַעַם אַחַת, אוֹ שְׁנֵי בַּדִּין, וְלַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶן שֶׁמֶן, וּמַשְׁאִיר מֵהֶן בַּמַּעֲטָן הַרְבֵּה:


רצה ליטול מהם בד אחד (If he wanted to take – enough from – them for a single pressing) – olives which had completed their work of preparation and they were not fit for Levitical uncleanness as for example, that it was his intention to purchase and to add upon them as is taught in the Mishnah at the beginning of our chapter (Mishnah 2), and he comes to take from them a small/minute measure that they place in the olive press on one occasion, or two presses and make [olive] oil from them, and leave from them a lot in the vat/pit where olives are packed until they form a viscid mass.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים קוֹצֶה בְטֻמְאָה.** חוֹתֵךְ מַה שֶּׁרוֹצֶה לַחְתֹּךְ מִן הָאוֹם שֶׁל זֵיתִים שֶׁבַּמַּעֲטָן, בַּד אוֹ שְׁנֵי בַּדִּים בְּטֻמְאָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן וְלֹא הֻכְשְׁרוּ:


בית שמאי אומרים קוצה בטומאה (let him set them apart in a state of uncleanness)- he cuts off what he wants to cut off from the cluster of olives that are in the vat where the olives are packed until they form a viscid mass, a single pressing or two pressings in defilement, since they had not completed their work of preparation and were not fit for Levitical uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:3)


**וּמְחַפֶּה בְטָהֳרָה.** כְּשֶׁגָּמַר לִקַּח מַה שֶּׁרָצָה לִקַּח, מְכַסֶּה הַמּוֹתָר שֶׁבַּמַּעֲטָן בְּטָהֳרָה כְּאִלּוּ הֻכְשַׁר, דְּמוֹעֶלֶת לְקִיחָתוֹ מַה שֶּׁלּוֹקֵחַ לְהַחֲשִׁיב אֶת הַמּוֹתָר כְּאִלּוּ נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן:


ומחפה בטהרה (and cover up in a state of ritual purity) – when he finished taking what he wanted to take, he covers the remainder that is in the vat in purity as if it was made fit for Levitical uncleanness, for his taking of what he takes is defrauded to consider the remainder as if he completed his work of preparation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:4)


**אַף מְחַפֶּה בְטֻמְאָה.** דְּאַף הַנִּשְׁאָר לֹא הֻכְשַׁר, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתָּן וְדַעְתּוֹ לְהוֹסִיף עֲלֵיהֶם. וּמִיהוּ אִם עָקַר כָּל הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁבַּמַּעֲטָן כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹתָן בְּבֵית הַבַּד, הָיוּ מֻכְשָׁרִים לְבֵית הִלֵּל:


אף מחפה בטומאה – for even that which remains is not fit to receive Levitical uncleanness, since their work of preparation had not been completed and it was his intention to add upon them. But however, if he uprooted all the olives that were in the vat in order to make them in the olive press, they were fit to receive Levitical uncleanness according to the House of Hillel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:5)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר חוֹפֵר בְּקַרְדֻּמּוֹת.** וְעוֹקֵר כָּל הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁבַּמַּעֲטָן:


ר' יוסי אומר חופר בקרדומות (Rabbi Yossi says he digs out with metal axes/hatchets) – and uproots all of the olives that are in the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:7:6)


**וּמוֹלִיךְ לְבֵית הַבַּד בְּטֻמְאָה.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הֻכְשְׁרוּ. וְקַרְדֻּמּוֹת דְּנָקַט, לִרְבוּתָא, לְפִי שֶׁהֵן כְּלֵי בַּרְזֶל וּמוּכָנִים לְטֻמְאַת מֵת יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר כֵּלִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּבֵית הִלֵּל:


ומוליך לבית הבד בטומאה – because they are not fit to receive Levitical uncleanness, and it (i.e., the Mishnah’s) use of the word קרדומות, it is a noteworthy point because they are vessels of iron and they are prepared for the defilement through a corpse more than other vessels. And the Halakha is according to the School of Hillel.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:1)


**בָּרֵחַיִם.** שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בָּהֶן זֵיתִים קֹדֶם נְתִינָתָן לְבֵית הַבַּד וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוֹתְנָן בְּבֵית הַבַּד וְטוֹעֲנָן בְּקוֹרָה:


בריחים (in the millstones) – that they grind/mill the olives before placing them in the olive press and afterwards they place them in the olive press and mill them with a beam.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:2)


**וְאִם הָיָה מַשְׁקֶה.** נִטְמָא הַמַּשְׁקֶה בַּשֶּׁרֶץ וְנִטְמְאוּ כָּל הַזֵּיתִים עַל יְדֵי חִבּוּר מַשְׁקֶה:


ואם היה משקה – the liquid is defiled by the [dead] creeping thing, and all of the olives are defiled through connection of the liquid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:3)


**נִמְצָא עַל גַּבֵּי הֶעָלִים.** נִמְצָא הַשֶּׁרֶץ עַל גַּבֵּי הֶעָלִים הַמְכַסִּים אֶת הַזֵּיתִים, וְעָלִים לָאו בַּר קַבּוֹלֵי טֻמְאָה נִינְהוּ:


נמצא על גבי העלים – if the [dead] creeping thing is found on top of the leaves that cover the olives, and the leaves are not receptacles of defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:4)


**יִשָּׁאֲלוּ הַבַּדָּדִים.** עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ שֶׁטִּהֲרָן בַּעַל הַשֶּׁמֶן לַעֲשׂוֹת זֵיתָיו בְּטָהֳרָה, וְנֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר לֹא נָגַעְנוּ בַּשֶּׁרֶץ שֶׁעַל הֶעָלִים וְלֹא נִטְמֵינוּ:


ישאלו הבדדים (ask the olive press workers) – commoners who are not careful in their observance of the laws of Levitical purity and tithing whom the owner of the oil purified to work on his olives in ritual purity, and they are believed to state: “we have not touched the creeping thing that is on the leaves and we were not defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:5)


**וְאִם הָיָה נוֹגֵעַ בָּאוֹם.** אִם הָיָה הַשֶּׁרֶץ יוֹצֵא מִן הֶעָלִים שֶׁמְּכַסִּים אֶת הַזֵּיתִים וְנוֹגֵעַ בָּאוֹם:


ואם היה נוגע באום – if the creeping thing left from the leaves that cover the olives and touched the cluster/mass of olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ.** שֵׂעָר אֶחָד מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ נוֹגֵעַ בָּאוֹם:


אפילו וכו' – one hair from the creeping thing touches the cluster/mass [of olives].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:8:7)


**טָמֵא.** כָּל הָאוֹם, דַּחֲשִׁיב חִבּוּר. אוֹם, הַזֵּיתִים הַמְקֻבָּצִים יַחַד וּמְחֻבָּרִין זֶה בָּזֶה בַּמַּעֲטָן, נִקְרָאִים אוֹם:


טמא – all of the cluster/mass [of olives is ritually impure], for it is considered attachment/joining. [The word] אום/cluster, mass, the olives that are collected together and attached each with another in the vat are called an אום.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:1)


**נִמְצָא עַל גַּבֵּי פְרוּדִים.** לְאַחַר שֶׁתָּקַע יָתֵד בָּאוֹם לְהַפְרִיד הָאוֹם לְכַמָּה חֲלָקִים פְּרוּדִים, וּבְכָל גּוּשׁ וְגוּשׁ מֵאֵלֶּה הַחֲלָקִים הַפְּרוּדִים מֵהָאוֹם יֵשׁ זֵיתִים מְדֻבָּקִים:


נמצא על גבי פרודים (if [the insect] was found on top of the broken olives) = after he inserted a peg in the cluster/mass of olives to separate the cluster to several separate parts, and in each clod of earth from these parts from the cluster, there are olives that are attached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:2)


**וְהוּא נוֹגֵעַ בְּכַבֵּיצָה.** שֶׁאִם בָּאתָ לְצָרֵף זֵיתִים שֶׁהַשֶּׁרֶץ נוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶן יֵשׁ בָּהֶן כַּבֵּיצָה:


והוא נוגע בכביצה (and it touches an egg’s bulk) – that if he came to combine the olives that the creeping insect touches that have an egg’s bulk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:3)


**טָמֵא.** כָּל הַגּוּשׁ. אַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין הַגּוּשׁ חִבּוּר מִשֶּׁתָּקַע אֶת הַיָּתֵד בָּאוֹם לְהַפְרִידָם, מִכָּל מָקוֹם כֵּיוָן דְּיֵשׁ בְּגוּשׁ זֶה זֵיתִים טְמֵאִין כְּשִׁעוּר לְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים, דְּהַיְנוּ כַּבֵּיצָה, נַעֲשָׂה חִבּוּר לְטַמֵּא כָּל שְׁאָר הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁבּוֹ:


טמא – all of the clod of earth [is impure] even though the clod of earth is attached from when inserted the peg in the mass/cluster to separate them, nevertheless, since there is in this clod of earth impure olives like the appropriate measure to defile others, which is like an egg’s bulk, it becomes attached to defile all the rest of the olives in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:4)


**פְּרוּדִים עַל גַּבֵּי פְרוּדִים.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ גּוּשִׁים עַל גַּבֵּי גוּשִׁים וְהַשֶּׁרֶץ עַל הַגּוּשׁ הָעֶלְיוֹן:


פירודים על גבי פירודים (broken-off blocks on top of other broken-off blocks) – there are clods of earth on top of clods of earth that the creeping inspect is on the uppermost clod.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:5)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַשֶּׁרֶץ נוֹגֵעַ בְּכַבֵּיצָה.** שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ גּוּשׁ:


אע"פ שהשרץ נוגע בכביצה – of that clod of earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:6)


**אֵין טָמֵא אֶלָּא מְקוֹם מַגָּעוֹ.** שֶׁל שֶׁרֶץ, כְּלוֹמַר אוֹתוֹ גּוּשׁ בִּלְבַד שֶׁהַשֶּׁרֶץ נָגַע בּוֹ. אֲבָל שְׁאָר גּוּשִׁים, טְהוֹרִים:


אין טמא אלא מקום מגעו – of the creeping insect [only what it touched through contact is impure], that is to say, that clod of earth that the creeping insect touched, but the rest of the clods of earth are ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:7)


**נִמְצָא.** הַשֶּׁרֶץ:


נמצא – the creeping insect [is found]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:8)


**בֵּין הַכֹּתֶל לַזֵּיתִים טָהוֹר.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הָיָה עַל הַזֵּיתִים:


בין כותל לזיתים טהור - and we are not concerned that perhaps it (i.e., the creeping insect) was on the olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:9)


**נִמְצָא בַגַּג.** הַמַּעֲלֶה זֵיתִים מִן הַמַּעֲטָן לַגַּג, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁרֶץ אַחַר כָּךְ בַּזֵּיתִים שֶׁעַל הַגַּג:


נמצא בגג – that one brings up olives from the vat to the roof and the creeping insect is found afterwards in the olives that are on the roof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:10)


**הַמַּעֲטָן טָהוֹר.** כְּלוֹמַר זֵיתִים שֶׁבַּמַּעֲטָן טְהוֹרִין, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן עִם הַזֵּיתִים הוּבָא וְהָיָה בַּמַּעֲטָן:


המעטן טהור – meaning to say that the olives that are in the vat are ritually pure, and we don’t say with the olives that were brought and were in the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:11)


**נִמְצָא בַמַּעֲטָן.** זֵיתִים שֶׁהֶעֱלָה בַּגַּג, טְמֵאִין, דְּמִקֹּדֶם שֶׁהֶעֱלָה הַזֵּיתִים לַגַּג הָיָה הַשֶּׁרֶץ בַּמַּעֲטָן:


נמצא במעטן – olives that he brought up on the roof, are ritually impure, for before he brought the the olives to the roof the creeping insect was in the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:12)


**נִמְצָא שָׂרוּף.** וְשֶׁרֶץ שָׂרוּף לֹא מְטַמֵּא, דִּכְתִיב [וַיִּקְרָא יא] בְּמוֹתָם, כְּעֵין מוֹתָם. וּכְשֶׁנִּמְצָא שָׂרוּף עַל גַּבֵּי זֵיתִים טְהוֹרִים, לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נָגַע בָּהֶן קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּשְׂרַף:


נמצא שרוף (if it found burned – and clean on the olives) – but a burnt creeping insect does not defile, as it is written (Leviticus 11:31): “whoever touches them when they are dead [shall be impure until evening],” similar to their being dead. And when it is found burnt on top of ritually pure olives, we are not concerned that perhaps it touched them before it was burned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:13)


**מְהוּהָא.** בֶּגֶד שֶׁנִּתְחַרֵךְ בָּאֵשׁ וְנַעֲשָׂה כְּמִין קוּרֵי עַכָּבִישׁ, וְשׁוּב אֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


מהוהא (tattered, scorched) – a cloth that was burned by fire and became like a kind of קורי עכבים/an impeding beam, and it no longer is susceptible to receive ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:14)


**טְהוֹרָה.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא טָמֵא הָיָה מִתְּחִלָּה קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּתְחַרֵךְ:


טהורה – and we don’t say that perhaps it was ritually impure from the outset before it was singed/burnt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 9:9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/9:9:15)


**כִּשְׁעַת מְצִיאָתָן.** בֵּין לְהָקֵל בֵּין לְהַחְמִיר:


בשעת מציאתן – whether or leniency or whether for stringency (see Tractate Taharot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 5 where this principle is enunciated – at the time when they are found).



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:1)


**הַנּוֹעֵל בֵּית הַבַּד מִפְּנֵי הַבַּדָּדִין.** עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ הָעוֹשִׂים מְלָאכָה בְּבֵית הַבַּד, וְטִהֲרָן בַּעַל הַזֵּיתִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעֲשׂוּ הַשֶּׁמֶן בְּטָהֳרָה, וְנָעַל בֵּית הַבַּד בִּפְנֵיהֶם שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנְסוּ עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ לְשָׁם וְשֶׁלֹּא יֵצְאוּ הֵם וְיִטַּמְּאוּ בְּמַגַּע עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ חַבְרֵיהֶם:


הנועל בית הבד מפני הבדדין – commoners who are not careful in their observance of Levitical uncleanness and tithing who do work in the olive press, the owner of the olives purified the in order that they would make the [olive] oil in ritual purity, and locked the olive press in their faces so that commoners should not enter there and they should not leave and be defiled through the contact of their commoner colleagues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:2)


**וְהָיוּ שָׁם.** בְּתוֹךְ בֵּית הַבַּד:


והיו שם – within the olive press.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:3)


**כֵּלִים טְמֵאִין מִדְרָס.** שֶׁהֵן אַב הַטֻּמְאָה וּמְטַמְּאִין אָדָם בֵּין בְּמַגָּע בֵּין בְּמַשָּׂא בֵּין בְּהֶסֵּט:


כלים טמאין מדרס – which is a primary source of ritual impurity an they defile humans whether by contact, whether by carrying or whether by shaking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:4)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר בֵּית הַבַּד טָמֵא.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נִטְמְאוּ בָּהֶן אוֹתָן עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ הַטְּהוֹרִים וְחָזְרוּ וְטִמְּאוּ אֶת הַכֹּל, דְּאֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁים לִזָּהֵר עַל הַטֻּמְאָה:


ר' מאיר אומר בית הבד אמא – for we are concerned that perhaps these ritually pure commoners were defiled and they returned and defiled everything, for they are not concerned about being careful on [matters of] defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בֵּית הַבַּד טָהוֹר.** דְּנִזְהָרִין עַל טֻמְאַת וַדַּאי, הוֹאִיל וְחָבֵר זֶה טִהֲרָן לְכָךְ:


רבי יהודה אומר בית הבד טהור – for they are careful on definitive defilement, since this member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse purified them for this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:6)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר אִם טְהוֹרִים לָהֶם.** אֵלּוּ הַכֵּלִים. כְּגוֹן שֶׁיָּשְׁבָה עֲלֵיהֶם אֵשֶׁת עַם הָאָרֶץ אוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ, דִּבְעֵינֵיהֶן טְהוֹרִין הֵן:


ר' שמעון אומר אם טהורים להם – these are the vessels. As for example, that the wife of a commoner sat upon them or that they belonged to a commoner, that in their eyes were ritually pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:7)


**בֵּית הַבַּד טָמֵא.** שֶׁאֵין הַבַּדָּדִים נִזְהָרִים מִלִּגַּע בָּהֶן, וְאִיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא נִטְמְאוּ:


בית הבד טמא – for the workmen in the olive press are not careful from touching them, and one has to be concerned that perhaps they were defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:8)


**וְאִם טְמֵאִין הֵן.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁיָּדְעוּ שֶׁיָּשְׁבָה עֲלֵיהֶן אִשָּׁה נִדָּה אוֹ זָבָה:


ואם טמאין הן – as, for example, that they knew that a menstruating woman or a woman with a flux sat upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:9)


**בֵּית הַבַּד טָהוֹר.** דְּנִזְהָרִין הֵן:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:1:10)


**אָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי.** [רַבִּי יוֹסֵי] כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּבֵית הַבַּד טָמֵא, אֶלָּא טַעֲמָא דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר מִשּׁוּם דְּאֵין הַבַּדָּדִין חוֹשְׁשִׁים בַּטֻּמְאָה, וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי סָבַר דְּחוֹשְׁשִׁין בְּמַגָּע אֲבָל אֵין בְּקִיאִין בְּהֶסֵּט, וְחוֹשְׁבִים שֶׁהֵן טְהוֹרִין כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נָגְעוּ, הִלְכָּךְ בֵּית הַבַּד טָמֵא, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הִסִּיטוּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגְעוּ בָּהֶם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


א"ר יוסי – like Rabbi Meir, he (i.e., Rabbi Yossi) holds that the olive press is ritually impure, but the reason of Rabbi Meir is because the olive press workmen are not concerned about defilement, whereas Rabbi Yossi holds that they are concerned about contact but they are not expert in [questions of] shaking, and they think that they are pure all the tine that they didn’t touch it. Therefore, the olive press is ritually impure, for we are concerned that perhaps they shook it, even though they didn’t touch them. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:1)


**וּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין בְּתוֹךְ בֵּית הַבַּד.** וְהֵן נִשְׁפָּכִים עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע וְדוֹרְכִים הַבַּדָּדִים עֲלֵיהֶן וְנִדְבָּקִים בְּרַגְלֵיהֶן, וְחוֹזְרִים אוֹתָן מַשְׁקִין וּמְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַזֵּיתִים כְּשֶׁהַבַּדָּדִין עוֹלִים וְדוֹרְכִים עַל גַּבֵּי הַזֵּיתִים, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֵשׁ בֵּין הַמַּשְׁקִין לַזֵּיתִים אָרֶץ כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּנַגְּבוּ רַגְלֵיהֶן מִן הַמַּשְׁקִין הַטְּמֵאִים עַל יְדֵי דְּרִיסָתָם בָּאָרֶץ. וּכְשֶׁאֵין מִנְעָלִים בְּרַגְלֵיהֶן אַיְרֵי, דְּהָא מַשְׁקֶה מְטַמֵּא כְּלִי וְחוֹזֵר וּמְטַמֵּא מַשְׁקִין שֶׁעִם הַזֵּיתִים. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁאֵין מִנְעָלִים בְּרַגְלֵיהֶן וְנִתְנַגְּבוּ הַמַּשְׁקִין הַטְּמֵאִין שֶׁבְּרַגְלֵיהֶן עַל יְדֵי דְּרִיסָתָן בָּאָרֶץ, שׁוּב אֵין מִטַּמְּאִים הַזֵּיתִים כְּשֶׁדּוֹרְכִין אוֹתָן, דְּמַשְׁקִין טְמֵאִין מְטַמְּאִין כֵּלִים וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין אָדָם:


ומשקין טמאין בתוך בית הבד – and they are spilled on the ground and the olive press workmen tread upon them and they (i.e., the liquids) become attached to their feet and they return those liquids and defile the olives when the olive press workmen ascend and tread upon the olives unless there is land between the liquids and the olives in order that they can dry their feet from the impure liquids through their treading/walking on the land. But when there aren’t shoes on their feet and the impure liquids that are on their feet dried through their treading on the land, furthermore, the olives do not become defiled when they tread on them, for ritually impure liquids defile vessels but do not defile humans.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:2)


**הַבַּדָּדִין וְהַבּוֹצְרִין.** הָא דְּלֹא נָקַט הַמּוֹסְקִין וְהַבּוֹצְרִין, לְפִי שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עַל הַבְּצִירָה שֶׁתֵּעָשֶׂה בְּטָהֳרָה, וְלֹא גָּזְרוּ עַל הַמְּסִיקָה. וְטַעֲמָא מְפֹרָשׁ בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְּשַׁבָּת, שֶׁפְּעָמִים אָדָם הוֹלֵךְ לְכַרְמוֹ לֵידַע אִם הִגִּיעוּ עֲנָבָיו לִבְצֹר אִם לָאו, וְנוֹטֵל אֶשְׁכּוֹל שֶׁל עֲנָבִים וְסוֹחֲטוֹ וּמְזַלֵּף עַל גַּבֵּי עֲנָבִים וּבִשְׁעַת בְּצִירָה עֲדַיִן מַשְׁקֶה טוֹפֵחַ עֲלֵיהֶן וְהֻכְשְׁרוּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה. וְטַעַם זֶה לֹא שַׁיָּךְ בִּמְסִיקָה:


הבדדין והבוצרין – surely that it (i.e., the Mishnah) did not use [the language] of those who harvest olives and those who cut grapes/"המוסקים והבוצרים" , because they (i.e., the Sages) made a degree on the cutting of grapes that it should be done [in a state of] ritual purity, but they didn’t make a decree on those who harvest olives, and the reason is explained in the first chapter of [Tractate] Shabbat (17a), for sometimes a person goes to his vineyard to know if his grapes have arrived [at the proper time] to cut them or not, and he takes a cluster of grapes and wrings them out and sprinkles them over the grapes, and at the time of the cutting of the grapes liquid still flows upon them and they have been made fit to be susceptible to receive ritual defilement. And this reason does not belong with the harvesting of olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:3)


**נֶאֱמָנִין לוֹמַר לֹא נָגָעְנוּ.** וּכְגוֹן דְּחָבֵר עוֹמֵד עִמָּהֶן בְּבֵית הַבַּד. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא נָגְעוּ וְלָאו אַדַּעְתֵּיהּ דְּחָבֵר:


נאמנים לומר לא נגענו – and as for example, that a member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse/חבר stands with them in the olive press, and we are not concerned that perhaps they touched it but it was not the intention of the member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:2:4)


**הַתִּינוֹקוֹת.** בַּדָּדִים שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ בְּנֵיהֶן עִמָּהֶן וְטִהֲרוּם לְכָךְ, וּכְשֶׁרוֹצִין בְּנֵיהֶם לִפָּנוֹת יוֹצְאִים אֲבִיהֶן לַחוּץ עִמָּהֶם וְנִפְנִים אֲחוֹרֵי הַגָּדֵר, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא נָגַע בְּטֻמְאָה וְלָאו אַדַּעְתָּא דְּחָבֵר:


התינוקות – olive press workmen who brought their children with them, and purified them for this, and when their sons want to defecate, their fathers exit outside with them and they defecate beyond the wall, and we are not concerned that perhaps he touched defilement, and it was not the intention of the member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse/חבר.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:1)


**כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִכְנִיסָן לִרְשׁוּת הַמְּעָרָה.** שֶׁטּוֹבְלִים בָּהּ. כְּמוֹ בּוֹרוֹת שִׁיחִין וּמְעָרוֹת:


כיון שנכנסו לרשות מערה – that they ritually immerse there [in the cavern], like narrow pits, lengthy ditches and spacious cavities/caverns.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:2)


**דַּיּוֹ.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא לֹא יִטְבֹּלוּ אוֹ לֹא יַטְבִּילוּ הַכֵּלִים וְיֹאמְרוּ טָבַלְנוּ וְהִטְבַּלְנוּ:


דיו – and we are not concerned that perhaps they would not ritually immerse or that they would not immerse the vessels and they would say, “we ritually immersed and we ritually immersed [the vessels].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:3)


**צָרִיךְ לַעֲמֹד עֲלֵיהֶן.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין בְּקִיאִין בְּדִין טְבִילָה וַחֲצִיצָה:


צריך לעמוד עליהן. לפי שאין בקיאין בדין טבילה וחציצה (this section is not found in the printed editions of the Mishnah and is a reading of the Bartenura commentary on this Mishnah)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:3:4)


**טְהוֹרִים לָהֶם.** כְּגוֹן כֵּלִים שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ אַחֵר, שֶׁבְּעֵינֵיהֶם הֵן כֵּלִים טְהוֹרִים לְפִי שֶׁאֵין נִזְהָרִים מִמַּגַּע עַם הָאָרֶץ. וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לְטַעֲמֵיהּ כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא לְעֵיל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


טהורים להם – as, for example, the vessels of another commoner who is not careful in the observance of the laws of Levitical uncleanness and tithing, that in their eyes these are ritually pure vessels because they are not warned/careful against the contact of a commoner. And Rabbi Shimon is according to his reasoning that we have explained above (in Mishnah 1 of this chapter). And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:1)


**הַנּוֹתֵן מִן הַסַּלִּים וּמִן הַמַּשְׁטֵחַ שֶׁל אֲדָמָה.** עֲנָבִים שֶׁבְּצָרָן מִתְּחִלָּה לַאֲכִילָה, וְלֹא הֻכְשְׁרוּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, דְּלֹא גָּזְרוּ אֶלָּא עַל הַבּוֹצֵר מִתְּחִלָּה לַגַּת וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִמְלַךְ עֲלֵיהֶן וּלְקָחָן מִן הַסַּלִּים אוֹ מִן הָאֲדָמָה שֶׁשָּׁטַח הָעֲנָבִים עָלֶיהָ וּנְתָנָן לַגַּת:


הנותן מן הסלים ומן המשטיח של אדמה (he who puts in the press grapes collected in baskets or as such as were spread on the ground) – grapes that were cut from the beginning for consumption and were not fit to be susceptible to receive ritual defilement, for they (i.e., the Rabbis) did not decree other on the person who cut the grapes from the beginning to [bring them to] the vat for wine-pressing and afterwards changed his mind regarding them and took them from the baskets or from the ground that he had spread the grapes upon them and placed them in the vat for wine-pressing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים נוֹתֵן בְּיָדַיִם טְהוֹרוֹת.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁבָּא לְנָתְנָן לַגַּת הֲרֵי הוּא כְּאִלּוּ בְּצָרָן מִתְּחִלָּה לַגַּת, וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן לַגַּת בְּטָהֳרָה:


בית שמאי אומרים נותן בידים טהורות – since he came to placed them in the vat for wine-pressing, he is as if he cut them from the beginning [to bring them] to the vat for wine-pressing, and he needs to bring them to the vat for wine-pressing in ritual purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:3)


**וְאִם נָתַן בְּיָדַיִם טְמֵאוֹת טִמְּאָן.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּסְתָם יָדַיִם שְׁנִיּוֹת הֵן, וְאֵין שֵׁנִי עוֹשֶׂה שְׁלִישִׁי בְּחֻלִּין, דִּלְמָא קָסָבְרֵי הַטְּבוּלִים לִתְרוּמָה כִּתְרוּמָה דָּמוּ. אִי נַמִּי, בְּיָדַיִם תְּחִלּוֹת עָסְקִינַן, וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן אֶלְעָזָר הִיא כִּדְאִיתָא פֶּרֶק ב'‏ דְּחֻלִּין:


ואם נתן בידים טמאות טמאן – but even though mere hands are second-degree [of ritual impurity], and nothing that is second-degree [of ritual impurity] can make something third-degree [of ritual impurity] with non-consecrated produce, perhaps they thought that what is ritually immersed for heave-offering is like heave-offering itself, alternatively, we are dealing with hands that first-degree [of ritual impurity]. And this is the opinion of Rabbi Shimon ben Eleazar as it is brought in the second chapter of [Tractate] Hullin [folio 33b].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים נוֹתֵן לַגַּת בְּיָדַיִם טְמֵאוֹת וּמַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמָתוֹ בְּטָהֳרָה.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הֻכְשְׁרוּ כֵּיוָן דִּבְצָרָן מִתְּחִלָּה לַאֲכִילָה:


ובית הלל אומרים נותן לגת בידים טמאות ומפריש תרומתו בטהרוה – because they were not fitted for Levitical uncleanness since they were cut from the outset for consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:4:5)


**מִן הָעֲבַט וּמִן הַמַּשְׁטֵחַ שֶׁל עָלִים.** עָבִיט הוּא כְּלִי שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בּוֹ הָעֲנָבִים כְּשֶׁבּוֹצְרִים אוֹתָן לַגַּת. וּמַשְׁטֵחַ שֶׁל עָלִים, שֶׁשָּׁטְחוּ הֶעָלִים עַל פְּנֵי הָאֲדָמָה וְנָתְנוּ הָעֲנָבִים עֲלֵיהֶם. וְהַשְׁתָּא מוֹכְחָא מִלְּתָא דְּהַבּוֹצֵר עֲנָבִים הַלָּלוּ מִתְּחִלָּה, לַגַּת בְּצָרָן וְלֹא לַאֲכִילָה, הִלְכָּךְ שָׁוִים בֵּית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל בְּהָא דְּנוֹתֵן בְּיָדַיִם טְהוֹרוֹת, דְּהָא שָׁתִיק לֵיהּ הִלֵּל לְשַׁמַּאי בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְּשַׁבָּת וְהוֹדָה לוֹ שֶׁהַבּוֹצֵר לַגַּת הֻכְשַׁר:


מן העביט ומן המשטיח של עלים (from the large basket, strapped on the carrier’s back, in which the grapes are carried during the vintage and/or from the grapes spread out on leaves) – the עביט is a vessel that they place the grapes in it that they cut to bring to the vat for wine-pressing. And the grapes spread out on leaves on the face of the ground, and now the matter is proven that the person who cuts these grapes from the outset, he cut them for the vat for wine-pressing and not for consumption, therefore, the School of Shammai and the School of Hillel are in agreement in this that one brings them with ritually pure hands, for this that [the School of] Hillel is silent in the first chapter of [Tractate] Shabbat (Mishnah 4) and agrees him with the person who cuts grapes for wine-pressing in the vat is fitted for Levitical uncleanness.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל.** עֲנָבִים:


האוכל – [he, (who is ritually unclean) eats] grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:2)


**מִן הַסַּלִּים וּמִן הַמַּשְׁטֵחַ שֶׁל אֲדָמָה.** דִּסְתָמָן לַאֲכִילָה נִבְצְרוּ. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיָּדָיו טְמֵאוֹת וְהָעֲנָבִים נוֹטְפִים יַיִן לַגַּת:


מן הסלים ומן המשטיח של אדמה – for they were cut, undefined, for consumption. Even though his hands were ritually impure and the grapes were dripping wine into the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:3)


**הֲרֵי הַגַּת טְהוֹרָה.** דְּאוֹתָן טִפִּין אֵין לָהֶם תּוֹרַת מַשְׁקֶה, הוֹאִיל וְלַאֲכִילָה נִבְצְרוּ וְלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּמַאי דְּטָיֵף. אֲבָל הָאוֹכֵל מִן הֶעָבִיט וּמִן הַמַּשְׁטֵחַ שֶׁל עָלִים, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין הָעֲנָבִים מְבֻקָּעוֹת וְאֵינָן מְנַטְּפוֹת, אִם נָפַל מִמֶּנּוּ גַּרְגֵּר יְחִידִי וְאֵין חוֹתָם לְאוֹתוֹ גַּרְגֵּר, דְּהַיְנוּ בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָעֹקֶץ עַל פִּי הַגַּרְגֵּר יֵשׁ כְּמִין חוֹתָם וְאוֹתוֹ חוֹתָם סוֹתֵם פִּי הַגַּרְגֵּר שֶׁאֵין מַשְׁקֶה יוֹצֵא. וּכְשֶׁנֶּעֱקָר הָעֹקֶץ עִם הַחוֹתָם, מַשְׁקֶה יוֹצֵא, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנְּטָלָם לְאָכְלָם יֵשׁ לוֹ תּוֹרַת מַשְׁקֶה, הוֹאִיל וּמִתְּחִלָּה בְּצָרָם לַגַּת, וְנִטְמָא אוֹתוֹ מַשְׁקֶה בְּיָדָיו טְמֵאוֹת, וּכְשֶׁנָּפַל לַגַּת נִטְמָא. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁנָּגַע בְּיָדָיו בִּמְקוֹם הַחוֹתָם. אֲבָל אִם לֹא נָגַע בִּמְקוֹם הַחוֹתָם, לֹא נִטְמָא הַגַּת, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא נָגְעוּ יָדָיו בַּמַּשְׁקִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּחֲזֹר הַמַּשְׁקֶה וִיטַמֵּא הַגַּת:


הרי הגת טהורה – for those drops do not have the status of liquid, since they were cut for consumption, and it was not satisfactory to him with what it drips. But a person who eats from the large basket, strapped on the carrier’s back, in which grapes are carried during the vintage or from the gapes spread on leaves, even though the grapes are not split and they are not flowing in drops, if there fell from him a single berry and there isn’t the oblate side of a berry to which the stalk is attached for that berry, that is, in the place where the peduncle/stalk is on the mouth of the berry there is a kind of oblate side of a berry to which the stalk is attached and that oblate side of the berry to which the stalk is attached closes/conceals the mouth of berry so that liquid does not exude, and when the peduncle/stalk is uprooted with the oblate side of a berry to which the stalk is attached, liquid exudes and even though he took them to consume them, they have the status of liquid, since from the outset, he cut them for the vat and that liquid was defiled with his ritually unclean hands, and when it fell to the vat, it became defiled. And these words apply when he touched it with his hands in the place of the oblate side of the berry to which the stalk is attached, but if he didn’t touch in the place of the oblate side of the berry to which the stalk is attached, the vat was not defiled, for his hands didn’t touch the liquids in order that the liquid would return and defile the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:4)


**נָפְלוּ מִמֶּנּוּ עֲנָבִים.** כְּלוֹמַר אֶשְׁכּוֹל שֶׁל עֲנָבִים, שֶׁהַגַּרְגְּרִים מְחֻבָּרִים בָּאֶשְׁכּוֹל וְאֵין מַשְׁקֶה יוֹצֵא, וְנִטְמְאוּ בְּיָדָיו טְמֵאוֹת, דְּהֻכְשְׁרוּ לְפִי שֶׁנִּבְצַר לַגַּת:


נפלו ממנו ענבים – meaning to say a cluster of grapes, that the berries are attached to the cluster and the liquid does not exude, and they were defiled by his ritually impure hands, and they were made fit for Levitical uncleanness because it was cut for the vat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:5)


**וּדְרָכָן בְּמָקוֹם הַמֻפְנֶה.** מָקוֹם פָּנוּי בַּגַּת שֶׁאֵין שָׁם כְּלוּם לֹא יַיִן וְלֹא עֲנָבִים וּדְרָכוּהוּ בְּטָהֳרָה:


ודרכן במקום המפונה (and one trampled them in an empty part) – an empty/vacant place in the vat where there is nothing – not wine nor grapes and he trampled them ritual purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:5:6)


**כַּבֵּיצָה מְכֻוָּן טָהוֹר.** הַגַּת, דְּאֵין אֹכֶל מְטַמֵּא כְּלִי, וּמִשֶׁיָּצָאת טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה חָסֵר לֵיהּ מִכַּבֵּיצָה וְאֵין אֹכֶל מְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים בְּפָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה, נִמְצָא דְּאֵין טֻמְאָה לְטִפָּה זוֹ אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת טֻמְאָה שֶׁקִּבְּלָהּ כְּשֶׁהָיְתָה אֹכֶל, הִלְכָּךְ הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי דְּנַעֲשֵׂית מַשְׁקֶה אֵין לָהּ כֹּחַ אֶלָּא כְּאֹכֶל. אֲבָל יוֹתֵר מִכַּבֵּיצָה, טָמֵא, שֶׁהַבֵּיצָה מְטַמְּאָה טִפָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה כְּדִין מַשְׁקֶה, וְחָזְרָה הַטִּפָּה וְטִמְּאָה אֶת הַגַּת, דְּמַשְׁקֶה מְטַמֵּא כְּלִי:


כביצה מכוון טהור (if there was exactly an egg’s bulk there – it is pure) – the vat, for a food does not defile a vessel and when it exuded the first drop, it was missing from an egg’s bulk and food does not defile other food with less than an egg’s bulk. It is found that there is no defilement for this drop other than on account of the defilement that it received when it was food, therefore now also that it became a liquid, it lacks the strength other than as a food. But more than an egg’s bulk, it is ritually impure, for the egg’s bulk defiles the first drop according to the law of liquids, and the drop returned and defiled the vat, for the liquid defiles a vessel.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:6:1)


**וְנִתְּזָה צִנּוֹרָא.** נִתַּז רֹק מִפִּיו שֶׁל עַם הָאָרֶץ הַמְדַבֵּר, וְרֹק הַיּוֹצֵא מִפִּי עַם הָאָרֶץ מְטַמֵּא אָדָם וְכֵלִים מִדְּרַבָּנָן, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים:


וניתזה צנורא (and spittle splashed forth) – spittle splashed forth from the mouth of a commoner who is speaking, and the spittle that exits from the mouth of a commoner defiles people and vessels according to the Rabbis, and all the more so food and liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:6:2)


**סְפֵקוֹ טָהוֹר.** דְּאָמְרִינַן שְׂפַת הַבּוֹר קָלְטָה הַצִּנּוֹרָא וּמְנָעַתָּה מִלָּרֶדֶת לְתוֹךְ הַיַּיִן. וּמַתְנִיתִין אַיְרֵי בְּבוֹר סִיד שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה. וּבְתוֹסֶפְתָּא אָמְרוּ, דִּבְבוֹר שֶׁל שֶׁמֶן טָמֵא, לְפִי שֶׁהַשֶּׁמֶן מַחֲלִיק שְׂפַת הַבּוֹר [וְהַצִּנּוֹרָא] מַחְלֶקֶת וְיוֹרֶדֶת לְמַטָּה לַשֶּׁמֶן שֶׁבַּבּוֹר:


פסיקו טהור – for we said that the edge of a cistern collected the ejection of spittle and prevents it from descending into the wine. And our Mishnah is speaking about a plastered cistern that does not receive ritual defilement. But in the Tosefta [Tractate Taharot, Chapter 11, Halakha 12] they stated that the cistern of [olive] oil is ritually impure, because the oil smoothens the edge of the cistern and [the ejection of spittle] smoothens and descends below to the oil that is in the cistern.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:1)


**הַזּוֹלֵף אֶת הַבּוֹר.** הַמּוֹצִיא יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן שֶׁבַּבּוֹר. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹצִיאוֹ כֻּלּוֹ בְּבַת אַחַת אֶלָּא מְעַט מְעַט, קָרֵי לֵיהּ זוֹלֵף:


הזולף את הבור (if one empties the pit- of wine/oil) – he removes the wine or oil that is in the cistern/pit, and because he doesn’t remove all of it at once but rather bit by bit, it (i.e., the Mishnah) calls it זולף/pours, drips.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:2)


**וְנִמְצָא שֶׁרֶץ בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה.** שֶׁהִכְנִיס חָבִית בַּבּוֹר וּמִלְּאָהּ, וְכֵן שְׁנִיָּה, וְכֵן שְׁלִישִׁית, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִמְצָא שֶׁרֶץ בֶּחָבִית שֶׁמִּלֵּא רִאשׁוֹנָה:


ונמצא שרץ בראשונה – that he brought in a jug into the cistern and filled it, and similarly and second and similarly a third, and afterwards found a creeping insect in the jug that he filled first.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:3)


**כֻּלָּן טְמֵאוֹת.** דְּמֵהָרִאשׁוֹנָה נִטְמָא כָּל מַה שֶּׁבַּבּוֹר, בֵּין שֶׁהָיָה שֶׁרֶץ בַּבּוֹר בֵּין שֶׁהָיָה בֶּחָבִית:


כולן טמאות that from the first, everything that was in the cistern was defiled, whether the creeping insect was in the cistern or whether it was in the jug.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:4)


**בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה הִיא טְמֵאָה וְכֻלָּן טְהוֹרוֹת.** דְּמִמֶּנָּה הָיָה הַשֶּׁרֶץ וְלֹא מִן הַבּוֹר. וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה מִן הַבּוֹר, שֶׁמָּא נָפַל בַּבּוֹר אַחַר שֶׁנִּתְמַלְּאוּ כָּל הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת:


באחרונה היא טמאה וכולן טהורות – for from it (i.e., in the last jug) was the creeping insect but not from the cistern, and even if t was from cistern, perhaps it fell into the cistern after all the first [jugs] had been filled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:5)


**אֵימָתַי בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא זוֹלֵף.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מַכְנִיס הֶחָבִיּוֹת עַצְמָן בְּתוֹךְ הַבּוֹר וּמְמַלֵּא אוֹתָן אַחַת אַחַת:


אימתי בזמן שהוא זולף – when he brings in the jugs themselves into the cistern and fills each of them one at a time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:6)


**אֲבָל הָיָה זוֹלֵף בְּמַחַץ.** שֶׁהֶחָבִיּוֹת עוֹמְדוֹת חוּץ לַבּוֹר, וְהוּא מְמַלֵּא אוֹתָן עַל יְדֵי הַמַּחַץ, וְהוּא כְּלִי חֶרֶס הֶעָשׂוּי לְמַלְּאוֹת בּוֹ יַיִן:


אבל היה זולף במחץ (but if he emptied the pit/cistern by using a ladle – to pour into the vessels) – that the jugs stand outside of the cistern, and he fills them through the ladle, and it is an earthenware vessel that is made/used to fill wine with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:7)


**נִמְצָא שֶׁרֶץ בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן הִיא טְמֵאָה בִּלְבָד.** דְּאֵמוּר בְּתוֹךְ הֶחָבִית הָיָה הַשֶּׁרֶץ וְלֹא בַּבּוֹר:


נמצא שרץ באחת מהן היא טמאה בלבד – for I could say that the creeping insect was within the jug and not in the cistern.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:8)


**אֵימָתַי בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא בוֹדֵק.** הַמַּחַץ וְהֶחָבִיּוֹת. שֶׁבְּדָקָן שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בָּהֶן שֶׁרֶץ קֹדֶם שֶׁנָּתַן לְתוֹכוֹ שֶׁמֶן, אֲבָל לֹא הָיָה מְכַסֶּה לֹא הַבּוֹר וְלֹא הֶחָבִיּוֹת כָּל שָׁעָה שֶׁהָיָה מֵבִיא הַמַּחַץ מִזֶּה לָזֶה, דְּאֵמוּר בַּהֲלִיכָתוֹ נָפַל הַשֶּׁרֶץ בֶּחָבִית. וְכֵן מְכַסֶּה וְלֹא בּוֹדֵק נַמִּי שֶׁמָּא הָיָה בֶּחָבִית קֹדֶם לָכֵן:


אימתי בזמן שהוא בודק – [when he examines] the ladle and the jugs, that he examined them that were wasn’t in them a creeping insect before he placed [olive] oil into tit, but he did not cover neither the cistern/pit nor the jugs all the time that he was bringing the ladle from this one to that one, for I could say that while he was walking the creeping insect fell into the jug, and similarly, if he covered it and did not examine also, perhaps it (i.e., the creeping insect) was in the jug beforehand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:7:9)


**אֲבָל הָיָה בוֹדֵק וּמְכַסֶּה.** שֶׁבָּדַק הֶחָבִיּוֹת, וּבְכָל פַּעַם שֶׁהָיָה מוֹלִיךְ הַמַּחַץ מִזֶּה לָזֶה הָיָה מְכַסֶּה בֵּין בּוֹר בֵּין חָבִית, מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ שֶׁרֶץ הָיָה בַּבּוֹר מִתְּחִלָּתוֹ, בֵּין שֶׁנִּמְצָא בֶּחָבִית, בֵּין שֶׁנִּמְצָא בַּבּוֹר, בֵּין שֶׁנִּמְצָא בַּמַּחַץ:


אבל היה בודק ומכסה – that he examined the jugs, and on every occasion that he would bring the ladle from one to another he would cover whether the pit/cistern or whether the jug, and whichever way you turn, this creeping insect was in the cistern from the outset, whether it was found in the jug or whether it was found in the pit, or whether it was found in the ladle.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:1)


**הָעִגּוּלִים לַזַּגִּים.** עִגּוּל כְּמִין רֵיחַיִם גָּדוֹל הָיוּ מְשִׂימִין עַל הַזָּגִים לְאַחַר שֶׁדָּרְכוּ הָעֲנָבִים בָּרֶגֶל, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְמַצּוּ וְיֵצֵא כָּל הַיַּיִן הַנִּשְׁאָר בָּהֶן, וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּבְדוֹ וְגָדְלוֹ שֶׁל עִגּוּל זֶה צְרִיכִים בְּנֵי אָדָם הַרְבֵּה לְהַעֲלוֹתוֹ עַל הַצִּבּוּר שֶׁל הַזָּגִין הֶעָשׂוּי כְּמִין תַּפּוּחַ וּלְהוֹרִידוֹ, לְכָךְ נֶחֱשָׁב רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְטַהֵר סְפֵק טֻמְאָה, מִשּׁוּם דְּרַבִּים מְצוּיִין שָׁם:


העגולים לזגים (the space between the molds and the deposit of grape shells) – An עיגול is a kind of large millstone that they would put on the grape shells after they tread on the grapes by foot, in order that they wring out and all of the wine exuded that remained in them, and as a result of its weight and size of this mold/roller, they need many people to raise it on the pile of grape shells that are made like a kind of pile and to lower it, therefore it is considered the public domain to ritually purify a matter of doubtful defilement, because man are found there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:2)


**כֶּרֶם שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַבּוֹצְרִים רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד.** שֶׁיְּרֵאִין בְּנֵי אָדָם לִכָּנֵס שָׁם, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּתְפָּס עָלָיו כְּגַנָּב:


כרם שלפני הבוצרים רשות היחיד (that [part of the] vineyard that is in front of the grape gatherers is the private domain) – for people are fearful to enter there, because he will be arrested/seized like a thief.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:3)


**אַחַר הַבּוֹצְרִים רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ רְשׁוּת לְכָל אָדָם לִכָּנֵס:


אחר הבוצרים רשות הרבים (that which is behind the grape gatherers is public domain) – for there is a domain for all people to enter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:4)


**כְּלֵי בֵית הַבַּד שֶׁל גַּת.** כְּלוֹמַר וְשֶׁל גַּת. דְּבֵית הַבַּד לְשֶׁמֶן, וְגַת לְיַיִן:


כלי בית הבד של גת – meaning to say, and of (i.e., belonging to) the vat, for the olive press is for [olive] oil and there is a vat for wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:5)


**הָעֵקֶל.** עָשׂוּי כְּמִין קֻפָּה מְסֹרֶגֶת כְּעֵין רֶשֶׁת, וְאַחַר שֶׁסָּחֲטוּ הַזֵּיתִים נוֹתְנִים הַגֶּפֶת בְּאוֹתוֹ עֵקֶל, וּמַנִּיחִים עָלָיו דָּבָר מַכְבִּיד לְהוֹצִיא הַנִּשְׁאָר:


והעקל (and a bale of loose texture containing the olive pulp to be pressed) - it is made like a kind of knit box similar to a net, and after they have squeezed/wrung out the olives, they place the olive peat in that bale of loose texture containing the olive pulp to be pressed and place upon it something heavy to remove the remainder.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:6)


**מְנַגְּבָן.** וְקֹדֶם לָכֵן לֹא מְהַנְיָא טְבִילָה אִם נִטְמְאוּ, עַד שֶׁיֵּצֵא מֵהֶן הַמַּשְׁקֶה הַטָּמֵא שֶׁנִּדְבַּק בָּהֶן:


מנגבן – but prior to this the ritually immersion is not benefitting/effective if they were defiled before the ritually impure liquids exuded from them that had been attached to them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:7)


**חוֹלְטָן.** שׁוֹפֵךְ עֲלֵיהֶן מַיִם רוֹתְחִים וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַטְבִּילָן:


חולטן (scalds them in hot water) – pours upon them hot water and afterwards ritually immerses them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Tahorot 10:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Tahorot/r/10:8:8)


**בְּשִׁבֹּלֶת הַנָּהָר.** מָקוֹם שֶׁהַמַּיִם רוֹדְפִין, כְּדֵי לְהוֹצִיא הַמַּשְׁקֶה הַנִּדְבַּק בָּהֶן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


בשבולת הנהר (in a rapid stream/ the current of the river) – a place where the waters flow/run, in order to remove the liquid that is attached on them. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.
